It is important to use blood, blood products and replacement fluids appropriately and to be aware of the principles designed to assist health workers in deciding when (and when not) to transfuse. The appropriate use of blood products is defined as the transfusion of safe blood products to treat a condition leading to significant morbidity or mortality that cannot be prevented or managed effectively by other means.

Conditions that might require blood transfusion include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; postpartum haemorrhage leading to shock;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; loss of a large volume of blood at operative birth; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe anaemia, especially in later pregnancy or if accompanied by

### Replacement Fluids C-37 — phần 2

cardiac failure. Note: For anaemia in early pregnancy, treat the cause of anaemia and provide haematinics. District hospitals should be prepared for the urgent need for blood transfusion. It is mandatory for obstetric units to keep stored blood available, especially type O negative blood and fresh frozen plasma, as these can be life-saving.

## Unnecessary Use Of Blood Products

### Unnecessary Use Of Blood Products — phần 1

Used correctly, blood transfusions can save lives and improve health. Like any therapeutic intervention, however, transfusion can result in acute or delayed complications, and it carries the risk of transmission of infectious agents. It is also expensive and uses scarce resources.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfusion is often unnecessary for the following reasons:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Conditions that might eventually require transfusion can often be

prevented by early treatment or prevention programmes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfusions of whole blood, red cells or plasma are often given to

prepare a woman quickly for planned surgery or to allow earlier discharge from the hospital. Other treatments, such as the infusion of IV fluids, are often cheaper, safer and equally effective (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Unnecessary transfusion can:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; expose the woman to unnecessary risks; and

### Unnecessary Use Of Blood Products — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-38; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cause a shortage of blood products for women in real need. Blood

is an expensive, scarce resource.

## Risks Of Transfusion

### Risks Of Transfusion — phần 1

Before prescribing blood or blood products for a woman, it is essential to consider the risks of transfusing against the risks of not transfusing.

## Whole Blood Or Red Cell Transfusion

### Whole Blood Or Red Cell Transfusion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The transfusion of red blood cell products carries a risk of

incompatible transfusion and serious haemolytic transfusion reactions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Blood products can transmit infectious agents—including HIV,

hepatitis B, hepatitis C, syphilis, malaria and Chagas disease—to the recipient.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Any blood product can become bacterially contaminated and very

dangerous if it is manufactured or stored incorrectly.

## Plasma Transfusion

### Plasma Transfusion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Plasma can transmit most of the infections present in whole blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Plasma can also cause transfusion reactions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There are very few clear indications for plasma transfusion

(e.g. coagulopathy) and the risks often outweigh any possible benefit.

## Blood Safety

### Blood Safety — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The risks associated with transfusion can be reduced by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; effective blood donor selection, deferral and exclusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; screening for transfusion-transmissible infections in the blood

donor population (e.g. HIV/AIDS and hepatitis);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; quality-assurance programmes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; high-quality blood grouping, compatibility testing, component

separation, and storage and transportation of blood products; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; appropriate clinical use of blood and blood products.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids; C-39

## Screening For Infectious Agents

### Screening For Infectious Agents — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Every unit of donated blood should be screened for transfusion-

transmissible infections using the most appropriate and effective tests, in accordance with both national policies and the prevalence of infectious agents in the potential blood donor population.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All donated blood should be screened for the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; HIV-1 and HIV-2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hepatitis B surface antigen

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treponema pallidum antibody (syphilis).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where possible, all donated blood should also be screened for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hepatitis C;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Chagas disease, in countries where the seroprevalence is

significant;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; malaria, in areas with high prevalence of malaria and in low-

prevalence countries when donors have travelled to malarial areas.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; No blood or blood product should be released for transfusion until all

### Screening For Infectious Agents — phần 2

nationally required tests are shown to be negative.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform compatibility tests on all blood components transfused, even

if, in life-threatening emergencies, the tests are performed after the blood products have been issued.

Blood that has not been obtained from appropriately selected donors and that has not been screened for transfusion-transmissible infectious agents (e.g. HIV, hepatitis) in accordance with national requirements should not be issued for transfusion, other than in the most exceptional life-threatening situations.

## Principles Of Clinical Transfusion

### Principles Of Clinical Transfusion — phần 1

The fundamental principle of appropriate use of blood or blood product is that transfusion is only one element of managing urgent care for women. When there is sudden rapid loss of blood due to haemorrhage, surgery or complications of childbirth, the most urgent need is usually the rapid replacement of the fluid lost from circulation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-40; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

Transfusion of red cells might also be vital to restoring the oxygen- carrying capacity of the blood. Minimize “wastage” of a woman’s blood (to reduce the need for transfusion) by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; using replacement fluids for resuscitation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; minimizing the blood taken for laboratory use;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; using the best anaesthetic and surgical techniques to minimize blood

loss during surgery;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; salvaging and reinfusing surgical blood lost during procedures

### Principles Of Clinical Transfusion — phần 2

(autotransfusion), where appropriate (page S-17). Principles to remember:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfusion is only one element of managing a woman’s care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Decisions about prescribing a transfusion should be based on national

guidelines on the clinical use of blood, taking the woman’s needs into account.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Blood loss should be minimized to reduce a woman’s need for

transfusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman with acute blood loss should receive effective resuscitation

(IV replacement fluids, oxygen, etc.) while the need for transfusion is being assessed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman’s haemoglobin value, although important, should not be

the sole deciding factor in starting the transfusion. The decision to transfuse should be supported by the need to relieve clinical signs and symptoms and prevent significant morbidity and mortality.

### Principles Of Clinical Transfusion — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The clinician should be aware of the risks of transfusion-transmissible

infection in blood products that are available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfusion should be prescribed only when the benefits to the woman

are likely to outweigh the risks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A trained person should monitor the transfused woman and respond

immediately if any adverse effects occur (page C-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The clinician should record the reason for transfusion and investigate

any adverse effects (page C-43).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids; C-41

## Prescribing Blood

### Prescribing Blood — phần 1

Prescribing decisions should be based on national guidelines on the clinical use of blood, taking the woman’s needs into account.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Before prescribing blood or blood products for a woman, keep in mind

the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; expected improvement in the woman’s clinical condition;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; methods to minimize blood loss to reduce the woman’s need for

transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; alternative treatments that could be given, including IV

replacement fluids or oxygen, before making the decision to transfuse;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; specific clinical or laboratory indications for transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; risks of transmitting HIV, hepatitis, syphilis or other infectious

agents through the blood products that are available;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; benefits of transfusion versus risk for the particular woman;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; other treatment options if blood is not available in time;

### Prescribing Blood — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the need for a trained person to monitor the woman and

immediately respond if a transfusion reaction occurs.

## Monitoring The Transfused Woman

### Monitoring The Transfused Woman — phần 1

For each unit of blood transfused, monitor the woman at the following stages:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; before starting the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; at the onset of the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; 15 minutes after starting the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; at least every hour during the transfusion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; at four-hour intervals after completing the transfusion.

Closely monitor the woman during the first 15 minutes of the transfusion and regularly thereafter to detect early symptoms and signs of adverse effects.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-42; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

At each of these stages, record the following information on the woman’s chart:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; general appearance

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; temperature

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; pulse

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; blood pressure

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; respiration

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; fluid balance (oral and IV fluid intake, urinary output).

### Monitoring The Transfused Woman — phần 2

In addition, record:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the time the transfusion is started;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the time the transfusion is completed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the volume and type of all products transfused;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the unique donation numbers of all products transfused; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; any adverse effects.

## Responding To A Transfusion Reaction

### Responding To A Transfusion Reaction — phần 1

Transfusion reactions may range from a minor skin rash to anaphylactic shock. Stop the transfusion and keep the IV line open with IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate) while making an initial assessment of the acute transfusion reaction and seeking advice. If the reaction is minor, give promethazine 10 mg by mouth and observe.

## Transfusion

### Transfusion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Manage as for shock (page S-1) and give:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; adrenaline 1:1000 solution (0.1 mL in 10 mL normal saline or

Ringer’s lactate) IV slowly;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; promethazine 10 mg IV; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hydrocortisone 1 g IV every two hours as needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bronchospasm occurs, give aminophylline 250 mg in 10 mL

normal saline or Ringer’s lactate IV slowly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Combine resuscitation measures above until stabilized.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids; C-43

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor renal, pulmonary and cardiovascular functions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfer to referral centre when stable.

## Documenting A Transfusion Reaction

### Documenting A Transfusion Reaction — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immediately after the reaction occurs, take the following samples and

send with a request form to the blood bank for laboratory investigations:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; immediate post-transfusion blood samples:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; one clotted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; one anticoagulated (EDTA/Sequestrene) taken from the vein

opposite the infusion site;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the blood unit and giving set containing red cell and plasma

residues from the transfused donor blood;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the first specimen of the woman’s urine following the reaction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If septic shock is suspected due to a contaminated blood unit, take

a blood culture in a special blood culture bottle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Complete a transfusion reaction report form.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the initial investigation of the transfusion reaction, send the

following to the blood bank for laboratory investigations:

### Documenting A Transfusion Reaction — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; blood samples at 12 hours and 24 hours after the start of the

reaction:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; one clotted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; one anticoagulated (EDTA/Sequestrene) taken from the vein

opposite the infusion site;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; all urine for at least 24 hours after the start of the reaction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immediately report all acute transfusion reactions, with the exception

of mild skin rashes, to a medical officer and to the blood bank that supplied the blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the following information on the woman’s chart:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; type of transfusion reaction;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; length of time after the start of transfusion that the reaction

occurred;

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-44; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; volume and type of blood products transfused; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; unique donation numbers of all products transfused.

## Transfusion

### Transfusion — phần 1

Only normal saline (sodium chloride 0.9%) or balanced salt solutions that have a similar concentration of sodium to plasma are effective replacement fluids. These should be available in all hospitals where IV replacement fluids are used. Replacement fluids are used to replace abnormal losses of blood, plasma or other extracellular fluids by increasing the volume of the vascular compartment. They are used principally in:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; management of women with established hypovolaemia

(e.g. haemorrhagic shock);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; maintenance of normovolaemia in women with on-going fluid losses

(e.g. surgical blood loss).

## Intravenous Replacement Therapy

### Intravenous Replacement Therapy — phần 1

Intravenous replacement fluids are the first-line treatment for hypovolaemia. Initial treatment with these fluids may be life-saving and can provide some time to control bleeding and obtain blood for transfusion if it becomes necessary.

## Crystalloid Fluids

### Crystalloid Fluids — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Crystalloid replacement fluids:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; contain a similar concentration of sodium to plasma;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cannot enter cells because the cell membrane is impermeable to

sodium; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pass from the vascular compartment to the extracellular space

compartment (normally only a quarter of the volume of crystalloid infused remains in the vascular compartment).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To restore circulating blood volume (intravascular volume), infuse

crystalloids in a volume at least three times the volume lost.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids; C-45

Dextrose (glucose) solutions are poor replacement fluids. Do not use them to treat hypovolaemia unless there is no other alternative.

## Colloid Fluids

### Colloid Fluids — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Colloid solutions are composed of a suspension of particles that are

larger than crystalloids. Colloids tend to remain in the blood where they mimic plasma proteins to maintain or raise the colloid osmotic pressure of blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Colloids are usually given in a volume equal to the blood volume lost.

In many conditions where the capillary permeability is increased (e.g. trauma, sepsis), leakage out of the circulation will occur and additional infusions will be necessary to maintain blood volume. Points to remember:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is no evidence that colloid solutions (albumin, dextrans,

gelatins, hydroxyethyl starch solutions) have advantages over normal saline or balanced salt solutions for resuscitation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There is evidence that colloid solutions may have an adverse effect on

survival.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Colloid solutions are much more expensive than normal saline and

### Colloid Fluids — phần 2

balanced salt solutions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Human plasma should not be used as a replacement fluid. All forms of

plasma carry a risk, similar to that of whole blood, of transmitting infection, such as HIV and hepatitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Plain water should never be infused intravenously. It will cause

haemolysis and will probably be fatal.

There is a very limited role for colloids in resuscitation.

## Safety

### Safety — phần 1

Before giving any IV infusion:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; check that the seal of the infusion bottle or bag is not broken;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; check the expiry date;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; check that the solution is clear and free of visible particles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-46; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

## Maintenance Fluid Therapy

### Maintenance Fluid Therapy — phần 1

Maintenance fluids are crystalloid solutions, such as dextrose or dextrose in normal saline, used to replace normal physiological losses through skin, lungs, faeces and urine. If it is anticipated that the woman will receive IV fluids for 48 hours or more, infuse a balanced electrolyte solution (e.g. potassium chloride 1.5 g in 1 L IV fluids) with dextrose. The volume of maintenance fluids required by a woman will vary, particularly if the

woman has fever or if the ambient temperature or humidity is high, in which case losses will increase.

## Other Routes Of Fluid Administration

### Other Routes Of Fluid Administration — phần 1

There are other routes of fluid administration in addition to the IV route.

## Oral And Nasogastric Administration

### Oral And Nasogastric Administration — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; This route can often be used for women who are mildly hypovolaemic

and for women who can receive oral fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Oral and nasogastric administration should not be used if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the woman is severely hypovolaemic;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the woman is unconscious;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; there are gastrointestinal lesions or reduced gut motility

(e.g. obstruction);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; surgery with general anaesthesia is imminent.

## Rectal Administration

### Rectal Administration — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rectal administration of fluids is not suitable for severely

hypovolaemic women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advantages of rectal administration include the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; It allows the ready absorption of fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Absorption ceases and fluids are ejected when hydration is complete.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; It is administered through a plastic or rubber enema tube inserted

into the rectum and connected to a bag or bottle of fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The fluid rate can be controlled by using an IV set, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids; C-47

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The fluids do not have to be sterile. A safe and effective solution

for rectal rehydration is 1 L of clean drinking water to which a teaspoon of table salt is added.

## Subcutaneous Administration

### Subcutaneous Administration — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Subcutaneous administration can occasionally be used when other

routes of administration are unavailable, but this method is unsuitable for severely hypovolaemic women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Sterile fluids are administered through a cannula or needle inserted

into the subcutaneous tissue (the abdominal wall is a preferred site).

Solutions containing dextrose can cause tissue to die and should not be given subcutaneously.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-48; Clinical use of blood, blood products, and replacement fluids

## Antibiotic Therapy C-49

### Antibiotic Therapy C-49 — phần 1

This chapter briefly discusses the use of prophylactic antibiotics before an obstetrical procedure, therapeutic use of antibiotics for suspected or established severe pelvic infection, and management of antibiotic allergies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prophylactic antibiotics are given to help prevent infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman is suspected to have or is diagnosed as having an

infection, therapeutic antibiotics are indicated. Recommendations for antibiotic treatment and prevention of specific conditions are discussed in the chapters on shock (S-1), operative care principles (C-65), normal labour and childbirth (C-77), vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy (S-7), vaginal bleeding after childbirth (S-29), fever during pregnancy and labour (S-113) and after childbirth (S-127), unsatisfactory progress of labour (S-71), difficulty in breathing (S-149), abdominal pain in

### Antibiotic Therapy C-49 — phần 2

early pregnancy (S-137), abdominal pain in later pregnancy and after childbirth (S-141), loss of fetal movements (S-155), prelabour rupture of membranes (S-159), induction and augmentation of labour (P-17), breech birth (P-45), caesarean birth (P-53), episiotomy (P-85), manual removal of placenta (P-91), repair of vaginal and perineal tears (P-97), correcting uterine inversion (P-111), repair of ruptured uterus (P-115), uterine and

utero-ovarian artery ligation (P-119), postpartum hysterectomy (P-123), and salpingectomy for ectopic pregnancy (P-131). Infection during pregnancy and the postpartum period can be caused by a combination of organisms, including aerobic and anaerobic cocci and bacilli. Antibiotics should be started based on specific indications, including:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; prevention of infection in the setting of established risk factors

(e.g. vaginal colonization with Group B streptococcus);

### Antibiotic Therapy C-49 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; prophylaxis for medical procedures; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; treatment of confirmed or suspected infection based on the clinical

presentation of the woman. Whenever possible, cultures and antibiotic sensitivities should be obtained (e.g. urine, vaginal discharge, pus) before initiating antibiotic treatment for a suspected infection so that treatment can be adjusted based on culture results or if there is no clinical response with treatment. However, prompt treatment of severe infections based on clinical presentation should not be delayed if a facility does not have the capacity to process cultures or to

collect cultures in a timely manner. If bacteraemia (presence of bacteria in the blood) or septicaemia (presence and multiplication of bacteria in the blood) is suspected, a blood culture should be done whenever feasible. Uterine infection can follow an abortion or

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-50; Antibiotic therapy

### Antibiotic Therapy C-49 — phần 4

childbirth and is a major cause of maternal death. Broad spectrum antibiotics often are required to treat these infections. In cases of unsafe abortion and non-institutional births, antitetanus prophylaxis should also be provided as part of comprehensive management.

## Providing Prophylactic Antibiotics

### Providing Prophylactic Antibiotics — phần 1

Performing certain obstetrical procedures (e.g. caesarean birth, manual removal of placenta) increases a woman’s risk of infectious morbidity. This risk can be reduced by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; following recommended infection prevention and control practices

(page C-25); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; providing prophylactic antibiotics at the time of the procedure.

Whenever possible, give prophylactic intravenous antibiotics 15–60 minutes before the start of a procedure to achieve adequate blood levels of the antibiotic at the time of the procedure. One dose of prophylactic antibiotics is sufficient and is no less effective than three doses or 24 hours of antibiotics for preventing infection after an obstetrical procedure. If the procedure lasts longer than six hours or blood loss is 1500 mL or more,

### Providing Prophylactic Antibiotics — phần 2

give a second dose of prophylactic antibiotics to maintain adequate blood levels during the procedure. Obstetrical procedures for which antibiotic prophylaxis is recommended for the woman include the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; elective and emergency caesarean (note: prophylaxis to be given

before starting the skin incision whenever possible) (P-53);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; suturing of third and fourth degree genital tears (P-104);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; manual removal of the placenta (P-91); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; placement of uterine balloon tamponade (S-35).

## Providing Therapeutic Antibiotics

### Providing Therapeutic Antibiotics — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For initial treatment of serious infections of the pelvic organs (e.g.

uterus, fallopian tubes, ovaries) or upper urinary tract, give a combination of antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antibiotic therapy; C-51

Note: If the infection is not severe, amoxicillin 500 mg by mouth every eight hours may be used instead of ampicillin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the clinical response is poor after 48 hours, ensure that adequate

dosages of antibiotics are being given and re-evaluate the woman for other sources of infection. Consider altering the treatment according to reported microbial sensitivity if cultures have been checked, and consider adding an additional agent to cover anaerobes if one was not included in the initial antibiotic combination.

### Providing Therapeutic Antibiotics — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If culture facilities are not available, re-examine for pus collection,

especially in the pelvis, and for non-infectious causes of pain and fever, such as deep vein and pelvic vein thrombosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Consider the possibility of infection due to organisms resistant to

the above combination of antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If staphylococcal infection is suspected, add:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; cloxacillin 1 g IV every four hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR vancomycin 1 g IV every 12 hours infused over one hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If clostridial infection or Group A haemolytic streptococci is

suspected, add penicillin 2 million units IV every four hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If neither of the above are possibilities, add ceftriaxone 2 g IV

every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Note: To avoid phlebitis, change the infusion site every three days

or at the first sign of inflammation.

### Providing Therapeutic Antibiotics — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection does not clear, re-evaluate for the source of

infection. For the treatment of postpartum endometritis, a combination of clindamycin and gentamycin is recommended, continuing until the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (see S-130). Discontinue antibiotics once the woman has been fever-free for 48 hours. There is no need to continue with oral antibiotics, as this has not been proven to have additional benefit. Women with bloodstream infections (bacteraemia), however, require antibiotics for at least seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-52; Antibiotic therapy

## Allergies

### Allergies — phần 1

Allergies can range from very mild skin rashes to life-threatening systemic anaphylactic reactions requiring immediate management to prevent death. Because of the potentially life-threatening risk of an allergic reaction to an antibiotic (or any medication), it is very important to rule out any previously known allergy to antibiotic or other medications before administering any medication. If an antibiotic to which a woman has developed an allergy is needed for a

significant infection for which no other options exist, then it is reasonable to decide whether or not to use the antibiotic based on the severity of the prior reported allergic reaction. In general, antibiotics that have been associated with a significant prior allergic reaction (e.g. anaphylaxis) should not be given unless under the close supervision of a trained physician. However, if the reported antibiotic allergic reaction was mild

### Allergies — phần 2

(e.g. rash) and did not involve systemic symptoms, then it is reasonable, if other options do not exist, to give a carefully supervised trial of the antibiotic. In general, there is up to a 10% chance that a woman with a prior allergic reaction to penicillin may have an allergic reaction to a cephalosporin.

## Anaphylaxis

### Anaphylaxis — phần 1

Anaphylaxis is a severe systemic allergic reaction that depends on rapid and timely management.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Symptoms of anaphylaxis can include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a sense of tingling

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; flushing

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; swelling of the face, lips and tongue

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; difficulty breathing due to swelling of the throat and airway

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; shortness of breath

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abdominal cramps

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; palpitations

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; syncope

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer adrenaline 0.3–0.5 mg IM immediately, and repeat every

10 to 15 minutes, as needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Antibiotic therapy; C-53

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (blood pressure, pulse, respiration rate, oxygen

saturation) and admit for observation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Try to identify the cause of the anaphylactic reaction (e.g. specific

medication, food) and immediately discontinue the triggering factor.

### Anaphylaxis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After an anaphylactic reaction a woman should be monitored closely

for at least 24–48 hours because there is a risk of a second rebound reaction. Rebound (biphasic) reactions almost always occur within the first 72 hours after an anaphylactic event. Consider beginning prednisolone 40–60 mg by mouth for three days to reduce the severity of a possible rebound reaction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the drug allergy in the woman’s case notes, and counsel her to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; write down the name of the medication;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; inform all future providers that she has an allergy to this

medication; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; always avoid this medication in the future.

## Mild Allergic Reactions

### Mild Allergic Reactions — phần 1

Mild allergic reactions usually involve itching and swelling and other cutaneous manifestations such as a new rash.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give antihistamines (e.g. loratadine 10 mg by mouth once daily) for

mild cases.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For more severe cases, add prednisolone 40–60 mg by mouth per day

for five to seven days. If prednisolone is needed for longer than seven days to control symptoms, then taper down the prednisolone gradually over several days while observing symptoms (e.g. 30, 20, 10 and 5 mg). Pain relief may be required during labour and is required during and after operative procedures. Analgesic drugs and methods of support during labour, local anaesthesia, general principles for using anaesthesia and analgesia, and postoperative analgesia are discussed in this chapter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-54; Antibiotic therapy

## Anaesthesia And Analgesia C-55

### Anaesthesia And Analgesia C-55 — phần 1

Pain relief may be required during labour and is required during and after operative procedures. Analgesic drugs and methods of support during labour, local anaesthesia, general principles for using anaesthesia and analgesia, and postoperative analgesia are discussed in this chapter.

## Analgesic Drugs During Labour

### Analgesic Drugs During Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The perception of pain during labour depends greatly on a woman’s

emotional state. Supportive care during labour provides reassurance and decreases the perception of pain (page C-86).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman is distressed by pain, encourage her to walk around or

assume any comfortable position. Encourage her companion to massage her back or sponge her face between contractions. Encourage the use of breathing techniques and allow the woman to take a warm bath or shower if she chooses. For most women, this is enough to cope with the pain of labour. If necessary, offer the woman:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM every four hours as needed,

informing her of the advantages and disadvantages (see below) and obtaining consent;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; promethazine 25 mg IM or IV if vomiting occurs.

Barbiturates and sedatives should not be used to relieve anxiety in labour.

## Danger

### Danger — phần 1

If morphine is given to the woman within four hours before she gives birth, the baby may suffer from respiratory depression. Naloxone is the antidote. Note: Do not administer naloxone to newborns whose mothers are suspected of having recently abused narcotic drugs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of respiratory depression in the newborn, begin

resuscitation immediately:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After vital signs have been established, give naloxone 0.1 mg/kg

body weight IV to the newborn.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infant has adequate peripheral circulation after

successful resuscitation, naloxone can be given IM. Repeated doses may be required to prevent recurrent respiratory depression.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-56; Anaesthesia and analgesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of respiratory depression in the newborn, but

### Danger — phần 2

morphine was given within four hours before birth, observe the baby expectantly for signs of respiratory depression and treat as above if they occur.

## Premedication With Promethazine And Diazepam

### Premedication With Promethazine And Diazepam — phần 1

Premedication is required for procedures that last longer than 30 minutes. The dose must be adjusted to the weight and condition of the woman and to the condition of the fetus (when present). Inform the woman of the advantages and disadvantages, and obtain consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM, informing the woman of

the advantages and disadvantages (page C-55) and obtaining consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give diazepam in increments of 1 mg IV and wait at least two minutes

before giving another increment. A safe and sufficient level of sedation has been achieved when the woman’s upper eyelid droops and just covers the edge of the pupil.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Monitor the respiratory rate every minute. If the respiratory rate

falls below 10 breaths per minute, stop administration of all sedative or analgesic drugs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Monitor the fetal heart rate at least every 15 minutes. If the fetal

### Premedication With Promethazine And Diazepam — phần 2

heart rate falls below 100 beats per minute, stop administration of all sedative or analgesic drugs.

## Local Anaesthesia

### Local Anaesthesia — phần 1

Local anaesthesia (lidocaine with or without adrenaline) is used to infiltrate tissue and block the sensory nerves.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Because a woman under local anaesthesia remains awake and alert

during the procedure, it is especially important to ensure:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; counselling to increase cooperation and minimize her fears;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; good communication throughout the procedure as well as physical

reassurance from the provider, if necessary;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; time and patience, as local anaesthetics do not take effect

immediately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Anaesthesia and analgesia; C-57

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The following conditions are required for the safe use of local

anaesthesia:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; All members of the operating team must be knowledgeable and

experienced in the use of local anaesthetics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Emergency drugs and equipment (suction, oxygen, resuscitation

### Local Anaesthesia — phần 2

equipment) should be readily available and in usable condition, and all members of the operating team trained in their use.

## Lidocaine

### Lidocaine — phần 1

Lidocaine preparations are usually 2% or 1% and require dilution before use (Box C-1). For most obstetric procedures, the preparation is diluted to 0.5%, which gives the maximum effect with the least toxicity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX C-1.; Preparation of lidocaine 0.5% solution

Combine: • lidocaine 2%, one part; and • normal saline or sterile distilled water, three parts (do not use glucose solution as it increases the risk of infection). Or combine: • lidocaine 1%, one part; and • normal saline or sterile distilled water, one part.

## Adrenaline

### Adrenaline — phần 1

Adrenaline causes local vasoconstriction. Its use with lidocaine has the following advantages:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; less blood loss;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; longer effect of anaesthetic (usually one to two hours); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; less risk of toxicity because of slower absorption into the general

circulation. If the procedure requires a small surface to be anaesthetized or requires less than 40 mL of lidocaine, adrenaline is not necessary. For larger surfaces, however, especially when more than 40 mL is needed, adrenaline is required to reduce the absorption rate and thereby reduce toxicity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-58; Anaesthesia and analgesia

### Adrenaline — phần 2

The best concentration of adrenaline is 1:200 000 (5 mcg/mL). This gives a maximum local effect with the least risk of toxicity from the adrenaline itself (Table C-3). Note: It is critical to measure adrenaline carefully and accurately using a syringe such as a bacillus Calmette-Guérin (BCG) or insulin syringe. Mixtures must be prepared observing strict infection prevention practices (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-3.; Formulas for preparing 0.5% lidocaine solutions

containing 1:200 000 adrenaline Desired Amount

- Dòng dữ liệu: of Local; Normal Saline/; Normal Saline/; Adrenaline

- Dòng dữ liệu: Anaesthetic; Lidocaine 2%; Lidocaine 1%; 1:1000

Needed

- Dòng dữ liệu: 20 mL; 15 mL/5 mL; 10 mL/10 mL; 0.1 mL

- Dòng dữ liệu: 40 mL; 30 mL/10 mL; 20 mL/20 mL; 0.2 mL

- Dòng dữ liệu: 100 mL; 75 mL/25 mL; 50 mL/50 mL; 0.5 mL

- Dòng dữ liệu: 200 mL; 150 mL/50 mL; 100 mL/100 mL; 1.0 mL

## Prevention Of Complications

### Prevention Of Complications — phần 1

All local anaesthetic drugs are potentially toxic. Major complications from local anaesthesia, however, are extremely rare (Table C-5, page C-59). The best way to avoid complications is to prevent them:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid using concentrations of lidocaine stronger than 0.5%.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If more than 40 mL of the anaesthetic solution is to be used, add

adrenaline to delay dispersion. Procedures that might require more than 40 mL of 0.5% lidocaine are caesarean and repair of extensive perineal tears.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the lowest effective dose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the maximum safe dose (Table C-4, page C-59). For an

adult, this is 4 mg/kg body weight of lidocaine without adrenaline and 7 mg/kg body weight of lidocaine with adrenaline. The anaesthetic effect should last for at least two hours. Doses can be repeated if needed after two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Anaesthesia and analgesia; C-59

### Prevention Of Complications — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-4.; Maximum safe doses of local anaesthetic drugs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Drug; Maximum Dose; Maximum Dose for

- Dòng dữ liệu: (mg/kg of body weight); 60 kg Adult (mg)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Lidocaine; 4; 240

Lidocaine + adrenaline

- Dòng dữ liệu: 7; 420

1:200 000 (5 mcg/mL)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject slowly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid accidental injection into a vessel. There are three ways of doing

this:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Moving needle technique (preferred for tissue infiltration): The

needle is constantly in motion while injecting; this makes it impossible for a substantial amount of solution to enter a vessel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Plunger withdrawal technique (preferred for nerve block when

considerable amounts are injected into one site): The syringe plunger is withdrawn before injecting; if blood appears, the needle is repositioned and attempted again.

### Prevention Of Complications — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Syringe withdrawal technique: The needle is inserted and the

anaesthetic is injected as the syringe is being withdrawn. To avoid lidocaine toxicity: • Use a dilute solution. • Add adrenaline when more than 40 mL will be used. • Use the lowest effective dose. • Observe the maximum dose. • Avoid IV injection.

## Diagnosis Of Lidocaine Allergy And Toxicity

### Diagnosis Of Lidocaine Allergy And Toxicity — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-5.; Symptoms and signs of lidocaine allergy and toxicity

Life-Threatening

- Dòng dữ liệu: Allergy; Mild Toxicity; Severe Toxicity; Toxicity

(very rare)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Shock; • Numbness of; • Sleepiness; • Tonic-clonic

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Redness of skin; lips and tongue; • Disorientation; convulsions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Skin rash/hives; • Metallic taste in; • Muscle; • Respiratory

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Bronchospasm; mouth; twitching and; depression or

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Vomiting; • Dizziness/light-; shivering; arrest

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Serum sickness; headedness; • Slurred speech; • Cardiac

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Ringing in ears; depression or

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Blurred vision; arrest

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-60; Anaesthesia and analgesia

## Management Of Lidocaine Allergy

### Management Of Lidocaine Allergy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give adrenaline 1:1000, 0.5 mL IM, and repeat every 10 minutes if

necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In acute situations, give hydrocortisone 100 mg IV every hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To prevent recurrence, give diphenhydramine 50 mg IM or IV slowly,

then 50 mg by mouth every six hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat bronchospasm with aminophylline 250 mg in normal saline 10

mL IV slowly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Laryngeal oedema may require immediate tracheostomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For shock, begin standard shock management (page S-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Severe or recurrent signs may require corticosteroids (e.g.

hydrocortisone IV 2 mg/kg body weight every four hours until condition improves). In chronic situations give prednisone 5 mg or prednisolone 10 mg by mouth every six hours until the condition improves.

## Management Of Lidocaine Toxicity

### Management Of Lidocaine Toxicity — phần 1

Symptoms and signs of toxicity (Table C-5, page C-59) should alert the practitioner to immediately stop injecting and prepare to treat severe and life-threatening side effects. If symptoms and signs of mild toxicity are observed, wait a few minutes to see if the symptoms subside, check vital signs, talk to the woman and then continue the procedure, if possible.

## Convulsions

### Convulsions — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Turn the woman to her left side, insert an airway and aspirate

secretions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen at 6–8 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give diazepam 1–5 mg IV in 1-mg increments. Repeat if convulsions

recur.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note: The use of diazepam to treat convulsions may cause respiratory

depression.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Anaesthesia and analgesia; C-61

## Respiratory Arrest

### Respiratory Arrest — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is not breathing, assist ventilation using an Ambu bag

and mask or via endotracheal tube; give oxygen at 4–6 L per minute.

## Cardiac Arrest

### Cardiac Arrest — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hyperventilate with oxygen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform cardiac massage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman has not yet given birth, immediately perform a

caesarean (page P-53) using general anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give adrenaline 1:10 000, 0.5 mL IV.

## Adrenaline Toxicity

### Adrenaline Toxicity — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Systemic adrenaline toxicity results from inadvertent or excessive

amounts of IV administration and results in:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; restlessness

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; sweating

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hypertension

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cerebral haemorrhage

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; rapid heart rate

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ventricular fibrillation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Local adrenaline toxicity occurs when the concentration is excessive,

and results in ischaemia at the infiltration site with poor healing.

## General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia

### General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The keys to pain management and comfort are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; supportive attention from staff before, during and after a

procedure (helps reduce anxiety and lessen pain);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a provider who is comfortable working with women who are

awake and who is trained to use instruments gently; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the selection of an appropriate type and level of pain medication.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-62; Anaesthesia and analgesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tips for performing procedures on women who are awake include the

following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain each step of the procedure before performing it.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use adequate premedication in cases expected to last longer than

30 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give analgesics or sedatives at an appropriate time before the

### General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia — phần 2

procedure (30 minutes before for IM and 60 minutes before for oral medication) so that maximum relief will be provided during the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use dilute solutions in adequate amounts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the level of anaesthesia by pinching the area with forceps.

If the woman feels the pinch, wait two minutes and then retest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wait a few seconds after performing each step or task to allow the

woman to prepare for the next one.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Move slowly, without jerky or quick motions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Handle tissue gently and avoid undue retraction, pulling or

pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use instruments with confidence.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoid saying things like “this won’t hurt” if, in fact, it will hurt;

or “I’m almost finished” if you are not almost finished.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Talk with the woman throughout the procedure.

### General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The need for supplemental analgesic or sedative medications (by

mouth, IM or IV) depends on:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the emotional state of the woman;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the procedure to be performed (Table C-6, page C-63);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the anticipated length of the procedure; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the skill of the provider and the assistance of the staff.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Anaesthesia and analgesia; C-63

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-6.; Analgesia and anaesthesia options

- Dòng dữ liệu: Procedure; Analgesia/Anaesthesia Optionsa

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breech birth; • General methods of labour support (page C-85)

• Pudendal block (page P-3)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean; • Spinal anaesthesia (page P-11)

• Local anaesthesia (page P-7) • Ketamine (page P-13) • General anaesthesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: Cervical tears; • Morphine and diazepam (pages C-55, C-56)

- Dòng dữ liệu: (extensive); • Ketamine (page P-13)

### General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Colpotomy/; • Local anaesthesia (page C-56)

Culdocentesis

- Dòng dữ liệu: Craniotomy/; • Emotional support and encouragement (page C-9)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Craniocentesis; • Diazepam (page C-56)

• Pudendal block (page P-3)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Dilatation and; • Paracervical block (page P-1)

- Dòng dữ liệu: curettage; • Morphine (page C-55)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Episiotomy; • Local anaesthesia (page C-56)

• Pudendal block (page P-3)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Forceps birth; • Emotional support and encouragement (page C-9)

• Pudendal block (page P-3) Labour and childbirth • General methods of labour support (page C-85) • Morphine and promethazine (pages C-55, C-56)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Laparotomy; • General anaesthesia

• Spinal anaesthesia (page P-11)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Manual removal of; • Morphine and diazepam (pages C-55, C-56)

- Dòng dữ liệu: placenta; • Ketamine (page P-13)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Manual vacuum; • Paracervical block (page P-1)

### General Principles For Anaesthesia And Analgesia — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: aspiration; • Morphine (page C-55)

Perineal tears (first and • Local anaesthesia (page C-56)

- Dòng dữ liệu: second degree); • Pudendal block (page P-3)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Perineal tears (third; • Pudendal block (page P-3)

- Dòng dữ liệu: and fourth degree); • Ketamine (page P-13)

• Local anaesthesia, morphine and diazepam (pages C-55, C-56) • Spinal anaesthesia (page P-11)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Uterine inversion; • Morphine and diazepam (pages C-55, C-56)

- Dòng dữ liệu: (correction of); • General anaesthesia

Vacuum-assisted birth • Emotional support and encouragement (page C-9) • Pudendal block (page P-3) a The preferred analgesia/anaesthesia option is listed in bold.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-64; Anaesthesia and analgesia

## Postoperative Analgesia

### Postoperative Analgesia — phần 1

Adequate postoperative pain control is important. A woman who is in severe pain does not recover well. Note: Avoid over-sedation as this will limit mobility, which is important during the postoperative period. Good postoperative pain control regimens include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; non-narcotic mild analgesics, such as paracetamol 500 mg by mouth

as needed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; narcotics such as morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM every four

hours as needed, informing the woman of the advantages and disadvantages (page C-55) and obtaining consent;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; combinations of lower doses of narcotics with paracetamol.

### Postoperative Analgesia — phần 2

Note: If the woman is vomiting, narcotics may be combined with anti-emetics such as promethazine 25 mg IM or IV every four hours as needed. The woman is the primary focus of the physician/midwife and nurse during any procedure. The surgical or scrub nurse has her attention focused on the procedure and the needs of the physician/midwife performing the procedure.

## Operative Care Principles C-65

### Operative Care Principles C-65 — phần 1

The woman is the primary focus of the physician/midwife and nurse during any procedure. The surgical or scrub nurse has her attention focused on the procedure and the needs of the physician/midwife performing the procedure.

## Preparing The Operating Theatre

### Preparing The Operating Theatre — phần 1

Ensure that:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the operating theatre is clean (it should be cleaned after every

procedure);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; necessary supplies and anaesthesia equipment are available, including

medications and an oxygen cylinder;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; emergency equipment, medication and supplies for both adult and

newborn resuscitation are available, in working order and not past the expiry date;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; there is an adequate supply of theatre dress for the anticipated

members of the surgical team;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; clean/sterile linens are available; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sterile surgical instruments and supplies (e.g. gloves, gauze,

instruments) are available and not beyond expiry date.

## Preparing A Woman For A Surgical Procedure

### Preparing A Woman For A Surgical Procedure — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain the procedure to be performed and its purpose to the woman

and any accompanying family members the woman would like to be involved in decision-making. If the woman is unconscious, explain the procedure to her family.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain informed consent for the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that all members of the operating, anaesthesia and newborn (if

indicated) teams have been mobilized and the level of urgency of the surgery has been communicated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the indication for the procedure is documented.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assist the woman and her family in preparing emotionally and

psychologically for the procedure (page C-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-66; Operative care principles

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review the woman’s medical history:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check for any possible allergies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the woman has received the complete antitetanus

### Preparing A Woman For A Surgical Procedure — phần 2

regimen, and give one dose of tetanus vaccine, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review any potential anaesthetic risks (difficult airway), anticipated

difficulty with the procedure, anticipated duration of the procedure and blood loss, and any specific concerns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Send a blood sample for haemoglobin (or haematocrit), type and

screen, and order blood for possible transfusion. Do not delay transfusion if needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer premedication appropriate for the anaesthesia used

(page C-56).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give an antacid (sodium citrate 0.3% 30 mL or magnesium trisilicate

300 mg) to reduce stomach acid in case there is aspiration.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure prophylactic intravenous antibiotics have been ordered and

given at least 15–60 minutes before surgery.

### Preparing A Woman For A Surgical Procedure — phần 3

For caesarean birth, prophylactic antibiotics should be given prior to skin incision, rather than intra-operatively after umbilical cord clamping.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash the area around the proposed incision site with soap and water,

if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not shave the woman’s pubic hair as this increases the risk of

wound infection. The hair may be trimmed, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record vital signs (blood pressure, pulse, respiratory rate,

temperature, oxygen saturation, and fetal heart rate, if applicable).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm that the indication for the procedure is still valid just before

the start of the procedure, as this might have changed, rendering the need for the procedure obsolete (e.g. an emergency caesarean birth for fetal distress may have to be abandoned if the baby is found to have died before the procedure is started).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Operative care principles; C-67

### Preparing A Woman For A Surgical Procedure — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder if necessary and monitor urine output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that all relevant information is passed on to other members of

the team (doctor/midwife, nurse, anaesthetist, assistant and others).

## Position

### Position — phần 1

Place the woman in a position appropriate for the procedure to allow:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the optimum exposure of the operative site;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; access for the anaesthetist;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; access for the nurse to take vital signs and monitor IV drugs and infusions;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the safety of the woman, preventing injuries and maintaining

circulation; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; maintenance of the woman’s dignity and modesty.

Note: If the woman has not given birth, tilt the operating table to the left or place a pillow or folded linen under her right lower back to decrease supine hypotension syndrome.

## Surgical Handscrub

### Surgical Handscrub — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove all jewellery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold hands above the level of the elbow, wet hands thoroughly and

apply soap (preferably an iodophore such as betadine).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Begin at the fingertips and lather and wash, using a circular motion:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash between all fingers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Move from the fingertips to the elbows of one hand and then

repeat with the second hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash for three to five minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rinse each arm separately, fingertips first, holding hands above the

level of the elbows.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-68; Operative care principles

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dry hands with a sterile towel, wiping from the fingertips to the

elbows, or allow hands to air dry.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that scrubbed hands do not come into contact with objects (e.g.

### Surgical Handscrub — phần 2

equipment, protective gown) that are not sterile. If the hands touch a contaminated surface, repeat surgical handscrub.

## Preparing The Incision Site

### Preparing The Incision Site — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin with an antiseptic (e.g. iodophors):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply antiseptic solution three times to the incision site using a

sterile ring forceps and a cotton or gauze swab. If the swab is held with a gloved hand, do not contaminate the glove by touching unprepared skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Begin at the proposed incision site and work outward in a circular

motion away from the incision site.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; At the edge of the sterile field, discard the swab.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Never go back to the middle of the prepared area with the same swab.

Keep your arms and elbows high, and keep surgical dress away from the surgical field.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Drape the woman immediately after the area is prepared to avoid

contamination:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the drape has a window, place the window directly over the

incision site first.

### Preparing The Incision Site — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Unfold the drape away from the incision site to avoid

contamination.

## Monitoring

### Monitoring — phần 1

Monitor the woman’s condition regularly throughout the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (blood pressure, pulse, and respiratory rate), level

of consciousness and blood loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Record the findings on a monitoring sheet to allow quick recognition

if the woman’s condition deteriorates.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain adequate hydration throughout surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Operative care principles; C-69

## Managing Pain

### Managing Pain — phần 1

Maintain adequate pain management throughout the procedure (page C-55). Women who are comfortable during a procedure are less likely to move and cause injury to themselves. Pain management can include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; emotional support and encouragement

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; local anaesthesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; regional anaesthesia (e.g. spinal)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; general anaesthesia.

## Antibiotics

### Antibiotics — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give prophylactic antibiotics before starting the procedure

(page C-50).

## Making The Incision

### Making The Incision — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When choosing the incision type, carefully consider the woman’s past

surgical history, the indication for surgery, and potential technical difficulties and complications. For example, for an obstructed labour case with Bandl’s ring formation, a sub-umbilical midline incision may be preferable to a Pfannenstiel’s incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make the incision only as large as necessary for the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make the incision with great care and proceed one layer at a time.

## Handling Tissue

### Handling Tissue — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Handle tissue gently.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When using clamps, close the clamp only one ratchet (click), when

possible. This will minimize discomfort and reduce the amount of dead tissue that remains behind at the end of the procedure, thus decreasing the risk of infection.

## Haemostasis

### Haemostasis — phần 1

Haemostasis is the process in which blood is maintained in a fluid state and confined to the vascular system.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-70; Operative care principles

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure haemostasis throughout the procedure to help maintain

adequate visualization and to optimize outcomes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women with obstetrical complications often have anaemia. Therefore,

keep blood loss to a minimum.

## Instruments And Sharps

### Instruments And Sharps — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start and finish the procedure with a count of instruments, sharps and

sponges:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform the count every time a body cavity (e.g. uterus) is closed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Document in the woman’s record that the surgical counts were

correct.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use instruments, especially sharps, carefully to reduce the risk of

injury (page C-32). Use “safe zones” when handling and passing instruments and sharps:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use a pan such as a kidney basin to carry and pass sharp items,

and pass suture needles on a needle holder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, pass the instrument with the handle, rather than the

sharp end, pointing toward the receiver.

## Drainage

### Drainage — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Always leave an abdominal drain in place if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; bleeding persists after hysterectomy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a clotting disorder is suspected;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; infection is present or suspected; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a bladder or ureteral injury is suspected or has been identified and

repaired.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A closed drainage system can be used, or a corrugated rubber drain

can be placed through the abdominal wall or pouch of Douglas.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the drain once the infection has cleared or when no pus or

blood-stained fluid has drained for 48 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Operative care principles; C-71

## Suture

### Suture — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Select the appropriate type and size of suture for the tissue

(Table C-7). Sizes are reported by the number of “0”s:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A smaller suture has a greater number of “0”s (e.g. a 000 [3-0]

suture is smaller than a 00 [2-0] suture); a suture labelled as “1” is larger in diameter than a “0” suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A suture that is too small will be weak and may break easily; a

suture that is too large in diameter will tear through tissue.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Refer to the section describing specific procedures for the

recommended size and type of suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-7.; Recommended suture types

- Dòng dữ liệu: Suture Type; Tissue; Recommended Number of

Knots

- Dòng dữ liệu: Plain catgut; Fallopian tube; 3a

- Dòng dữ liệu: Chromic catgut; Muscle; 3a

- Dòng dữ liệu: Polyglycolic; Muscle, fascia, skin; 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Nylon; Skin; 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: Silk; Skin, bowel; 3a

### Suture — phần 2

a These are natural sutures. Do not use more than three knots because this will abrade the

suture and weaken the knot.

## Dressing

### Dressing — phần 1

At the conclusion of surgery, cover the surgical wound with a sterile dressing (page C-73).

## Initial Care

### Initial Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the woman in the recovery position:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position the woman on her side with her head slightly extended to

ensure a clear airway.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place her upper arm in front of her body for easy access to check

blood pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-72; Operative care principles

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place legs so that they are flexed, with the upper leg slightly more

flexed than the lower to maintain balance.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the woman’s condition immediately after the procedure:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check vital signs (blood pressure, pulse, respiratory rate,

temperature) and oxygen saturation every 15 minutes during the first hour, then every 30 minutes for the next hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Evaluate for airway obstruction, hypoxia, haemorrhage (internal

### Initial Care — phần 2

and external), hypertension and/or hypotension, postoperative pain, hypothermia/shivering, vomiting and aspiration, and residual narcosis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the woman’s level of consciousness every 15 minutes until

she is alert. Note: Ensure that the woman has constant supervision until conscious.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that there is a clear airway and adequate ventilation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse if necessary (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vital signs become unstable or if the haematocrit continues to fall

despite transfusion, quickly return to the operating theatre because bleeding might be the cause.

## Gastrointestinal Function

### Gastrointestinal Function — phần 1

Gastrointestinal function typically returns rapidly for obstetrical patients. For most uncomplicated procedures, bowel function should be normal within 12 hours of surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the surgical procedure was uncomplicated, give the woman a

liquid diet and advance to a regular diet as tolerated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there were signs of infection, or if the caesarean was for

obstructed labour or uterine rupture, wait until bowel sounds are heard before giving liquids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is receiving IV fluids, continue the fluids until she is

taking liquids well.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If you anticipate that the woman will receive IV fluids for 48 hours

or more, infuse a balanced electrolyte solution (e.g. potassium chloride 1.5 g in 1 L IV fluids).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Operative care principles; C-73

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman receives IV fluids for more than 48 hours, monitor

### Gastrointestinal Function — phần 2

electrolytes every 48 hours. Prolonged infusion of IV fluids can alter electrolyte balance.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Whenever a woman is receiving IV fluids, monitor her intake and

output closely.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is eating a regular diet before discharge from

the hospital.

## Dressing And Wound Care

### Dressing And Wound Care — phần 1

The dressing is a protective barrier against infection while a healing process known as “re-epithelialization” occurs. Keep the dressing on the wound for the first day after surgery to protect against infection while re-epithelialization occurs. Thereafter, a dressing is not necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If blood or fluid is leaking through the initial dressing, do not

change the dressing:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reinforce the dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Monitor the amount of blood/fluid lost by outlining the blood

stain on the dressing with a pen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If bleeding increases or the blood stain covers half the

dressing or more, remove the dressing and inspect the wound. Replace with another sterile dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the dressing comes loose, reinforce with more tape rather than

removing the dressing. This will help maintain the sterility of the dressing and reduce the risk of wound infection.

### Dressing And Wound Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Change the dressing using sterile technique.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The wound should be clean and dry, without evidence of infection or

seroma, before the woman’s discharge from the hospital.

## Analgesia

### Analgesia — phần 1

Adequate postoperative pain control is important (page C-55). A woman who is in severe pain does not recover well. Note: Avoid over-sedation as this will limit mobility, which is important during the postoperative period.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-74; Operative care principles

## Bladder Care

### Bladder Care — phần 1

A urinary catheter may be required for some procedures. Early catheter removal reduces the risk of infection and encourages the woman to walk.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the urine is clear, remove the catheter eight hours after surgery or

after the first postoperative night.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the urine is not clear, leave the catheter in place until the urine is

clear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wait 48 hours after surgery before removing the catheter if there was:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; uterine rupture

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prolonged or obstructed labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; massive perineal oedema

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; puerperal sepsis with pelvic peritonitis.

Note: Ensure that the urine is clear before removing the catheter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the bladder was injured (from either uterine rupture or during

caesarean or laparotomy):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Leave the catheter in place for a minimum of seven days and until

the urine is clear.

### Bladder Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman is not currently receiving antibiotics, give

nitrofurantoin 100 mg by mouth once daily until the catheter is removed, for prophylaxis against cystitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is a suspicion of a bladder injury (e.g. the woman has

had prolonged obstructed labour), consider leaving the catheter in for seven days.

## Antibiotics

### Antibiotics — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there were signs of infection or the woman currently has a fever,

continue antibiotics until the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49).

## Suture Removal

### Suture Removal — phần 1

Major support for abdominal incisions comes from the closure of the fascial layer. Remove skin sutures five days after surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Operative care principles; C-75

## Fever

### Fever — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A fever (temperature of 38°C or more) that occurs postoperatively

should be evaluated (page S-127).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is fever-free for a minimum of

24 hours before discharge from the hospital.

## Ambulation

### Ambulation — phần 1

Ambulation enhances circulation, encourages deep breathing and stimulates the return of normal gastrointestinal function. Encourage foot and leg exercises and mobilize as soon as possible, usually within 24 hours. Encourage the woman to mobilize as soon as appropriate after giving birth, taking gentle exercise and making time to rest during the postnatal period.

## Safe Surgery Checklist

### Safe Surgery Checklist — phần 1

The World Health Organization (WHO) has undertaken a number of global and regional initiatives to address surgical safety, including the development of the WHO Surgical Safety Checklist (WHO, 2009). Countries are encouraged to adapt the checklist to their practice settings and circumstances using the checklist’s accompanying implementation manual.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-76; Operative care principles

## Normal Labour And Childbirth C-77

### Normal Labour And Childbirth C-77 — phần 1

Every woman has a right to the highest attainable standard of health, the right to dignified, respectful health care throughout pregnancy and childbirth, and the right to be free from violence and discrimination. Abuse, neglect or disrespect during childbirth is a violation of a woman’s fundamental human rights, as described in internationally adopted human rights standards and principles.

## Diagnosis And Confirmation Of Labour

### Diagnosis And Confirmation Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Suspect or anticipate labour if a woman has:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; intermittent abdominal pain after 22 weeks of gestation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abdominal pain/contractions often associated with blood-stained

mucus discharge (show); and/or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; watery vaginal discharge or a sudden gush of water.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm the onset of labour if there is:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical effacement—the progressive shortening and thinning of

the cervix during labour; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical dilatation—the increase in diameter of the cervical

opening, measured in centimetres (Fig. C-4, page C-81).

## Diagnosis Of Stage And Phase Of Labour

### Diagnosis Of Stage And Phase Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Diagnose the stage of labour based on cervical dilatation

(Table C-8, page C-78).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Diagnose the phase of labour (Table C-8, page C-78) based on:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical dilatation during the first stage of labour; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the woman’s urge to push during the second stage of labour.

An incorrect diagnosis of labour can lead to unnecessary anxiety and interventions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-78; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE C-8.; Diagnosis of stage and phase of labour based on cervical

dilatation

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Stage; Phase

False labour/

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervix not dilated

not in labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervix dilated less than 4 cm; First; Latent

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervix dilated 4 cm up to 10 cm

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rate of dilatation typically 1 cm per hour

- Dòng dữ liệu: First; Active

or more

### Diagnosis Of Stage And Phase Of Labour — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Fetal descent begins

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervix fully dilated (10 cm)

Early

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Fetal descent continues; Second

(nonexpulsive)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; No urge to push

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervix fully dilated (10 cm)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Presenting part of fetus reaches pelvic

- Dòng dữ liệu: Second; Late (expulsive)

floor

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Woman has the urge to push

The third stage of labour begins with the birth of the baby and ends with the expulsion of the placenta.

## Rapid Assessment

### Rapid Assessment — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman if she has had any danger signs (vaginal bleeding,

gush of fluid or any problems such as fever, difficulty breathing, cough, leg pain, etc.) or has any concerns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid initial assessment (page C-1) of the woman (airway,

breathing, circulation, vaginal bleeding, level of consciousness, convulsions, temperature higher than 38°C, abdominal pain).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess fetal condition:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Listen to the fetal heart rate immediately after a contraction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Count the fetal heart rate for a full minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-79

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Respond immediately to any abnormalities in vital signs or other

### Rapid Assessment — phần 2

problems identified during the rapid initial assessment.

## History

### History — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman about the history of this labour: approximate time

contractions began and membranes ruptured.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman about or check her record for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; past obstetric, medical and surgical history, especially abdominal

or pelvic surgeries or past caesarean(s);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; problems during the current pregnancy (e.g. high blood pressure,

pre-eclampsia, anaemia, malaria, sexually transmitted infections) and management;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; haemoglobin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; tetanus vaccination status; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; syphilis, HIV and tuberculosis status.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the woman’s record. If she has no record:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ask her when the birth is expected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine the gestational age. If preterm (before

37 weeks of pregnancy are completed), manage as preterm labour (page S-144).

## Abdominal Palpation

### Abdominal Palpation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the woman’s abdomen for:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a caesarean scar;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a horizontal ridge across lower abdomen (if present, ask the

woman to empty her bladder and observe her again).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess uterine contractions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe the woman’s response to contractions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Is she coping well or is she distressed?

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Is she pushing or grunting? If so, manage as second stage of

labour (page C-97).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-80; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the number of uterine contractions in 10 minutes and their

duration in seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess fetal lie—longitudinal or transverse—and fetal presentation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fetal lie other than longitudinal and presentations in labour other

than cephalic are considered malpositions or malpresentations (page S-83).

### Abdominal Palpation — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess descent in terms of fifths of fetal head palpable above the

symphysis pubis (Fig. C-3 A–D):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A head that is entirely above the symphysis pubis is five fifths

(5/5) palpable (Fig. C-3 A–B).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A head that is entirely below the symphysis pubis is zero fifths

(0/5) palpable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-3.; Abdominal palpation for descent of the fetal head

VAGINAL EXAMINATION (page C-90)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess cervical dilatation and effacement:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-81

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-4.; Effacement and dilatation of the cervix

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note the status of membranes. If the membranes have ruptured, note

the colour of the draining amniotic fluid:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The presence of thick meconium indicates the need for close

monitoring and possible intervention for management of fetal distress (page S-109).

### Abdominal Palpation — phần 3

Routine antibiotics are not recommended in cases of meconium- stained amniotic fluid in the absence of other indications. Personnel experienced in neonatal resuscitation should attend the birth of infants if thick meconium liquor is noted, as meconium aspiration is clinically more significant in cases of thick meconium.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The presence of foul-smelling watery discharge indicates the need

to manage as amnionitis (page S-163).

Antibiotics should be administered when characteristics of the liquor suggest infection (page S-182).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; An absence of fluid draining after rupture of the membranes is an

indication of reduced volume of amniotic fluid, which may be associated with fetal distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If necessary, a vaginal examination may be used to assess descent by

relating the level of the fetal presenting part to the ischial spines of the maternal pelvis (Fig. C-5, page C-82).

### Abdominal Palpation — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-82; Normal labour and childbirth

FIGURE C-5. Assessing descent of the fetal head by vaginal examination; 0 station is at the level of the ischial spine (Sp)

Note: When there is a significant degree of caput or moulding, assessment by abdominal palpation using fifths of head palpable is more useful than assessment by vaginal exam.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine presenting part during vaginal examination:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The most common presenting part is the vertex of the fetal head.

If the vertex is not the presenting part, manage as a malpresentation (Table S-16, page S-88).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the vertex is the presenting part, use landmarks on the fetal

skull to determine the position of the fetal head in relation to the maternal pelvis (Fig. C-6).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-83

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-6.; Landmarks of the fetal skull

### Abdominal Palpation — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the position of the fetal head during vaginal examination:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The fetal head normally engages in the maternal pelvis in an

occiput transverse position, with the fetal occiput transverse in the maternal pelvis (Fig. C-7).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-7.; Occiput transverse positions

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-84; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-8.; Occiput anterior positions

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; With descent, the fetal head rotates so that the fetal occiput is

anterior in the maternal pelvis (occiput anterior position; Fig. C-8). Failure of an occiput transverse position to rotate to an occiput anterior position should be managed as an occiput posterior position (page S-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; An additional feature of a normal presentation is a well-flexed

vertex (Fig. C-9, page C-85), with the occiput lower in the vagina than the sinciput.
