### Abdominal Palpation — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-85

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE C-9.; Well-flexed vertex

NOTE: If there are positions or presentations in labour other than occiput anterior with a well-flexed vertex, manage as malpositions or malpresentations (page S-85).

## Care During Labour And Childbirth

### Care During Labour And Childbirth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review findings from the woman’s history, physical examination and

assessment, and make plans to care for the woman based on her individual needs and the stage and phase of labour. Communicate with the woman and her companion about the plan:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Administer intrapartum antibiotic (ampicillin or penicillin G) to

all women with documented Group B streptococcus colonization, except where there are contraindications.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide counselling and testing for HIV and syphilis if this was

not done during the third trimester of pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; As needed, provide prophylactic treatments and ongoing

management of obstetric or medical problems per protocols.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Plan to assess the progress of maternal condition, fetal condition

and labour based on the phase and stage of labour, and any identified problems (page C-88–89).

### Care During Labour And Childbirth — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide supportive care (page C-86).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-86; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman is in the latent phase of the first stage of labour and

able to be receptive to health education counselling, provide information regarding infant feeding and postpartum family planning.

## Supportive Care

### Supportive Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman has a companion of her choice and, where

possible, the same health care provider throughout labour and childbirth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to have support from a person of her choice

throughout labour and childbirth. Supportive companionship can enable a woman to face fear and pain, while reducing loneliness and distress, and can promote positive physiologic birth outcomes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Where possible, encourage companions to take an active role in

her care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the companion to give support to the woman during

labour and childbirth (e.g. by rubbing her back, wiping her brow with a wet cloth, helping her move about).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Arrange seating for the companion next to the woman.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During the second stage, position the companion at the top of the

### Supportive Care — phần 2

bed to allow the companion to focus on caring for the woman’s emotional needs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure good communication and support by staff:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Explain all procedures, seek permission and discuss findings with

the woman.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide a supportive, encouraging atmosphere for birth that is

respectful of the woman’s wishes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure privacy and confidentiality.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain cleanliness of the woman and her environment:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to wash herself or bathe or shower at the

onset of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash the vulval and perineal areas before each examination.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wash your hands with soap before and after each examination.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-87

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure the cleanliness of the labouring and birthing area(s).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clean up all spills immediately.

### Supportive Care — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure mobility:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to move about freely, especially to be in an

upright position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Support the woman’s choice of position during labour and birth

(Fig. C-10).

FIGURE C-10. Some positions that a woman may adopt during labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to empty her bladder regularly. Note: Do not

routinely catheterize women in labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to eat and drink as she wishes. If the woman

has visible severe wasting or tires during labour, make sure she is fed. Nutritious liquid drinks are important, even in late labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the woman breathing techniques for labour and childbirth.

Encourage the woman to breathe out more slowly than usual and relax with each expiration.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the woman in labour who is anxious, fearful or in pain:

### Supportive Care — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give her praise, encouragement and reassurance.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give her information on the process and progress of her labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Listen to the woman and be sensitive to her feelings.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage her birth companion to provide the same support.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is distressed by pain:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Suggest changes of position (Fig. C-10).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-88; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage mobility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage her companion to massage her back or hold her hand,

sponge her face between contractions and place a cool cloth at the back of her neck.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to use the breathing techniques.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to take a warm bath or shower.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If necessary, offer morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM,

### Supportive Care — phần 5

informing the woman of the advantages and disadvantages and obtaining consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid the following practices:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not routinely shave the perineal/pubic area prior to a vaginal

birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not routinely cleanse the vagina with an antiseptic

(e.g. chlorhexidine) during labour for the purpose of preventing infectious morbidities, even in women with documented Group B streptococcus colonization. - Do not routinely give an enema to women in labour.

## Assessment Of Maternal Condition

### Assessment Of Maternal Condition — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman’s condition:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During the latent phase of the first stage of labour: Check

maternal mood and behaviour (distressed, anxious) at least once every hour; check blood pressure, pulse and temperature at least once every four hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During the active phase of first stage of labour: Check maternal

mood and behaviour (distressed, anxious) at least once every 30 minutes; check blood pressure at least once every four hours, temperature at least once every two hours and pulse once every 30 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During second stage: Check maternal mood and behaviour

(distressed, anxious) at least once every five minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evaluate the woman for emergency signs and for signs of distress, and

respond appropriately:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman’s pulse is increasing, she may be dehydrated or in

pain or she may be developing a fever.

### Assessment Of Maternal Condition — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-89

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure adequate hydration via oral or IV routes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide adequate analgesia (page C-55).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman’s temperature is higher than 38°C, manage the

cause of the fever (page S-113) and monitor temperature at least every two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman’s blood pressure decreases, suspect occult or

frank haemorrhage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If acetone is present in the woman’s urine, suspect poor

nutrition or dehydration and encourage her to eat or drink; otherwise, give dextrose IV.

## Assessment Of Fetal Condition

### Assessment Of Fetal Condition — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Count the fetal heart rate for a full minute at least once every hour

during the latent phase, once every 30 minutes during the active phase and every five minutes during the second stage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

## Assessment Of Progress Of Labour

### Assessment Of Progress Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once diagnosed, progress of labour is assessed by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; measuring changes in cervical effacement and dilatation

(Fig. C-4, page C-81) during the latent phase;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; measuring the rate of cervical dilatation and fetal descent

(Fig. C-4, page C-81, and Fig. C-5, page C-82) during the active phase; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; assessing further fetal descent during the second stage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If unsatisfactory progress of labour or prolonged labour is

suspected, manage the cause of slow progress (page S-73). Progress of the first stage of labour should be plotted on a partograph once the woman enters the active phase of labour. A sample partograph is shown in Fig. C-12, page C-94. Alternatively, plot a simple graph of cervical dilatation (centimetres) on the vertical axis against time (hours) on the horizontal axis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-90; Normal labour and childbirth

## Uterine Contractions

### Uterine Contractions — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Count the number of contractions in a 10-minute time period and their

duration in seconds at least once every hour during the latent phase and once every 30 minutes during the active phase.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Contractions are considered to be efficient if they result in

progressive dilatation of the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In general, contractions are considered to be efficient during the

active phase if there are three or more contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The uterus should relax between contractions.

## Vaginal Examinations

### Vaginal Examinations — phần 1

During the first stage of labour and after rupture of the membranes, vaginal examinations should be carried out once every four hours, unless there is a clinical indication to perform them more frequently. Plot the findings on a partograph if the woman is in the active phase of labour.

Multiple vaginal examinations increase a woman's risk of infection, especially in the presence of other infection risk factors (e.g. prolonged rupture of membranes and/or labor.) Do not perform vaginal exams more frequently than every four hours unless there is a clear indication. Always maintain hygienic technique when performing vaginal exams (see page C-26).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inform the woman adequately about the necessity of the vaginal

examination, what to expect and information to be gained by performing the exam; address the woman’s fears and anxieties, and give her the opportunity to ask questions.

### Vaginal Examinations — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman should not be assisted in the removal of her clothing

unless it has been clarified that assistance is required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain the woman’s informed consent and permission before

conducting the examination.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not force the woman’s legs apart. Talk her gently through the exam

and wait until she is ready for you to begin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If available, use a lubricant when performing a vaginal examination; if

a lubricant is not available, wet the glove with clean water.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-91

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assist the woman in assuming the position of her choice, and maintain

eye contact during the examination to decrease any feelings of vulnerability.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the examination, provide information on your findings and how

they affect the plan of care.

### Vaginal Examinations — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At each vaginal examination, record the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; status of membranes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; colour of amniotic fluid (liquor), if membranes are ruptured;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical dilatation and effacement; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; descent (can also be assessed abdominally).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not dilated on first examination, it might not be

possible to diagnose labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If contractions persist, re-examine the woman for cervical

changes after four hours. At this stage, if there is effacement and dilatation, the woman is in labour; if there is no change, the diagnosis is false labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In the second stage of labour, perform vaginal examinations once

every hour.

## Using The Partograph

### Using The Partograph — phần 1

The WHO partograph has been modified to make it simpler and easier to use. The latent phase has been removed, and plotting on the partograph begins in the active phase when the cervix is 4 cm dilated. A sample partograph is included (Fig. C-12, page C-94). Note that the partograph should be enlarged to full size before use. Record the following on the partograph: Client information: Record the woman’s name, gravida, para, hospital

number, date and time of admission, and time of ruptured membranes or time elapsed since rupture of membranes (if rupture occurred before charting on the partograph began). Fetal heart rate: Record every half hour. Amniotic fluid: Record the colour of the amniotic fluid and the status of membranes at every vaginal examination:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; I: membranes intact

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-92; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; R: membranes ruptured

### Using The Partograph — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; C: membranes ruptured, clear fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; M: meconium-stained fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; B: blood-stained fluid.

Moulding:

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1.; sutures apposed

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.; sutures overlapped but reducible

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.; sutures overlapped and not reducible.

Cervical dilatation: Assess at every vaginal examination and mark a cross (X) on the partograph. Begin plotting on the partograph at 4 cm. Alert line: A line starts at 4 cm of cervical dilatation to the point of expected full dilatation at the rate of 1 cm per hour. Action line: Parallel and four hours to the right of the alert line. Descent assessed by abdominal palpation: Refers to the part of the head (divided into five parts) palpable above the symphysis pubis; record as a

circle (O) at every abdominal examination. At 0/5, the sinciput (S) is at the level of the symphysis pubis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-93

### Using The Partograph — phần 3

FIGURE C-11. Descent assessed by abdominal palpation

Hours: Record the time elapsed since onset of active phase of labour (observed or extrapolated). Time: Record actual time. Contractions: Chart every half hour; count the number of contractions in a 10-minute time period and their duration in seconds:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Less than 20 seconds:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Between 20 and 40 seconds:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; More than 40 seconds:

Oxytocin: Record the amount of oxytocin per volume IV fluids in drops per minute every 30 minutes when used. Drugs given: Record any additional drugs given. Pulse: Record every 30 minutes and mark with a dot (●). Blood pressure: Record every four hours and mark with arrows. Temperature: Record every two hours. Protein, acetone and volume: Record when urine is passed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-94; Normal labour and childbirth

FIGURE C-12. The modified WHO Partograph

### Using The Partograph — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-95

Fig. C-13, page C-96, is a sample partograph for normal labour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A multigravida was admitted in the latent phase of labour at 5:00 with

the following circumstances:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head 4/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 2 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting 20 seconds; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; normal maternal and fetal condition.

Note: Because the woman was in the latent phase of labour, this information was not plotted on the partograph.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 9:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetal head was 3/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the cervix dilated 5 cm; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; four contractions were counted in 10 minutes, each lasting

35 seconds. Note: The woman was in the active phase of labour and this information was plotted on the partograph. Cervical dilatation was plotted on the alert line.

### Using The Partograph — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 11:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetal head was 2/5 palpable; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; four contractions were counted in 10 minutes, each lasting 45

seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 13:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetal head was 0/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical dilatation progressed at a rate of more than 1 cm per hour

and the cervix was fully dilated; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; five contractions were counted in 10 minutes, each lasting

45 seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Spontaneous vaginal birth occurred at 13:20.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-96; Normal labour and childbirth

FIGURE C-13. Sample partograph for normal labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-97

## Progress Of First Stage Of Labour

### Progress Of First Stage Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The following findings are suggestive of satisfactory progress in the

first stage of labour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; regular contractions of progressively increasing frequency and

duration;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; rate of cervical dilatation at least 1 cm per hour during the active

phase of labour (cervical dilatation on or to the left of the alert line); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix well applied to the presenting part.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The following findings are suggestive of unsatisfactory progress in

the first stage of labour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; woman still in latent phase after eight hours of regular

contractions;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR irregular and infrequent contractions (two contractions or

fewer in 10 minutes, each lasting less than 40 seconds) once active phase begins;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR rate of cervical dilatation slower than 1 cm per hour during

### Progress Of First Stage Of Labour — phần 2

the active phase of labour (cervical dilatation to the right of alert line);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cervix poorly applied to the presenting part;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR secondary arrest of cervical dilatation and descent of

presenting part in the presence of adequate frequency and strength of contractions. Unsatisfactory progress in labour can lead to prolonged labour and must be recognized and addressed promptly to reduce the risk of ruptured uterus and other complications (Table S-15, page S-74).

## Progress Of Second Stage Of Labour

### Progress Of Second Stage Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Findings suggestive of satisfactory progress in the second stage of

labour are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; steady descent of fetus through birth canal; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; onset of expulsive (pushing) phase.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Findings suggestive of unsatisfactory progress in the second stage of

labour are:

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-98; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; lack of descent of fetus through the birth canal;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; failure of expulsive effort to develop during the late (expulsive)

phase.

## Normal Childbirth

### Normal Childbirth — phần 1

Prepare for birth by preparing all equipment and supplies and, if feasible, calling for an assistant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the birthing room is warm (25°C), with no draughts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Never leave the woman alone.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman’s bladder is empty (only catheterize the woman

if the bladder is full and she is unable to void).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assist the woman into a position of her choice, as upright as possible

to facilitate physiologic birth, and ensure her comfort in this position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer her emotional and physical support.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the cervix is fully dilated and the woman is in the expulsive

phase of the second stage (woman has the urge to push), encourage her to assume the position she prefers (Fig. C-14, page C-99).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-99

FIGURE C-14. Some positions that a woman may adopt during childbirth

### Normal Childbirth — phần 2

General methods of supportive care during labour are most useful in helping the woman tolerate labour pains.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Support the woman to push as she wishes with contractions. Do not

urge her to push, particularly if the fetus remains at the pelvic midpoint.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to monitor the woman’s condition (mood and behaviour), the

condition of the fetus (fetal heart rate) and progress of labour (frequency, intensity and duration of contractions; perineum thinning and bulging; visible descent of the fetal head during contractions).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If, after 30 minutes of spontaneous expulsive efforts, the perineum

### Normal Childbirth — phần 3

does not begin to thin and stretch with contractions, do a vaginal examination to confirm full dilatation of the cervix. Note: Episiotomy is not recommended as a routine procedure. There is no evidence that routine episiotomy decreases perineal damage, future vaginal prolapse or urinary incontinence. In fact, routine episiotomy is associated with an increase of third and fourth degree tears and subsequent anal sphincter muscle dysfunction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-100; Normal labour and childbirth

## Birth Of The Head

### Birth Of The Head — phần 1

• Ask the woman to pant or give only small pushes with contractions as the baby’s head is born.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To control the birth of the head, place the fingers of one hand against

the baby’s head to keep it flexed (bent).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to gently support the perineum as the baby’s head is born.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the baby’s head is born, ask the woman not to push.

Note: Routine intrapartum nasal or oral suction should not be done, even in babies born through liquor with meconium.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Feel around the baby’s neck for the umbilical cord:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cord is around the neck but it is loose, slip it over the

baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cord is tight around the neck, doubly clamp and cut it

before unwinding it from around the neck.

## Completion Of The Birth

### Completion Of The Birth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the baby’s head to turn spontaneously.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the head turns, place a hand on each side of the baby’s head. Tell

the woman to push gently with the next contraction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reduce tears by supporting the birth of one shoulder at a time. Move

the baby’s head posteriorly to facilitate birth of the anterior shoulder. Note: If there is difficulty delivering the shoulders, or if the infant’s head retracts against the perineum as it is born, suspect shoulder dystocia (page S-99).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lift the baby’s head anteriorly to deliver the shoulder that is posterior.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Support the rest of the baby’s body with one hand as it slides out.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby on the mother’s abdomen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Thoroughly dry the baby and wipe the eyes. Remove the wet cloth.

### Completion Of The Birth — phần 2

Note: For neonates born through meconium-stained amniotic fluid who start breathing on their own, suctioning of the mouth or nose or tracheal suctioning should not be performed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note the time of birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby warm; position skin-to-skin with the mother and cover

the baby’s head and body.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-101

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the baby’s breathing:

Note: Most babies begin crying or breathing spontaneously within 30 seconds of birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is crying or breathing (chest rising at least 30 times per

minute), leave the baby with the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby does not start breathing, stimulate the baby by rubbing

the back two or three times.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If, after drying and brief stimulation, the baby is still not crying or

### Completion Of The Birth — phần 3

breathing, call for help, clamp and cut the umbilical cord and move the newborn to a flat surface to initiate resuscitation, including initiating positive-pressure ventilation within one minute after birth (page S-167).

Anticipate the need for resuscitation and have a plan to get assistance for every baby, but especially if the mother has a history of eclampsia, bleeding, prolonged or obstructed labour, preterm birth, or infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is breathing normally, clamp and cut the umbilical cord

one to three minutes after the birth of the baby, while initiating simultaneous essential newborn care. Note: Only clamp the cord early (within one minute) if the newborn needs to be moved immediately for resuscitation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is kept warm and in skin-to-skin contact on the

### Completion Of The Birth — phần 4

mother’s chest. Keep the baby covered with a soft, dry cloth or blanket, and ensure that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is not well, request the support of an assistant so that

both mother and baby can be appropriately monitored and cared for.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Palpate the woman’s abdomen to rule out the presence of an additional

baby(s), and proceed with active management of the third stage of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-102; Normal labour and childbirth

## Active Management Of The Third Stage Of Labour

### Active Management Of The Third Stage Of Labour — phần 1

Active management of the third stage (active delivery of the placenta) helps prevent postpartum haemorrhage. It includes administration of a uterotonic medication within one minute after the birth of the baby. It may also include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; controlled cord traction; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; verification of uterine tone and, if the uterus is not well contracted,

uterine massage.

The most important intervention to reduce postpartum hemorrhage is the immediate postpartum administration of a uterotonic within one minute of birth. Controlled cord traction is optional and should only be performed by a skilled provider.

## Immediate Postpartum Uterotonic

### Immediate Postpartum Uterotonic — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immediately after the birth of the baby, palpate the abdomen to rule

out the presence of an additional baby(s).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In the absence of an additional baby(s), give oxytocin 10 units IM.

Make sure there is no additional baby(s) before giving an injectable uterotonic medication IM or giving large doses of misoprostol orally.

Note: Oxytocin is preferred because it is effective two to three minutes after injection, has minimal side effects and can be used in all women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If oxytocin is not available, give:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; oral misoprostol 600 mcg;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR ergometrine (0.2 mg IM) or methylergometrine;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR the fixed drug combination of oxytocin and ergometrine

(1 mL = 5 IU oxytocin + 0.5 mg ergometrine).

### Immediate Postpartum Uterotonic — phần 2

Do not give ergometrine to women with pre-eclampsia, eclampsia or high blood pressure because it increases the risk of convulsions and cerebrovascular accidents.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-103

## Controlled Cord Traction

### Controlled Cord Traction — phần 1

The placenta may be allowed to deliver physiologically or can be delivered via controlled cord traction by a skilled provider. Controlled cord traction is contraindicated in settings without a skilled birth attendant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After cutting the cord, clamp the cord close to the perineum using

sponge forceps. Hold the clamped cord and the end of the forceps with one hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the other hand just above the woman’s pubic bone and stabilize

the uterus by applying counter-traction during controlled cord traction. This helps prevent inversion of the uterus.

Never apply cord traction (pull) without applying counter-traction (push) above the pubic bone with the other hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep slight tension on the cord and await a strong uterine contraction

(two to three minutes).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the uterus becomes rounded (globular) or the cord

### Controlled Cord Traction — phần 2

lengthens, very gently pull downward on the cord to deliver the placenta. Do not wait for a gush of blood before applying traction on the cord. Continue to apply counter-traction to the uterus with the other hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the placenta does not descend (i.e. there are no signs of placental

separation) during 30 to 40 seconds of controlled cord traction, do not continue to pull on the cord. Instead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently hold the cord and wait until the uterus is well contracted

again. If necessary, use a sponge forceps to clamp the cord closer to the perineum as it lengthens.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; With the next contraction, repeat controlled cord traction with

counter-traction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; As the placenta delivers, the thin membranes can tear off. Hold the

placenta in two hands and gently turn it several times in a clockwise fashion until the membranes are twisted.

### Controlled Cord Traction — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Slowly pull to complete the delivery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-104; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the membranes tear, gently examine the upper vagina and cervix

while wearing sterile gloves. Use a sponge forceps to remove any pieces of membrane that are present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the placenta carefully to be sure none of it is missing. If a

portion of the maternal surface is missing or if there are torn membranes with vessels, suspect retained placental fragments (page S-44).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If uterine inversion occurs, reposition the uterus (page P-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cord is pulled off, manual removal of the placenta may be

necessary (page P-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dispose of the placenta in a correct, safe and culturally appropriate

manner.

## Verification Of Uterine Tone

### Verification Of Uterine Tone — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immediately assess uterine tone. If the uterus is soft, massage the

fundus of the uterus through the woman’s abdomen until the uterus is contracted.

Note: Sustained uterine massage is not recommended as an intervention to prevent postpartum haemorrhage in women who have received a prophylactic uterotonic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the uterus does not become relaxed (soft) after you stop

uterine massage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Teach the woman how to assess uterine tone and massage her own

uterus should it become soft.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimate and record blood loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-105

## Examination For Tears

### Examination For Tears — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the woman carefully and repair any tears to the cervix (page

P-95) or vagina (page P-97), or repair the episiotomy (page P-87).

## Postpartum Intrauterine Device

### Postpartum Intrauterine Device — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman is medically eligible, has been appropriately counselled

and has chosen postpartum insertion of an intrauterine device (IUD), either copper or levonorgestrel, proceed to postplacental insertion of the IUD just after expulsion and verification of an intact placenta.

## Two Hours After Childbirth/Birth

### Two Hours After Childbirth/Birth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the woman and her newborn in the birthing room for at least one

hour after delivery of the placenta.

Avoid separating the woman from her baby whenever possible. Do not leave them unattended at any time.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Conduct an examination of the woman and newborn before

transferring them to the postpartum ward.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the room is warm (25°C).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not discharge the woman and her newborn from the facility earlier

than 24 hours after the birth.

## Care Of The Woman

### Care Of The Woman — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clean the woman and the area beneath her. Put a sanitary pad or

folded clean cloth under her buttocks to collect blood. Help her change clothes if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman’s condition every 15 minutes for the first two

hours and respond immediately if there are abnormal findings:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess uterine tone for early identification of uterine atony.

Massage the uterus if atonic. Ensure that the uterus does not become relaxed (soft) after you stop uterine massage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure blood pressure and pulse.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-106; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Measure and record blood loss.

Note: If bleeding is excessive, investigate the cause(s) for the bleeding (uterine atony, vaginal/cervical laceration, retained placenta) and treat appropriately (see Vaginal bleeding after childbirth, page S-29).

### Care Of The Woman — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For emergency signs, assess the woman using rapid assessment

and manage per protocols.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to empty her bladder and ensure that she has

passed urine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to eat, drink and rest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman on postpartum care, nutrition and hygiene.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman’s companion to watch her and call for help if bleeding

or pain increases, or if the woman feels dizzy or has severe headaches, visual disturbance or epigastric distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the woman’s record and give any treatment or prophylaxis that

is due.

### Care Of The Woman — phần 3

Do not give routine prophylactic antibiotic treatment in the absence of clinical signs of infection or recommended treatment criteria. Routine antibiotic prophylaxis is not recommended for: • women with uncomplicated vaginal birth • women undergoing operative vaginal birth • women with episiotomy • women with first or second degree lacerations. Routine antibiotic prophylaxis is recommended for: • repair of third and fourth degree tears • premature prelabour rupture of membranes • caesarean birth

• manual removal of placenta or placement of uterine balloon tamponade.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel the woman on healthy timing and spacing of pregnancy, the

lactational amenorrhoea method, return to fertility, and resumption of sexual relations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel the woman on family planning methods that she can initiate

immediately after birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; lactational amenorrhoea method;

### Care Of The Woman — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-107

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; copper or levonorgestrel IUD (up to 48 hours after childbirth and

then four weeks or more after childbirth);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; tubal ligation (up to one week after childbirth and then six weeks

or more after childbirth);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; progestin-only implants;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; progestin-only oral contraceptive pills;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; condoms;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; vasectomy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; DepoProvera (only for women who are not breastfeeding).

## Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation

### Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When providing newborn care, ensure strict adherence to infection

prevention and control principles (page C-113).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby naked between the mother’s breasts. Cover with a

clean, dry drape or cloth, and cover the baby’s head. Skin-to-skin contact should continue for at least one hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Only interrupt skin-to-skin contact for essential care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prevent hypothermia in the newborn when the baby is not in

skin-to-skin contact with the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All newborns, including low birth weight babies who are able to

breastfeed, should be put to the breast as soon as possible after birth when they are clinically stable and the mother and baby are ready.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to initiate breastfeeding within one hour after

birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage early and exclusive breastfeeding when the baby

### Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation — phần 2

appears ready (begins “rooting”).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; To encourage early breastfeeding, position the baby near the

mother’s breasts, where the baby can latch when ready to feed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not force the baby to the breast.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s condition every 15 minutes for the first two hours

after birth and respond immediately if there are abnormal findings:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the baby’s breathing and colour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-108; Normal labour and childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the baby becomes cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty

breathing (less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute), give oxygen by nasal catheter or prongs (page S-173).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Estimate the baby’s body temperature by feeling the baby’s skin

(foot or forehead):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the baby’s feet feel cold, check the baby’s axillary

temperature;

### Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the baby’s temperature is less than 36.5°C, rewarm the

baby (page S-178).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the cord for bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cord is bleeding, retie the cord more tightly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For emergency signs, assess the baby using rapid assessment and

respond immediately (page S-165).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wipe off any meconium or blood from the baby’s skin. Do not

remove vernix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Bathing should be delayed until 24 hours after birth (and should not be

given less than six hours after birth).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess breastfeeding:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Let the infant breastfeed on demand, if able to suck.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If problems with breastfeeding are present, observe

breastfeeding, looking for signs of poor attachment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Teach mothers how to recognize and manage common problems

that may prevent successful breastfeeding.

### Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the mother/parents to watch the baby and call for help if the baby

is having breathing difficulties, the baby’s temperature is too low or high, there is bleeding from the cord, or there are convulsions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide the following care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply antimicrobial drops (1% silver nitrate solution or 2.5%

povidone-iodine solution) or ointment (1% tetracycline ointment) to both of the baby’s eyes once, according to national guidelines.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Normal labour and childbirth; C-109

Note: Povidone-iodine should not be confused with tincture of iodine, which could cause blindness if used.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide cord care. Keep the umbilical cord clean and dry. Nothing

should be placed on the cord unless recommended by the health authority (for example, chlorhexidine).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Weigh the baby and initiate special care for babies weighing less than

### Care Of The Newborn Not Requiring Resuscitation — phần 5

2500 g (page C-112).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give vitamin K by IM injection (1 mg/0.5 mL for term babies;

0.4 mg/kg for a maximum dose of 1 mg for preterm babies).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the mother’s record and give antibiotic prophylaxis to infants

who have been exposed to risk factors for infection (i.e. preterm prelabour rupture of membranes; membranes ruptured more than 18 hours before birth; mother had fever higher than 38°C before birth or during labour; amniotic fluid was foul smelling or purulent; or mother has documented colonization with Group B streptococcus) (page S- 182).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the mother’s record and give any prophylaxis that is due if the

newborn has been exposed to syphilis (page S-183), tuberculosis (page S-184) or HIV (page S-185).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oral polio, hepatitis B, BCG and other vaccinations, depending

on national guidelines.

## Safe Childbirth Checklist

### Safe Childbirth Checklist — phần 1

Of the more than 130 million births occurring each year, an estimated 303 000 result in the mother’s death, 2.6 million in stillbirth and another 2.7 million in a newborn death within the first 28 days of birth. In response to unacceptably high rates of maternal and perinatal deaths occurring during labour and childbirth, the WHO developed the WHO Safe Childbirth Checklist (WHO, 2015) to support the delivery of essential maternal and perinatal care practices. The checklist addresses the major

causes of maternal death (haemorrhage, infection, obstructed labour and hypertensive disorders), intrapartum-related stillbirths (inadequate intrapartum care) and neonatal deaths (birth asphyxia, infection and complications related to prematurity). It was developed following a rigorous methodology and tested for usability in 10 countries across Africa and Asia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-110; Normal labour and childbirth

### Safe Childbirth Checklist — phần 2

The WHO Safe Childbirth Checklist Implementation Guide (WHO, 2015) for health facilities has been developed to help birth attendants and health care leaders successfully launch and sustain use of the safe childbirth checklist.

## Newborn Care Principles C-111

### Newborn Care Principles C-111 — phần 1

When a baby is born to a mother being treated for complications, management of the newborn is based on:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; whether the baby has a condition or problem requiring rapid treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; whether the mother’s condition permits her to care for her newborn

completely, partially or not at all; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; whether the mother’s complication might have an impact on the

newborn’s health.

## Newborn Babies With Urgent Problems

### Newborn Babies With Urgent Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn has an acute problem that requires treatment, all

health care providers who might come in contact with the newborn in the first 24 hours of life must recognize and give at least initial care for the problem (page S-165). Problems or conditions of the newborn requiring urgent interventions include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; gasping or not breathing;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; breathing with difficulty (less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per

minute, severe indrawing of the chest, or grunting);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; central cyanosis (blueness);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pallor;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; oozing or bleeding from cord;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; drowsiness or unconsciousness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; movement only when stimulated or no movement at all;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; not feeding well, inability to suck;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hypothermia (axillary temperature less than 36.5°C);

### Newborn Babies With Urgent Problems — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hyperthermia (axillary temperature greater than 37.5°C);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; convulsions; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; severe jaundice (appears on the face during the first day of life or

extends to the palms and soles at any time).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-112; Newborn care principles

## Newborn Babies Requiring Additional Care

### Newborn Babies Requiring Additional Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The following babies require special care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; preterm babies (less than 37 complete weeks’ gestation);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; very low birth weight babies (less than 1500 g);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; low birth weight babies (1500–2500 g); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; babies with obvious birth defects (e.g. cleft palate, ambiguous

genitalia, spina bifida).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn has a malformation or other problem that does not

require urgent care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide routine initial newborn care (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby to the appropriate service to care for sick

newborns as quickly as possible (page C-114).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Asymptomatic (without symptoms) babies with the following

circumstances are classified as at risk of infection and require early treatment (page S-182):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; membranes ruptured more than 18 hours before birth;

### Newborn Babies Requiring Additional Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother is being treated with antibiotics for infection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother has fever greater than 38ºC;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother has confirmed maternal colonization with Group B

streptococcus without adequate antibiotic therapy during labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother is infected with HIV (page S-185) and/or syphilis (page

S-183) and/or hepatitis B;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; mother started tuberculosis treatment less than two months before

birth (page S-184).

Infants exposed to infection should be referred to the appropriate service for the care of newborns.

## Newborn Babies Without Problems

### Newborn Babies Without Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn has no apparent problems, provide routine initial

newborn care and support early and exclusive breastfeeding (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Newborn care principles; C-113

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother’s condition permits, keep the baby in skin-to-skin

contact with the mother for at least the first hour after birth. NOTE: Babies with birth weight less than 2000 g who are clinically stable should be given kangaroo mother care early in the first week of life.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother’s condition does not permit her to maintain

skin-to-skin contact with the baby after childbirth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth, cover with a blanket and ensure

that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe the baby at least every 15 minutes for the first two hours

after birth.

### Newborn Babies Without Problems — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother’s condition requires prolonged separation from the

baby, transfer the baby to the appropriate service for the care of newborns (page C-114).

## Practices When Caring For A Newborn

### Practices When Caring For A Newborn — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid unnecessary separation of the newborn from the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe strict procedures for handwashing or alcohol handrubs for all

staff and for families before and after handling infants.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide appropriate umbilical cord care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide appropriate eye care.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Promote exclusive breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use kangaroo mother care and avoid the use of incubators for preterm

infants. If an incubator is used, do not use water (where Pseudomonas will easily colonize) for humidification, and ensure that the incubator is thoroughly cleaned with an antiseptic.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe strict sterility for all procedures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe clean injection practices.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-114; Newborn care principles

## Transferring Newborn Babies

### Transferring Newborn Babies — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the mother and/or her companion why the baby is being

transferred (page C-5).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the baby warm. Wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth; cover with a

blanket and ensure that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss. Babies with birth weight less than 2000 g should be transferred in skin-to-skin contact with the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfer the baby with the mother and a health care provider, if

possible. If the baby requires special treatment such as oxygen, transfer the baby in an incubator or bassinet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Initiate breastfeeding as soon as the baby is ready to suckle or as soon

as the mother’s condition permits.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If breastfeeding has to be delayed due to maternal or newborn

problems, teach the mother to express breastmilk as soon as possible and ensure that this milk is given to the newborn with a spoon.

### Transferring Newborn Babies — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the service caring for the newborn receives the records of

labour and childbirth and of any treatments given to the mother and newborn.

## Creating An Improved Health Care Environment

### Creating An Improved Health Care Environment — phần 1

The district hospital should strive to create a welcoming environment for women, community members and health care providers from peripheral health units. It should support the efforts of other providers and work with them to correct deficiencies. When dealing with other health care providers, doctors and midwives at the district hospital should:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; encourage and thank providers who refer patients, especially in the

presence of women and their families;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; offer clinical guidance and corrective suggestions in private, so as to

maintain the provider’s credibility in the community;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; involve the provider (to an appropriate extent) in the continued care of

the woman after discharge. When dealing with community members, doctors and midwives at the district hospital should:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; promote patient, family and community involvement in strategic

### Creating An Improved Health Care Environment — phần 2

planning and improvement activities;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; invite members of the community to be part of the district hospital or

health development committee;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; identify key persons in the community and invite them to the facility

to learn about its function, as well as its constraints and limitations;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; create opportunities for the community to view the district hospital as

a wellness facility (e.g. through vaccination campaigns and screening programmes).

## Meeting The Needs Of Women

### Meeting The Needs Of Women — phần 1

To enhance its appeal to women and the community, the district hospital should examine its own service delivery practices. The facility should create a culturally sensitive and comfortable environment that:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; respects women’s modesty and privacy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; welcomes family members; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; provides a comfortable place for women and/or newborns (e.g.

birthing bed in lowest possible position; warm and clean room).

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-116; Provider and community linkages

With careful planning, the facility can create this environment without interfering with its ability to respond to complications or emergencies

## Improving Referral Patterns

### Improving Referral Patterns — phần 1

Health systems need to have formal tools and systems to facilitate referral and counter-referral and ensure continuity of care. Each woman who is referred to the district hospital should be given a standard referral slip containing the following information:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; general patient information (name, age, address);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; obstetrical history (parity, gestational age, complications in the

antenatal period);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; relevant past obstetrical complications (e.g. previous caesarean,

postpartum haemorrhage);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; relevant medical and surgical history;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the specific problem for which she was referred; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; treatments applied thus far and the results of those treatments.

### Improving Referral Patterns — phần 2

Include the outcome of the referral on the referral slip. Send the referral slip back to the referring facility (counter-referral) with the woman or the person who brought her. Both the district hospital and the referring facility should keep a record of all referrals as a quality assurance mechanism:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Referring facilities can assess the success and appropriateness of their

referrals;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The district hospital can review the records for patterns indicating that

a health care provider or facility needs additional technical support or training.

## Providing Training And Supportive Supervision

### Providing Training And Supportive Supervision — phần 1

District hospitals should offer high-quality, participatory clinical training for peripheral providers. Participatory training is skill-focused and more effective than classroom-based training because it:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; improves the relationship between health care providers at the district

hospital and auxiliary and multipurpose workers from peripheral units;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; increases the familiarity of the peripheral providers with the clinical

care provided at the district hospital; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: Provider and community linkages; C-117

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; promotes team building and facilitates supervision of health workers

once they return to their community to implement the skills they have learned.

- Dòng dữ liệu: C-118; Provider and community linkages

## Shock S-1

### Shock S-1 — phần 1

Shock is characterized by failure of the circulatory system to maintain adequate perfusion of the vital organs. Shock is a life-threatening condition that requires immediate and intensive treatment. Suspect or anticipate shock if one or more of the following is present:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; bleeding in early pregnancy (e.g. abortion, ectopic or molar

pregnancy);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; bleeding in late pregnancy or labour (e.g. placenta praevia, abruptio

placentae, ruptured uterus);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; bleeding after childbirth (e.g. ruptured uterus, uterine atony, tears of

the genital tract, retained placenta or placental fragments);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; infection (e.g. unsafe or septic abortion, amnionitis, endometritis,

acute pyelonephritis);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; trauma (e.g. injury to uterus or bowel during abortion, ruptured uterus,

tears of genital tract).

### Shock S-1 — phần 2

SYMPTOMS AND SIGNS f Diagnose shock if the following symptoms and signs are present:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; fast, weak pulse (110 beats per minute or more);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; low blood pressure (systolic less than 90 mmHg).

Other symptoms and signs of shock include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; pallor (especially of inner eyelid, palms or around mouth);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; sweatiness or cold, clammy skin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; rapid breathing (rate of 30 breaths per minute or more);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; anxiousness, confusion or unconsciousness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; scanty urine output (less than 30 mL per hour).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-2; Shock

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

When managing the woman’s problem, apply basic principles when providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; SHOUT FOR HELP. Urgently mobilize all available personnel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration, temperature).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is unconscious, turn her onto her side to minimize the

risk of aspiration if she vomits, and to ensure that an airway is open.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the woman warm but do not overheat her, as this will increase

peripheral circulation and reduce blood supply to the vital centres.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Elevate the legs to increase return of blood to the heart (if possible,

raise the foot end of the bed).

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion (two if possible) using a large-bore (16-gauge or

largest available) cannula or needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Collect blood for estimation of haemoglobin, immediate cross-match

and bedside clotting test (page S-3), just before infusion of fluids:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Rapidly infuse IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate)

initially at the rate of 1 L in 15–20 minutes. Note: Avoid using plasma substitutes (e.g. dextran). There is no evidence that plasma substitutes are superior to normal saline in the resuscitation of a shocked woman, and dextran can be harmful in large doses.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give at least 2 L of these fluids in the first hour. This is over and

### Specific Management — phần 2

above fluid replacement for ongoing losses. Note: A more rapid rate of infusion is required in the management of shock resulting from bleeding. Aim to replace two to three times the estimated fluid loss. Do not give fluids by mouth to a woman in shock.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a peripheral vein cannot be cannulated, perform a venous

cutdown (Fig. S-1. Page S-4).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to monitor vital signs (every 15 minutes) and blood loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder and monitor fluid intake and urine output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Shock; S-3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen at 6–8 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae.

If available, apply a non-pneumatic anti-shock garment (NASG) as a temporizing measure until appropriate care is available (page S-39).

## Bedside Clotting Test

### Bedside Clotting Test — phần 1

Assess clotting status using this bedside clotting test:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take 2 mL of venous blood into a small, dry, clean, plain glass test

tube (approximately 10 mm x 75 mm).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the tube in a closed fist to keep it warm (± 37°).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After four minutes, tip the tube slowly to see if a clot is forming. Then

tip it again every minute until the blood clots and the tube can be turned upside down.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks

down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-4; Shock

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-1.; Venous cutdown

## Determining And Managing The Cause Of Shock

### Determining And Managing The Cause Of Shock — phần 1

Determine the cause of shock after the woman is stabilized.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If heavy bleeding is suspected as the cause of shock:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Take steps simultaneously to stop bleeding (e.g. uterotonic drugs,

uterine massage, bimanual compression, uterine balloon tamponade, aortic compression, preparations for surgical intervention).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Shock; S-5

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfuse as soon as possible to replace blood loss (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine the cause of bleeding and manage accordingly:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If bleeding occurs during first 22 weeks of pregnancy,

suspect abortion or ectopic or molar pregnancy (page S-17);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If bleeding occurs after 22 weeks or during labour but

before childbirth, suspect placenta praevia (page S-25), abruptio placentae (page S-23) or ruptured uterus (page S-24);

### Determining And Managing The Cause Of Shock — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If bleeding occurs after childbirth, suspect ruptured uterus,

uterine atony, tears of genital tract, retained placenta or placental fragments (page S-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reassess the woman’s condition for signs of improvement (page

S-6).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If infection is suspected as the cause of shock:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Collect appropriate samples (blood, urine, pus) for microbial

culture, if facilities are available, before starting antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the woman a combination of antibiotics to cover aerobic and

anaerobic infections and continue until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

Do not give antibiotics by mouth to a woman in shock.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reassess the woman’s condition for signs of improvement (page

S-5).

### Determining And Managing The Cause Of Shock — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If trauma is suspected as the cause of shock, prepare for surgical

intervention.

## Reassessment

### Reassessment — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassess the woman’s response to fluids within 30 minutes to

determine if her condition is improving. Signs of improvement include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; stabilizing pulse (rate of 90 per minute or less);

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-6; Shock

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; increasing blood pressure (systolic 100 mmHg or more);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; improving mental status (less confusion or anxiety);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; increasing urine output (30 mL per hour or more).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman’s condition improves:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Adjust the rate of infusion of IV fluids to 1 L in six hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue management for the underlying cause of shock (page S-4).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman’s condition fails to improve or stabilize, provide

further management (see below).

## Further Management

### Further Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to infuse IV fluids, adjusting the rate of infusion to 1 L in six

hours, and maintain oxygen at 6–8 L per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Closely monitor the woman’s condition.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform laboratory tests, including repeat haemoglobin determination,

blood grouping and Rh typing. If facilities are available, check serum electrolytes, serum creatinine and blood pH.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Vaginal bleeding occurs during the first 22 weeks of pregnancy.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

When managing the woman’s problem, apply basic principles when providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and colour and temperature of skin (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even

if signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately. If the woman is in shock, consider ruptured ectopic pregnancy (Table S-7, page S-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Depending on gestational age, check the fetal heart rate and ask about

fetal movements:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

### Immediate Management — phần 2

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to listen

or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available. If fetal heart cannot be heard, suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

Note: Send a blood sample for haemoglobin or haematocrit and type and screen before infusing IV fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-8; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider ectopic pregnancy in any woman with anaemia, pelvic

inflammatory disease, threatened abortion or unusual complaints about abdominal pain. Note: If ectopic pregnancy is suspected, perform bimanual examination gently because an early ectopic pregnancy is easily ruptured.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When available, perform ultrasound with quantitative assessment of

beta-human chorionic gonadotropin as the essential diagnostic test.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider abortion in any woman of reproductive age who has a

missed period (delayed menstrual bleeding with more than one month having passed since her last menstrual period) and has one or more of the following: bleeding, cramping, partial expulsion of products of conception, dilated cervix or smaller uterus than expected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If abortion is a possible diagnosis, identify and treat any

complications immediately (Table S-2, page S-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Rigid (tense and hard) abdomen; aspiration (page P-75)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Shoulder pain; simultaneously. Seek

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Nausea/vomiting; further assistance if

required. • Fever a Clindamycin 600 mg IV every six to eight hours and gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours. If clindamycin is not available, give ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours and gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-1.; Types of abortion

### Diagnosis — phần 6

Spontaneous abortion is defined as the loss of a pregnancy before fetal viability (22 weeks of gestation). The stages of spontaneous abortion may include: • threatened abortion (pregnancy may continue); • inevitable abortion (pregnancy will not continue and will proceed to incomplete/complete abortion); • incomplete abortion (products of conception are partially expelled); • complete abortion (products of conception are completely expelled).

Induced abortion is defined as a process by which pregnancy is terminated before fetal viability. Unsafe abortion is defined as a procedure performed either by persons lacking necessary skills or in an environment lacking minimal medical standards, or both. Septic abortion is defined as abortion complicated by infection. Sepsis may result from infection if organisms rise from the lower genital tract following either

### Diagnosis — phần 7

spontaneous or unsafe abortion. Sepsis is more likely to occur if there are retained products of conception and evacuation has been delayed. Sepsis is a frequent complication of unsafe abortion involving instrumentation.

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

If unsafe abortion is suspected, examine for signs of infection or uterine, vaginal or bowel injury (Table S-2, page S-9), and thoroughly irrigate the vagina to remove any herbs, local medications or caustic substances.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy; S-11

## Threatened Abortion

### Threatened Abortion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Medical treatment is usually not necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman to avoid strenuous activity and sexual intercourse,

but bed rest is not necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding stops, follow up in an antenatal clinic. Reassess if

bleeding recurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding persists, assess for fetal viability (pregnancy

test/ultrasound) or ectopic pregnancy (ultrasound). Persistent bleeding, particularly in the presence of a uterus that is larger than expected, may indicate twins or molar pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-3.; Management options for threatened abortion by

gestational age Expectant

- Dòng dữ liệu: Surgical Management; Medical Management

Management

- Dòng dữ liệu: More; More

- Dòng dữ liệu: Less than; than; Less than; than; Any gestational age

- Dòng dữ liệu: 12–14 weeks; 12–14; 12–14 weeks; 12–14; less than 24 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: weeks; weeks

### Threatened Abortion — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Not; NA; NA; NA; Medical treatment

- Dòng dữ liệu: applicable; usually not necessary

- Dòng dữ liệu: (NA); (page S-12)

Do not give medications such as hormones (e.g. estrogens or progestins) or tocolytic agents (e.g. salbutamol or indomethacin), as they will not prevent abortion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-12; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

## Inevitable And Incomplete Abortion

### Inevitable And Incomplete Abortion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-2.; Managing inevitable and incomplete abortion

• In cases of inevitable and incomplete abortion, surgical evacuation and medical or expectant management are all reasonable management options. To determine the best course of action, one must take the following into consideration:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the urgency of the particular case (i.e. haemodynamic stability);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the skill set of the available providers and staff;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the health facility setting and available materials, supplies and medications;

and

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the woman’s preference, after complete counselling on options.

### Inevitable And Incomplete Abortion — phần 2

• Evacuation: If evacuation is appropriate and the woman prefers surgical management, examine the woman to determine if products of conception are visible in the os or the vagina and remove them with sponge or ring forceps. If products are not visible, proceed with manual vacuum aspiration if gestational age is less than 12–14 weeks (page P-75). Proceed with dilatation and evacuation (D and E) (page S-18) if gestational age is more than 12–14 weeks and the

### Inevitable And Incomplete Abortion — phần 3

majority of the products remain in the uterus. • Medical Management: If medical management is determined to be appropriate (i.e. the woman is haemodynamically stable and ectopic pregnancy is not suspected), and it is the woman’s preference, proceed with a medical protocol. - See Table S-4 (page S-13) for medical management protocols. Optimal doses for misoprostol in the setting of incomplete abortion have not been determined. However, a reasonable protocol can be found in the management table.

- If misoprostol is not available, oxytocin is an option for medical management after 16 weeks. - If expulsion of products of conception is not achieved, proceed to surgical evacuation. - Ensure follow-up of the woman after treatment (page S-14). • Expectant Management: Expectant management is a reasonable option if the woman is haemodynamically stable and prefers to avoid surgical or medical intervention.

### Inevitable And Incomplete Abortion — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Await spontaneous expulsion of products of conception.

– If spontaneous expulsion does not occur in a reasonable timeframe, depending on the woman’s tolerance for the discomfort and wait and her haemodynamic status, proceed to either medical management or evacuation of the uterus via manual vacuum aspiration (page P-75) or D and E (page S-18).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy; S-13

## Complete Abortion

### Complete Abortion — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evacuation of the uterus is usually not necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe for heavy bleeding. If heavy bleeding ensues, proceed to

manual vacuum aspiration to ensure that there are no remaining products, and administer 800 mcg of misoprostol for management of post-abortal haemorrhage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure follow-up of the woman after treatment (see below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-14; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-5.; Management options for complete abortion by gestational

age Expectant

Management

- Dòng dữ liệu: Less than 12–14; Less than; More than

### Complete Abortion — phần 2

aspiration (page P-75) in case of heavy bleedinga a Heavy bleeding: takes less than five minutes for a clean pad or cloth to be soaked

## Abortion

### Abortion — phần 1

Before discharge, tell a woman who has had a spontaneous abortion that spontaneous abortion is common and occurs in at least 15% (one in every seven) of clinically recognized pregnancies. Reassure her that the chances for a subsequent successful pregnancy are good unless there has been sepsis or a cause of the abortion is identified that may have an adverse effect on future pregnancies (this is rare). Some women may want to become pregnant soon after having an abortion.

They should be encouraged to delay the next pregnancy until they have completely recovered. Abortion care should always include comprehensive contraceptive counselling with initiation of the method of choice as soon as desired after the abortion. Before the desired method is initiated, women should be assessed for medical eligibility (see WHO, Medical Eligibility Criteria for Contraceptive Use, 5th ed., 2015).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy; S-15

needed

### Abortion — phần 3

Identify any other reproductive health services that a woman might need. For example, some women may need:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; a tetanus prophylaxis or tetanus booster;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; treatment for sexually transmitted infections; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; cervical cancer screening.

## Ectopic Pregnancy

### Ectopic Pregnancy — phần 1

An ectopic pregnancy is one in which implantation occurs outside the uterine cavity. The fallopian tube is the most common site of ectopic implantation (greater than 90%). Symptoms and signs are extremely variable, depending on whether or not the pregnancy has ruptured (Table S-7, page S-16). Culdocentesis (cul-de-sac puncture, page P-81) is an important tool for the diagnosis of ruptured ectopic pregnancy, but it is less useful than a serum pregnancy

test combined with ultrasonography. If nonclotting blood is obtained, begin immediate management.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-16; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-7.; Symptoms and signs of unruptured and ruptured ectopic

pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unruptured Ectopic Pregnancy; Ruptured Ectopic Pregnancy
