## Differential Diagnosis

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 1

The most common differential diagnosis for ectopic pregnancy is threatened abortion. Others are acute or chronic pelvic inflammatory disease, ovarian cysts (torsion or rupture), and acute appendicitis. If available, ultrasound can help to distinguish a threatened abortion or twisted ovarian cyst from an ectopic pregnancy.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cross-match blood and arrange for immediate laparotomy. Do not

wait for blood before performing surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At surgery, inspect both ovaries and fallopian tubes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is extensive damage to the tube, perform salpingectomy

(the bleeding tube and the products of conception are removed together). This is the treatment of choice in most cases (page P-131).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Rarely, if there is little tubal damage, perform salpingostomy

(the products of conception can be removed and the tube conserved). Because the risk of another ectopic pregnancy is high, this should be done only when the conservation of fertility is very important to the woman (page P-131).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy; S-17

### Immediate Management — phần 2

Autotransfusion If significant haemorrhage occurs, autotransfusion can be used if the blood is unquestionably fresh and free of infection (in later stages of pregnancy, blood is contaminated [e.g. with amniotic fluid] and should not be used for autotransfusion). The blood can be collected before surgery or after the abdomen is opened:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the woman is on the operating table before surgery and the

abdomen is distended with blood, it is sometimes possible to insert a needle through the abdominal wall and collect the blood in a donor set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Alternatively, open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Scoop the blood into a basin and strain through gauze to remove

clots.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clean the top portion of a blood donor bag with antiseptic solution

and open it with a sterile blade.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Pour the woman’s blood into the bag and reinfuse it through a

filtered set in the usual way.

### Immediate Management — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a donor bag with anticoagulant is not available, add sodium

citrate 10 mL to each 90 mL of blood.

## Subsequent Management

### Subsequent Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Before discharge, provide counselling and advice on prognosis for

fertility. Given the increased risk of a future ectopic pregnancy, family planning counselling and provision of a family planning method, if desired, is especially important (Table S-6, page S-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Correct anaemia with ferrous sulfate or ferrous fumarate 60 mg by

mouth daily for three months.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Schedule a follow-up visit at four weeks.

## Molar Pregnancy

### Molar Pregnancy — phần 1

Molar pregnancy is characterized by an abnormal proliferation of chorionic villi.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the diagnosis of molar pregnancy is certain, cross-match blood if

possible and arrange for evacuation of the uterus:

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-18; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If cervical dilatation is needed, use a paracervical block (page

P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use vacuum aspiration (page P-75). Manual vacuum aspiration is

safer and associated with less blood loss than sharp metal curettage. The risk of perforation using the metal curette is high.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have three syringes prepared and ready for use during the

evacuation. The uterine contents are copious and it is important to evacuate them rapidly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) at a rate of 60 drops per minute to prevent haemorrhage once evacuation is under way.

## Subsequent Management

### Subsequent Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Recommend a hormonal family planning method for at least one year

to prevent pregnancy (Table S-6, page S-15). Voluntary tubal ligation may be offered if the woman has completed her family.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up every eight weeks for at least one year with urine pregnancy

tests because of the risk of persistent trophoblastic disease or choriocarcinoma. Assess the woman for irregular bleeding through a history and physical examination. If the urine pregnancy test is not negative after eight weeks or if it becomes positive again within the first year, urgently refer the woman to a tertiary care centre for further follow-up and management of choriocarcinoma.

## Dilatation And Evacuation Procedure

### Dilatation And Evacuation Procedure — phần 1

After 14 weeks, a manual vacuum aspiration procedure and cannula might not be sufficient to evacuate the remaining intrauterine contents. In this case, it is necessary to use a heavy forceps to complete the evacuation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow the manual vacuum aspiration procedure steps through

placement of the tenaculum or vulsellum forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Leave the tenaculum or vulsellum forceps in place on the anterior lip

of the cervix, or replace the forceps with a ring or sponge forceps if the tenaculum appears to be tearing the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use graduated dilators to further dilate the cervix if the forceps do not

pass easily through the cervix (page P-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Take care not to tear the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy; S-19

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evacuate the remaining contents of the uterus using a ring/sponge or other

### Dilatation And Evacuation Procedure — phần 2

heavy forceps. This may require multiple passes with the forceps. Note: The uterus is very soft in pregnancy and can easily be injured during the procedure. It is important that the operator understands the depth of the uterus and that the uterus will contract and the depth will decrease as the contents are removed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When all of the remaining fetal parts and placenta are removed, pass the

manual vacuum aspiration cannula once more to ensure a gritty sensation throughout the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evaluate all of the removed contents to ensure that all fetal parts are

identified. Send the contents to histopathology if available and applicable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the tenaculum and ensure haemostasis at the puncture sites.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the cervix for bleeding and tears.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If no tears are identified and bleeding is minimal, remove the speculum

### Dilatation And Evacuation Procedure — phần 3

and perform a bimanual exam to check the uterus for size and firmness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow recommended post-procedure care (page P-73).

Anti-D Immunoglobulin In settings where the prevalence of Rh-negative status is high and Rh-immunoglobulin is routinely provided to Rh-negative women, administration of anti-D immunoglobulin should occur at the time of management of the abortion or ectopic or molar pregnancy. The dose of Rh-immunoglobulin may be reduced from 300 mcg (the dose used after a term birth) to 50 mcg in pregnancies less than 12 weeks duration. Rh testing is not a

requirement for management of abortions or ectopic or molar pregnancies where it is not available or the prevalence of Rh-negative status is low.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-20; Vaginal bleeding in early pregnancy

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; SHOUT FOR HELP. Urgently mobilize all available personnel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, volume of blood loss, whether bleeding is accompanied by pain, colour and temperature of skin (page C-1). Do not do a vaginal examination at this stage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even

if signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate and ask about fetal movements:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

### General Management — phần 2

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to listen

or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-22; Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal movements are not felt or the fetal heart cannot be

heard, suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

Note: Send a blood sample for haemoglobin or haematocrit and type and screen, and order blood for possible transfusion, before infusing IV fluids.

## Abruptio Placentae

### Abruptio Placentae — phần 1

Abruptio placentae is the detachment of a normally located placenta from the uterus before birth of the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess clotting status using a bedside clotting test (page S-3). Failure

of a clot to form after seven minutes, or a soft clot that breaks down easily, suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary, preferably with fresh blood (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding is heavy (evident or hidden), deliver as soon as possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is fully dilated, assist birth of the baby using an

obstetric vacuum (page P-33).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If vaginal birth is not imminent, perform a caesarean (page

P-53). Note: In every case of abruptio placentae, be prepared for postpartum haemorrhage (page S-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding is light to moderate (the woman is not in immediate

danger), the course of action depends on the fetal heart rate:

### Abruptio Placentae — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is normal or absent:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the woman is in labour and uterine contractions are poor,

augment labour with oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the cervix is unfavourable (has a Bishop score of 5 or

less, Table P-6, page P-19), perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Perform rapid vaginal birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If vaginal birth is not possible, perform an immediate

caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-24; Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour

## Coagulopathy (Clotting Failure)

### Coagulopathy (Clotting Failure) — phần 1

Coagulopathy is both a cause and a result of massive obstetric haemorrhage. It can be triggered by abruptio placentae, fetal death in utero, eclampsia, amniotic fluid embolism and many other causes. The clinical picture ranges from major haemorrhage, with or without thrombotic complications, to a clinically stable state that can be detected only by laboratory testing. Note: In many cases of acute blood loss, the development of coagulopathy

can be prevented if blood volume is restored promptly by infusion of IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat the possible cause of coagulation failure:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abruptio placentae (page S-23)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; eclampsia (page S-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use blood products to help control haemorrhage (page C-37):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give fresh whole blood, if available, to replace clotting factors

and red cells.

### Coagulopathy (Clotting Failure) — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fresh whole blood is not available, choose one of the

following based on availability:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; fresh frozen plasma for replacement of clotting factors

(15 mL/kg body weight);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; packed (or sedimented) red cells for red cell replacement;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; cryoprecipitate to replace fibrinogen; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; platelet concentrates (if bleeding continues and the platelet

count is less than 20,000).

## Ruptured Uterus

### Ruptured Uterus — phần 1

The amount and type of vaginal bleeding will depend on the uterine site and if rupture involves the bladder; vaginal bleeding may be modest, despite major intra-abdominal haemorrhage. Bleeding tends to be heavy when the cervix and upper vaginal wall are involved. Rupture of the lower uterine segment into the broad ligament, however, will not release blood into the abdominal cavity (Fig. S-2, page S-25). Haematuria may occur if the rupture extends into the bladder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour; S-25

FIGURE S-2. Rupture of lower uterine segment into broad ligament will not release blood into the abdominal cavity

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Restore blood volume by infusing IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) before surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When stable, immediately perform a laparotomy to facilitate birth of

the baby and delivery of the placenta.

### Ruptured Uterus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the uterus can be repaired with less operative risk than

hysterectomy (the edges of the tear are not necrotic), repair the uterus (page P-113). This involves less time and blood loss than hysterectomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the uterus cannot be repaired, perform subtotal hysterectomy

(page P-122). If the tear extends through the cervix and vagina, total hysterectomy might be necessary. Because there is an increased risk of rupture with subsequent pregnancies, the option of permanent contraception needs to be discussed with the woman after the emergency is over.

## Placenta Praevia

### Placenta Praevia — phần 1

Placenta praevia is implantation of the placenta at or near the cervix (Fig. S-3, page S-26).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-26; Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-3.; Implantation of the placenta at or near the cervix

Warning: Do not perform a vaginal examination unless preparations have been made for an immediate caesarean. A careful speculum examination may be performed to rule out other causes of bleeding such as cervicitis, trauma, cervical polyps and cervical malignancy. The presence of these, however, does not rule out placenta praevia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Restore blood volume by infusing IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the amount of bleeding:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If bleeding is heavy and continuous, arrange for caesarean birth

irrespective of fetal maturity (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If bleeding is light or has stopped and the fetus is alive but

### Placenta Praevia — phần 2

premature, consider expectant management until birth of the baby or heavy bleeding occurs:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Keep the woman in the hospital until she has given birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Correct anaemia with ferrous sulfate or ferrous fumarate

60 mg by mouth daily for six months.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Ensure that blood is available for transfusion, if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If bleeding recurs, decide management after weighing

benefits and risks for the woman and fetus of further expectant management versus planned birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour; S-27

## Confirming The Diagnosis

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a reliable ultrasound examination can be performed, localize the

placenta. If placenta praevia is confirmed and the fetus is mature, plan for the woman to give birth (page S-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If ultrasound is not available or the report is unreliable and the

pregnancy is less than 37 weeks, manage as placenta praevia until 37 weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If ultrasound is not available or the report is unreliable and the

pregnancy is 37 weeks or more, examine the woman and be prepared for either vaginal or caesarean birth, as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Have IV lines running and cross-matched blood available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Examine the woman in the operating theatre with the surgical

team present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use a vaginal speculum to examine the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is partly dilated and placental tissue is visible (placenta

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 2

praevia is confirmed), plan for the woman to give birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not dilated, cautiously palpate the vaginal fornices:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If spongy tissue is felt (placenta praevia is confirmed), plan for

the woman to give birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If a firm fetal head is felt (major placenta praevia is ruled out),

proceed to induction (page P-17).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a diagnosis of placenta praevia is still in doubt, perform a

cautious digital examination:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If soft tissue is felt within the cervix (placenta praevia is

confirmed), plan for the woman to give birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If membranes and fetal parts are felt both centrally and

marginally (placenta praevia is ruled out), proceed to induction (page P-17).

## Birth Of The Baby

### Birth Of The Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Plan for the woman to give birth if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetus is mature;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetus is dead or has an anomaly not compatible with life

(e.g. anencephaly); or

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-28; Vaginal bleeding in later pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the woman’s life is at risk because of excessive blood loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is low placental implantation (Fig. S-3 A, page S-26) and

bleeding is light, vaginal birth may be possible. Otherwise, perform a caesarean (page P-53). Note: Women with placenta praevia are at high risk for postpartum haemorrhage and placenta accreta/increta, a common finding at the site of a previous caesarean scar.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If caesarean is performed and there is bleeding from the placental

site:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Under-run the bleeding sites with sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Infuse oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or

### Birth Of The Baby — phần 2

Ringer’s lactate) at 60 drops per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding occurs during the postpartum period, initiate

appropriate management (page S-30). This may include artery ligation (page P-117) or hysterectomy (page P-121).

## Vaginal Bleeding After Childbirth S-29

### Vaginal Bleeding After Childbirth S-29 — phần 1

Postpartum haemorrhage (PPH) is commonly defined as blood loss in excess of 500 mL within 24 hours after birth, while severe PPH is defined as blood loss of 1000 mL or more within the same timeframe. There are, however, some problems with these definitions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Estimates of blood loss are notoriously low, often half the actual loss.

Blood is mixed with amniotic fluid and sometimes with urine. It is dispersed on sponges, towels and linens, in buckets, and on the floor.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The importance of a given volume of blood loss varies with the

woman’s haemoglobin level before she gives birth. A woman with a normal haemoglobin level will tolerate blood loss that would be fatal for an anaemic woman.

Even healthy, nonanaemic women can have catastrophic blood loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Bleeding can occur at a slow rate over several hours; the condition

### Vaginal Bleeding After Childbirth S-29 — phần 2

might not be recognized until the woman suddenly enters shock. Risk assessment in the antenatal period does not effectively predict which women will have PPH. Active management of the third stage of labour should be offered to all women in labour because it reduces the incidence of PPH due to uterine atony (page C-102). Oxytocin (10 IU IM/IV) is the uterotonic drug of choice for active management of the third stage of labour.

Close monitoring of postpartum uterine tone, vaginal bleeding, pulse and blood pressure is recommended for all postpartum women to ensure prompt identification of and rapid response to uterine atony, excessive bleeding and haemodynamic changes.

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Increased vaginal bleeding within the first 24 hours after childbirth

(primary PPH)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Increased vaginal bleeding following the first 24 hours after childbirth

(secondary PPH) Continuous slow bleeding or sudden bleeding is an emergency; intervene early and aggressively.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-30; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; SHOUT FOR HELP. Urgently mobilize all available personnel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even

if signs of shock are not present, keep in mind that shock could develop as you evaluate the woman further because her status might worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Massage the uterus to expel blood and blood clots. Blood clots trapped

in the uterus will inhibit effective uterine contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxytocin 10 units IM (or IV as an infusion if an IV infusion line

is already in place) (Table S-11, page S-32).

### Immediate Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse isotonic crystalloids (e.g. normal saline

or Ringer’s lactate) (page C-34). Note: Send a blood sample for haemoglobin or haematocrit and type and screen, and order blood for possible transfusion, before infusing IV fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Anticipate the need for blood early, and transfuse as necessary (page

C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check to see if the placenta has been expelled, and examine the

placenta to be certain it is complete.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the cervix, vagina and perineum for tears.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine the cause of PPH (Table S-10, page S-31) and manage

accordingly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue close observation and monitoring of blood loss and clinical

parameters, including vital signs, during and following specific management of the underlying cause of PPH.

### Immediate Management — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-31

### Differential Diagnosis — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-32; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

## Uterine Massage And Medicines

### Uterine Massage And Medicines — phần 1

An atonic uterus fails to contract after childbirth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to massage the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use uterotonic drugs (Table S-11):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Intravenous oxytocin alone is the uterotonic drug of choice for the

treatment of PPH.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If intravenous oxytocin is unavailable, or if the bleeding does not

respond to oxytocin, use IV ergometrine, oxytocin-ergometrine fixed dose, or a prostaglandin (including misoprostol).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If oxytocin and other uterotonics fail to stop the bleeding or if the

bleeding may be partly due to trauma, administer tranexamic acid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check placenta again for completeness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of retained placental fragments (absence of a

portion of maternal surface or torn membranes with vessels), remove remaining placental tissue (page S-44).

### Uterine Massage And Medicines — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess clotting status using a bedside clotting test (page S-3).

Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes, or a soft clot that breaks down easily, suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

### Uterine Massage And Medicines — phần 4

Prostaglandins should not be given intravenously. They can be fatal.

## Bimanual Uterine Compression

### Bimanual Uterine Compression — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues in spite of management above, perform bimanual

compression of the uterus until appropriate care is available (Fig. S-4, page S-34):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wearing sterile gloves, insert a hand into the vagina and remove any

blood clots from the lower part of the uterus or cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Form a fist.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the fist into the anterior fornix and apply pressure against the

anterior wall of the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; With the other hand, press deeply into the abdomen behind the

uterus, applying pressure against the posterior wall of the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Maintain compression until bleeding is controlled and the uterus

contracts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-34; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-4.; Bimanual compression of the uterus

## External Aortic Compression

### External Aortic Compression — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Compress the aorta (Fig. S-5, page S-35) as a temporizing measure

until appropriate care is available:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply downward pressure with a closed fist over the abdominal

aorta directly through the abdominal wall.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The point of compression is just above the umbilicus and slightly

to the left.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Aortic pulsations can be felt easily through the anterior abdominal

wall in the immediate postpartum period.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; With the other hand, palpate the femoral pulse to check the

adequacy of compression:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the pulse is palpable during compression, the pressure

exerted by the fist is inadequate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the femoral pulse is not palpable, the pressure exerted is

adequate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Maintain compression until bleeding is controlled or alternative

measures can be taken.

### External Aortic Compression — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-35

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-5.; Compression of abdominal aorta and palpation of

femoral pulse

Packing the uterus is ineffective and wastes precious time.

## Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues in spite of bimanual and aortic compression,

perform intrauterine balloon tamponade (Fig. S-6, page S-38):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When a balloon catheter designed specifically for treatment of

PPH is not available, low-cost adaptations such as a condom balloon tamponade can function as a substitute (page S-36).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Before performing the procedure for condom balloon tamponade:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Provide emotional support and encouragement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Insert an indwelling Foley catheter into the bladder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Procedure for condom balloon tamponade:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Review general care principles (page C-25). Apply antiseptic

solution to the perineal area and vagina (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prior to commencing procedure, secure a condom to the end of a

Foley catheter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform a vaginal examination to identify the cervix.

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-36; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently insert a high-level disinfected or sterile speculum into the

vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently grasp the anterior lip of the cervix with a ring or sponge

forceps. Note: A ring or sponge forceps is preferable, as it is less likely than the tenaculum to tear the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the catheter with a high-level disinfected or sterile forceps

and gently introduce it through the cervix. Ensure that the inflatable bulb or catheter is beyond the internal cervical os.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the balloon end has been placed in the uterine cavity, inflate

the condom with 300–500 mL of warm saline solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Inflate the condom until it is visible in the cervix. Beware of

overfilling the balloon as this might cause the balloon to bulge out of the cervix and be expelled.

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is no bleeding through the cervix, the tamponade test is

positive. No further fluid is added and further surgery is not required at this stage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If bleeding continues, consider surgical interventions (see page

S-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Pack the upper vagina with roller gauze to prevent expulsion of

the balloon.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Palpate the uterine fundus abdominally and mark with a pen.

Note: This mark is used as a reference line from which any uterine enlargement or distension would be noted during the period of observation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer`s

lactate) at 60 drops per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A single dose of antibiotics (ampicillin or first-generation

cephalosporin) is recommended:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

Post-procedure care:

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Monitor vital signs, uterine fundal height and vaginal bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Monitor urine output every hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-37

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After 6–24 hours, if the uterus fundus remains at the same level

and there is no active vaginal bleeding, deflate the balloon 50–100 mL every hour as long as there is no further bleeding at each interval.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is no further vaginal bleeding 30 minutes after the

balloon is totally deflated, remove the balloon and stop the oxytocin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman starts to bleed when the balloon is deflated or

the oxytocin has stopped, reinflate the balloon and recommence the oxytocin infusion; prepare for surgical interventions when her condition becomes stable.

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the urinary catheter once the woman is stable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-38; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-6.; Intrauterine balloon tamponade

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-39

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-3.; Non-pneumatic anti-shock garment

If available, apply a non-pneumatic anti-shock garment (NASG) as a temporizing measure until appropriate care is available. An NASG applies pressure to the lower body and abdomen, thereby stabilizing vital signs and resolving hypovolaemic shock. Follow the manufacturer’s instructions below to apply and remove the NASG.

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 6

Application 1. Place the NASG under the woman, with the top edge at the level of her lowest rib. 2. Close segments 1 tightly around the ankles; check for snap sound. 3. Close segments 2 tightly around each calf; check for snap sound; leave the knee free so that the leg can bend. 4. Close segments 3 tightly around each thigh; check for snap sound; leave the knee free so that the leg can bend. 5. Close segment 4 around pelvis with lower edge at level of pubic bone.

6. Close segment 5 with pressure ball over the umbilicus. 7. Finish closing the NASG using segment 6.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 1&2; 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: 4; 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: 6; 7

Note: • Segments 1, 2 and 3 can be applied by two persons simultaneously. • Segments 4, 5 and 6 should only be applied by one person. • Make sure the woman can breathe normally with segment 6 in place.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-40; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 7

Removal 1. Only remove the NASG when the woman has been stable for two hours (bleeding less than 50 mL per hour; pulse less than 100 beats per minute; blood pressure [BP] greater than 90/60 mmHg). 2. The NASG should only be removed by clinicians who have been trained to do so. 3. Take pulse and BP. Confirm that both are stable. Simultaneously remove segments 1 from around both ankles. Wait 15 minutes. Take pulse and BP. If no change:

4. Simultaneously remove segments 2 from around both calves. Wait 15 minutes. Take pulse and BP. If no change: 5. Simultaneously remove segments 3 from around both thighs. Wait 15 minutes. Take pulse and BP. If no change: 6. Remove segment 4 from around pelvis. Wait 15 minutes. Take pulse and BP. If no change: 7. Simultaneously remove segments 5 and 6 from around abdomen. Wait 15 minutes before allowing the woman to sit up.

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3; 4; 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: 6; 7; 7

### Intrauterine Balloon Tamponade — phần 8

Caution: If BP falls by 20 mm/HG or pulse increases by 20 bpm after any segment is removed, rapidly replace all segments in any order and consider the need for more saline or blood transfusion.

Adapted from WHO Compendium of Innovative Health Technologies for Low-Resource Settings, 2015.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-41

## Surgical Interventions In The Treatment Of Pph

### Surgical Interventions In The Treatment Of Pph — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding does not stop in spite of treatment with uterotonics,

other available conservative interventions (e.g. uterine massage, balloon tamponade), and external or internal pressure on the uterus, surgical interventions should be initiated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Conservative approaches should be tried first, followed— if these are

not successful—by more invasive procedures. For example, compression sutures may be attempted first; if that intervention fails, uterine or utero-ovarian ligation (page P-117) can be tried. If life-threatening bleeding continues even after ligation, subtotal (also called supracervical or total) hysterectomy should be performed.

## Uterine Compression Sutures

### Uterine Compression Sutures — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that an IV infusion is in place with 20 units of oxytocin in 1 L

of normal saline at 40 drops per minute (0.04 IU/minute or 2 mL/minute) (Table P-8, page P-26).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide appropriate anaesthesia, as may be indicated (page C-55).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen (page P-54); reflect the uterovesical fold.

Applying Compression Sutures

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lift the uterus out of the pelvis in order to apply bimanual pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; To ensure that the uterus remains exteriorized and compressed

### Uterine Compression Sutures — phần 2

throughout the procedure, an assistant should compress the uterus with both hands. Note: If vaginal bleeding stops with bimanual compression, there is a good chance that the compression suture will be successful.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Using a 36-inch long 0 or 1 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture

with round-bodied needle, apply the following sutures (Fig. S-7, page S-42):

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-42; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the first stitch in the lower segment of the uterus,

approximately 3 cm from the edge of the right border and 3 cm below the level where the peritoneum was opened. Thread the suture through the uterine cavity to emerge 4 cm above the insertion point and 4 cm from the edge of the right border. Note: The needle should not completely penetrate the entire thickness of the wall of the uterine cavity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Loop the suture over the fundus and re-enter the right lower

### Uterine Compression Sutures — phần 3

uterine segment through the posterior wall, symmetrical to that on the front wall.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cross the suture to the other side of the lower uterine segment and

exit through the left posterior wall.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Loop the suture back over the fundus to enter the left anterior

lower uterine segment opposite and parallel to the initial stitches.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Pull the free end tightly and tie down securely to compress the

uterus, assisted by bimanual compression.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If bleeding has stopped, close the abdomen (page P-59).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-7.; Uterine compression sutures

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-43

## Uterine Or Utero-Ovarian Artery Ligation

### Uterine Or Utero-Ovarian Artery Ligation — phần 1

These conservative surgical measures can also be tried alone or together with compression sutures if bleeding is not resolved with uterotonic drugs, massage and balloon tamponade (page S-32).

If bleeding does not stop, further surgical intervention (subtotal or total hysterectomy) is required (page P-123).

Post-procedure care:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

discharge), give antibiotics as for postpartum endometritis (page S-130).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-55).

## Tears Of Cervix, Vagina Or Perineum

### Tears Of Cervix, Vagina Or Perineum — phần 1

Tears of the birth canal are the second most frequent cause of PPH. Tears may coexist with atonic uterus. Postpartum bleeding with a contracted uterus is usually due to a cervical or vaginal tear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the woman carefully and repair tears to the cervix (page

P-95) or vagina and perineum (page P-97).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues, assess clotting status using a bedside clotting

test (page S-3). Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider use of tranexamic acid (see Table S-11, page S-32).

## Retained Placenta

### Retained Placenta — phần 1

Sometimes there is no bleeding with retained placenta.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Sometimes a full bladder will hinder delivery of the placenta.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the bladder is empty. Catheterize the bladder, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that a uterotonic drug has been given as part of active

management of the third stage and apply controlled cord traction to remove the placenta.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-44; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

Note: Avoid forceful cord traction and fundal pressure, as they can cause uterine inversion or cord evulsion.

Do not use ergometrine for retained placenta because it causes tonic uterine contraction, which might delay expulsion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the placenta is not expelled, give an additional 10 units oxytocin

IM/IV in combination with controlled cord traction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the placenta is not expelled and the woman is bleeding, attempt

### Retained Placenta — phần 2

manual removal of the placenta (P-91).

Do not use prostaglandin E2 alpha (dinoprostone or sulprostone) for management of retained placenta.

Note: A single dose of antibiotics (ampicillin or first-generation cephalosporin) is recommended if manual removal of the placenta is attempted. Note: Very adherent tissue may be placenta accreta. Efforts to extract a placenta that does not separate easily may result in heavy bleeding or uterine perforation, which usually require hysterectomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues, assess clotting status using a bedside clotting

test (page S-3). Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

discharge), give antibiotics as for postpartum endometritis (page S-130).

## Retained Placental Fragments

### Retained Placental Fragments — phần 1

Sometimes there is no bleeding with retained placental fragments.

When a portion of the placenta—one or more lobes—is retained, it prevents the uterus from contracting effectively.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When an incomplete placenta is suspected, feel inside the uterus for

placental fragments. Manual exploration of the uterus is similar to the technique described for removal of the retained placenta (page S-43).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer a single dose of antibiotics (ampicillin or first-generation

cephalosporin) if the uterus is explored:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-45

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove placental fragments by hand, ovum forceps or wide curette

### Retained Placental Fragments — phần 2

(page P-77). Note: Very adherent tissue may be placenta accreta. Efforts to extract fragments that do not separate easily may result in heavy bleeding or uterine perforation, which usually require a hysterectomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding continues, assess clotting status using a bedside clotting

test (page S-3). Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

## Inverted Uterus

### Inverted Uterus — phần 1

The uterus is said to be inverted if it turns inside-out during delivery of the placenta.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is in severe pain, give morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight

IM. Note: Withhold or do not give uterotonic drugs until the inversion is corrected.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform repositioning of the uterus (page P-110).

Note: With the passage of time the constriction ring around the inverted uterus becomes more rigid and the uterus more engorged with blood. If bleeding continues, assess clotting status using a bedside clotting test (page S-3). Failure of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics after correcting the

inverted uterus (page C-35):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

### Inverted Uterus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

discharge), give antibiotics as for endometritis (page S-130).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If necrosis is suspected, perform vaginal hysterectomy. This may

require referral to a tertiary care centre.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-46; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

## Secondary Postpartum Haemorrhage

### Secondary Postpartum Haemorrhage — phần 1

Secondary PPH is defined as increased vaginal bleeding between 24 hours and six weeks after childbirth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding is severe, follow steps for general management (S-30).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

discharge), give antibiotics as for postpartum endometritis (page S-130).

Secondary PPH might be due to postpartum endometritis and/or retained products of conception.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give uterotonic drugs (Table S-11, page S-32).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is dilated, explore by hand to remove large clots and

placental fragments. Manual exploration of the uterus is similar to the technique described for removal of the retained placenta (page P-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not dilated, evacuate the uterus to remove placental

fragments (page P-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rarely, if bleeding continues, it may be necessary to consider surgical

### Secondary Postpartum Haemorrhage — phần 2

intervention with uterine and utero-ovarian artery ligation (page P-117) or hysterectomy (page P-123).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform histopathologic examination of curettings or hysterectomy

specimen, if possible, to rule out trophoblastic tumour.

## Care After Pph

### Care After Pph — phần 1

Women who have suffered PPH require careful follow-up and special education about self-care and risk of infection.

## General Self-Care Principles After Pph

### General Self-Care Principles After Pph — phần 1

Each woman who has been through an emergency situation will respond differently. To deliver effective care, it is important to foster good communication and demonstrate empathy. Women who have experienced PPH will likely require support from family members once they are released from the hospital. Including key support people in aftercare is important. Key principles of care after PPH are as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clearly explain the events and the treatment(s) that occurred so that

they are understood by the woman and her support person.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal bleeding after childbirth; S-47

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Educate the woman and support person on the symptoms of anaemia

and emphasize the importance of an immediate return to the hospital with any symptoms of severe anaemia, increased bleeding or persistent bleeding that does not improve.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women who have had excessive bleeding and/or intrauterine

### General Self-Care Principles After Pph — phần 2

procedures are at risk of developing uterine infections. Educate women on the signs and symptoms of intrauterine infection, and encourage them to return to the hospital with any fever, persistent pelvic pain and/or foul-smelling discharge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Emphasize the importance of good nutrition, including iron-rich foods,

to address the woman’s anaemia and improve her general health, and to facilitate milk production and breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discuss the importance of maternal recovery, neonatal development

and the role of healthy spacing in both. Discuss all available methods of contraception and facilitate initiation of the woman’s method of choice.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Schedule a follow-up visit to check on her progress and address any

questions or concerns.

## Treatment Of Anaemia And Prevention Of Future Anaemia

### Treatment Of Anaemia And Prevention Of Future Anaemia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for anaemia and evidence of haemodynamic instability after

bleeding has been stopped for 24 hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is evidence of haemodynamic instability, arrange for a

transfusion (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If haemoglobin is less than 7 g/dL or haematocrit is less than

20% (severe anaemia), arrange for a transfusion (page C-37) and give oral iron and folic acid:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Give ferrous sulfate or ferrous fumarate 120 mg orally plus

folic acid 400 mcg orally once daily for three months.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; After three months, continue supplementation with ferrous

sulfate or ferrous fumarate 60 mg orally plus folic acid 400 mcg orally once daily for three months.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If haemoglobin is 7–11 g/dL, give ferrous sulfate or ferrous

fumarate 60 mg by mouth plus folic acid 400 mcg by mouth once daily for three months.

### Treatment Of Anaemia And Prevention Of Future Anaemia — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where hookworm is endemic (prevalence of 20% or more), give one

of the following anthelminthic treatments:

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-48; Vaginal bleeding after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; albendazole 400 mg by mouth in a single dose;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR mebendazole 500 mg by mouth as a single dose or 100 mg

every twelve hours for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR levamisole 2.5 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily for

three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR pyrantel 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily for three

days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If hookworm is highly endemic (prevalence of 50% or more), repeat

the anthelminthic treatment 12 weeks after the first dose.

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; � pregnant woman or a woman who recently gave birth (i.e. is less

than six weeks postpartum):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; is found unconscious or having convulsions (seizures);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; has elevated blood pressure;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; complains of severe headache or blurred vision.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is not breathing or is unconscious or convulsing,

SHOUT FOR HELP. Urgently mobilize all available personnel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition (C-1)

while simultaneously asking her or her relatives about the history of her present and past illnesses.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is not breathing or her breathing is shallow:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check airway and intubate if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If she is not breathing, assist ventilation using an Ambu bag and

mask, or give oxygen at 4–6 L per minute by endotracheal tube.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If she is breathing, give oxygen at 4–6 L per minute by mask or

nasal cannulae.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is unconscious:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; check airway, pulse and temperature;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; position her on her left side;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; check for neck rigidity.

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is convulsing:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position her on her left side to reduce the risk of aspiration of

secretions, vomit and blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Protect her from injuries (fall), but do not attempt to restrain her.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-50; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Never leave the woman alone. Provide constant supervision. A

convulsion followed by aspiration of vomit may cause death of the woman and fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If eclampsia is diagnosed (Table S-12, page S-52), give

magnesium sulfate (Box S-4, page S-59).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cause of convulsions has not been determined, manage as

eclampsia and continue to investigate other causes.

## Diagnosis Of Hypertensive Disorders Of Pregnancy

### Diagnosis Of Hypertensive Disorders Of Pregnancy — phần 1

The hypertensive disorders of pregnancy include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; chronic hypertension (elevation of the blood pressure noted before

20 weeks of gestation or persisting beyond 12 weeks postpartum);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; gestational hypertension;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; mild pre-eclampsia;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe pre-eclampsia;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; eclampsia;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; chronic hypertension with superimposed pre-eclampsia.

Headaches, blurred vision, convulsions and loss of consciousness may be associated with hypertension in pregnancy, but they are not necessarily specific to it. Other conditions that may cause convulsions or coma include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; epilepsy

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; complicated malaria

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; head injury

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; meningitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; encephalitis.

(See Table S-12, page S-52 for more information on the differential diagnosis of hypertensive disorders of pregnancy).

## Blood Pressure

### Blood Pressure — phần 1

Diagnose hypertension in pregnancy if on two consecutive readings taken four hours or more apart:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-51

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; systolic blood pressure is 140 mmHg or higher and/or

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; diastolic blood pressure is 90 mmHg or higher.

Note: Blood pressure is in the severe range if the systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or higher and/or diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or higher. If hypertension occurs for the first time after 20 weeks of gestation, during labour and/or within 48 hours of giving birth, it is gestational hypertension, pre-eclampsia or eclampsia, depending on the presence of other features (see Table S-12, page S-52). If hypertension occurs before 20 weeks of gestation, it is most likely

### Blood Pressure — phần 2

chronic hypertension. Because some women’s blood pressure might not be measured before 20 weeks of gestation, chronic hypertension may be identified for the first time during pregnancy after 20 weeks of gestation. Chronic hypertension will persist beyond 12 weeks postpartum.

## Proteinuria

### Proteinuria — phần 1

The presence of proteinuria changes the diagnosis from gestational hypertension to pre-eclampsia. Because vaginal secretions or amniotic fluid may contaminate a urine specimen, only clean-catch midstream specimens should be used. Catheterization for this purpose is not justified due to the risk of urinary tract infection. Diagnostic criteria for proteinuria include: two urine dipstick measurements of at least 2+ (30 mg per dL) taken six hours apart; at least

300 mg of protein in a 24-hour urine sample; or a urinary protein/creatinine ratio of 0.3 or greater. It is important to rule out pre-eclampsia before assigning another etiology for the presence of proteinuria in a pregnant woman with elevated blood pressure. However, other conditions can cause proteinuria and false positive results are possible. Urinary tract infection, severe anaemia, heart failure and difficult labour may all cause proteinuria. Blood in the urine

### Proteinuria — phần 2

due to catheter trauma or schistosomiasis and contamination from vaginal blood can give false positive results. Random urine sampling, such as the dipstick test for protein, is a useful screening tool. A change from negative to positive during pregnancy is a warning sign. If dipsticks are not available, a sample of urine can be heated to boiling in a clean test tube. Add a drop of 2% acetic acid to check for persistent precipitates that can be quantified as a percentage of protein to

the volume of the total sample.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-52; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: higher after 20 weeks of; regular analgesics); S-57

- Dòng dữ liệu: gestation; • Vision changes (e.g. blurred

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Proteinuria 2+ on dipstick; vision)

• Oliguria (passing less than 400 mL urine in 24 hours) • Upper abdominal pain (epigastric pain or pain in right upper quadrant) • Difficulty breathing (rales on auscultation of lungs due to fluid in lungs) • Nausea and vomiting • Hyperreflexia or clonus

In facilities with laboratory capacity: • Liver enzymes (transaminases) more than twice the normal range • Serum creatinine higher than 1.1 mg/dL or a doubling, or higher, of the baseline serum creatinine concentration in the absence of other renal disease • Platelets less than 100,000 cells/mcL (100 × 109/L)

### Proteinuria — phần 7

- Dòng dữ liệu: uncomplicated malaria; • Jaundice; page S-121

• Coma • Anaemia

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Headache; • Convulsions; Meningitisc,d or

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Stiff neck; • Confusion; encephalitisc,d

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Photophobia; • Drowsiness

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fever; • Coma

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Headache; • Vomiting; Migrainee

• Blurred vision a If a woman has any one of the symptoms or signs listed for severe pre-eclampsia (with the

exception of proteinuria 2+ on the dipstick), diagnose severe pre-eclampsia. b The HELLP syndrome is a severe form of pre-eclampsia; the acronym stands for

“haemolysis, elevated liver enzymes and low platelets.” c If a diagnosis of eclampsia cannot be ruled out, continue treatment for eclampsia.

d Examine cerebrospinal fluid and give appropriate treatment for meningitis or encephalitis.

e Give analgesics (e.g. paracetamol 500 mg by mouth as needed).

### Proteinuria — phần 8

A small proportion of women with eclampsia have normal blood pressure. Treat all women with convulsions as if they have eclampsia until another diagnosis is confirmed.

Remember:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pre-eclampsia often has no symptoms, so it is important to be vigilant

with any woman with hypertension in pregnancy and to watch for the subtle or overt onset of symptoms that suggests worsening of disease.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Oedema of the feet and lower extremities is not considered a reliable

sign of pre-eclampsia.

In hypertensive disorders of pregnancy, there might be no symptoms and the only sign might be hypertension.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-55

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pre-eclampsia can progress rapidly to severe pre-eclampsia. The risk

of complications, including eclampsia, increases greatly once pre-eclampsia becomes severe.

### Proteinuria — phần 9

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Convulsions with signs of pre-eclampsia indicate eclampsia. These

convulsions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; can occur regardless of the severity of hypertension;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; are difficult to predict and typically occur in the absence of

headache or visual changes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; occur after childbirth in about 25% of cases;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; are tonic-clonic and resemble grand mal convulsions of epilepsy;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; may recur in rapid sequence, as in status epilepticus, and may end

in death;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; will not be observed if the woman is alone;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; may be followed by coma that lasts minutes or hours depending

on the frequency of convulsions.

Do not give ergometrine to women with pre-eclampsia, eclampsia or high blood pressure because it can increase blood pressure and increase the risk of stroke or convulsions.

## Gestational Hypertension

### Gestational Hypertension — phần 1

Manage on an outpatient basis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure, urine (for proteinuria) and fetal condition

weekly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If blood pressure worsens or the woman develops features of

pre-eclampsia, manage as pre-eclampsia (page S-56).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of severe fetal growth restriction or fetal

compromise, admit the woman to the hospital for assessment and possible expedited birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel the woman and her family about danger signs indicating

severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If all observations remain stable, allow to proceed with spontaneous

labour and childbirth (page C-77).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-56; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In women with gestational hypertension, if spontaneous labour has

not occurred before term, induce labour at term.

## Gestation Less Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks

### Gestation Less Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks — phần 1

As long as the well-being of the mother and fetus remains stable, the goal is for the woman to reach 37 + 0/7 weeks of gestation while monitoring of maternal and fetal status continues. However, it is important to remain vigilant because pre-eclampsia may progress rapidly to severe pre-eclampsia. The risk of complications, including eclampsia, increases greatly once pre-eclampsia becomes severe. Close monitoring and a high suspicion for worsening disease are important.

If blood pressure and signs of pre-eclampsia remain unchanged or normalized, follow up with the woman as an outpatient twice a week:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure, reflexes and fetal condition.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor for danger signs associated with features of severe

pre-eclampsia (Table S-12, page S-52).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel the woman and her family about danger signs associated with

severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia.

### Gestation Less Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to eat a normal diet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give anticonvulsants or antihypertensives unless clinically

indicated (see severe pre-eclampsia and eclampsia, page S-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give sedatives or tranquilizers.

If follow-up as an outpatient is not possible, admit the woman to the hospital:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide a normal diet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure (four to six times daily) and urine for daily

output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give anticonvulsants unless blood pressure increases or other

signs of severe pre-eclampsia appear (see severe pre-eclampsia and eclampsia, page S-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give sedatives or tranquilizers.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-57

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not administer diuretics. Diuretics are harmful and only indicated

### Gestation Less Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks — phần 3

for use in women with pre-eclampsia who have indications for a diuretic (such as pulmonary oedema).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor for danger signs associated with severe pre-eclampsia (page

S-57). If blood pressure decreases to normal levels or her condition remains stable, tell the woman that she can go home:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise her to watch for symptoms and signs of severe pre-eclampsia

(Table S-12, page S-52).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; See her twice weekly to monitor blood pressure and fetal well-being

and to assess for symptoms and signs of severe pre-eclampsia. If systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or higher and/or diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or higher, or if signs of severe pre- eclampsia appear, even if her blood pressure is normal, admit the woman and follow recommendations for management of severe pre- eclampsia and eclampsia (page S-57).

## Gestation At Or More Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks

### Gestation At Or More Than 37 + 0/7 Weeks — phần 1

In women with mild pre-eclampsia at term (37 + 0/7 weeks or more), induction of labour is recommended. Assess the cervix (page P-18) and induce or augment labour (page P-17).

## Severe Pre-Eclampsia And Eclampsia

### Severe Pre-Eclampsia And Eclampsia — phần 1

Severe pre-eclampsia and eclampsia are managed similarly, except that birth must occur within 12 hours of onset of convulsions in eclampsia. Note: All cases of severe pre-eclampsia should be managed actively. Symptoms and signs of “impending eclampsia” (e.g. blurred vision, hyperreflexia) are unreliable. Once symptoms consistent with severe pre-eclampsia begin, expectant management is not recommended.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer magnesium sulfate (page S-58).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-58; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration and pulse

oximetry), reflexes and fetal heart rate hourly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If systolic blood pressure remains at 160 mmHg or higher

and/or if diastolic blood pressure remains at 110 mmHg or higher, give antihypertensive drugs (page S-60). Note: An important principle is to maintain blood pressures above the lower limits of normal.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder to monitor urine output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain a strict fluid balance chart (monitor the amount of fluids

administered and urine output) to prevent fluid overload.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If urine output is less than 30 mL per hour:

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Withhold magnesium sulfate and infuse IV fluids (normal

saline or Ringer’s lactate) at 1 L in eight hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Monitor for the development of pulmonary oedema

(increased respiratory rate and/or work of breathing, rales on auscultation of lungs).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Never leave the woman alone. A convulsion followed by aspiration

of vomit may cause death of the woman and fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Auscultate the lung bases hourly for rales indicating pulmonary

oedema.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If rales are heard, withhold fluids and administer furosemide

40 mg IV once.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess clotting status with a bedside clotting test (page S-3). Failure

of a clot to form after seven minutes or a soft clot that breaks down easily suggests coagulopathy (page S-24).

## Eclampsia

### Eclampsia — phần 1

A key factor in anticonvulsive therapy is timely and adequate administration of anticonvulsive drugs. Convulsions in hospitalized women are most frequently caused by under-treatment. Magnesium sulfate is the drug of choice for preventing and treating convulsions in severe pre- eclampsia and eclampsia. An intramuscular or intravenous regimen can be used (Box S-4).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-59

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-4.; Magnesium sulfate regimens for severe pre-eclampsia and

eclampsia

### Eclampsia — phần 2

Intramuscular Regimen Loading dose (IV and IM): • Give 4 g of 20% magnesium sulfate solution IV over five minutes. • Follow promptly with 10 g of 50% magnesium sulfate solution: Give 5 g in each buttock as a deep IM injection with 1 mL of 2% lidocaine in the same syringe. Ensure aseptic technique when giving magnesium sulfate deep IM injection. Warn the woman that she will have a feeling of warmth when the magnesium sulfate is given.

• If convulsions recur after 15 minutes, give 2 g of 50% magnesium sulfate solution IV over five minutes.

Maintenance dose (IM): • Give 5 g of 50% magnesium sulfate solution with 1 mL of 2% lidocaine in the same syringe by deep IM injection into alternate buttocks every four hours. Continue treatment for 24 hours after birth or the last convulsion, whichever occurs last.

### Eclampsia — phần 3

Intravenous Regimen Intravenous administration can be considered, preferably using an infusion pump, if available: Loading dose: • Give 4g of 50% magnesium sulfate solution IV. • If convulsions recur after 15 minutes, give 2 g of 50% magnesium sulfate solution IV over five minutes.

Maintenance dose (IV): • Give intravenous infusion 1g/ hour. Continue treatment for 24 hours after childbirth or the last convulsion, whichever occurs last.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Although magnesium toxicity is rare, a key component of monitoring

women with severe pre-eclampsia and eclampsia is assessing for signs of magnesium toxicity. Before repeat administration, ensure that:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; respiratory rate is at least 16 per minute;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; patellar reflexes are present;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; urinary output is at least 30 mL per hour over four hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of toxicity, delay the next IM dose or withhold the

### Eclampsia — phần 4

IV infusion of magnesium sulfate (Box S-5, page S-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of toxicity, give the next IM dose or continue the

IV infusion of magnesium sulfate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-60; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-5.; Signs indicating the need to withhold or delay

maintenance dose of magnesium sulfate

Closely monitor the woman for signs of magnesium toxicity. To prevent magnesium intoxication, it is important to evaluate respiratory rate, deep tendon reflexes and urinary output before administering an additional dose.

Withhold or delay drug if: • respiratory rate falls below 16 breaths per minute; • patellar reflexes are absent; • urinary output falls below 30 mL per hour over preceding four hours.

### Eclampsia — phần 5

Keep antidote ready. In case of respiratory arrest: • assist ventilation (mask and bag, anaesthesia apparatus, intubation); • give calcium gluconate 1 g (10 mL of 10% solution) IV slowly over three minutes, until respiration begins to counteract the effect of magnesium sulfate.

## Antihypertensive Medications

### Antihypertensive Medications — phần 1

Antihypertensive medications should be started if the systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or higher and/or the diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or higher. Note: An important principle is to maintain blood pressures above the lower limits of normal.

The choice and route of administration of an antihypertensive drug for severe hypertension during pregnancy should be based primarily on the prescribing clinician’s experience with that particular drug and its cost and local availability, while ensuring that the medication has no adverse fetal effects. If antihypertensive medication for acute treatment of severe hypertension cannot be given intravenously, oral treatment can be given (Table S-14, page S-62).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-61

### Antihypertensive Medications — phần 2

TABLE S-13. Antihypertensive medications and dosing options for acute treatment of severe hypertension Antihypertensive Dosing Options

- Dòng dữ liệu: Hydralazine; Intravenous treatment:

• Administer 5 mg IV, slowly. • Repeat every five minutes until the blood pressure goal has been achieved. • Repeat hourly as needed or give 12.5 mg IM every two hours as needed. • The maximum dose is 20 mg per 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Labetalol; Oral treatment:

• Administer 200 mg. • Repeat dose after one hour until the treatment goal is achieved. • The maximum dose is 1200 mg in 24 hours.

Intravenous treatment: • Administer 10 mg IV. • If response is inadequate after 10 minutes, administer 20 mg IV. • The dose can be doubled to 40 mg and then 80 mg with 10-minute intervals between each increased dose until blood pressure is lowered below threshold. • The maximum total dose is 300 mg; then switch to oral treatment.

### Antihypertensive Medications — phần 3

Note: Women with congestive heart failure, hypovolaemic shock or predisposition to bronchospasm (asthma) should not receive labetalol.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Nifedipine; Oral treatment:

- Dòng dữ liệu: immediate-release; • Administer 5–10 mg orally.

- Dòng dữ liệu: capsule; • Repeat dose after 30 minutes if response is inadequate until

optimal blood pressure is reached. • The maximum total dose is 30 mg in the acute treatment setting.a

- Dòng dữ liệu: Alpha methyldopa; Oral treatment:

• Administer 750 mg orally. • Repeat dose after three hours until the treatment goal is achieved. • The maximum dose is 3 g in 24 hours. a Other treatment options should be considered if blood pressure is not lowered within

the acute treatment phase of 90 minutes with 30 mg immediate-release nifedipine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-62; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

### Antihypertensive Medications — phần 4

Once blood pressure is reduced to non-severe levels (lower than 160/110 mmHg), ongoing treatment should be continued using oral medication (Table S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-14.; Oral antihypertensive medications for non-severe

hypertension Antihypertensive Dosing Options

- Dòng dữ liệu: Alpha methyldopa; • Administer 250 mg every six to eight hours.

• The maximum dose is 2000 mg per 24 hours. Nifedipine immediate- • Administer 10–20 mg every 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: release capsulea; • The maximum dose is 120 mg per 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Labetalol; • Administer 200 mg every six to 12 hours.

• The maximum dose is 1200 mg per 24 hours.

Note: Women with congestive heart failure, hypovolaemic shock or predisposition to bronchospasm (asthma) should not receive labetalol. a Note: In some settings, nifedipine is available in more than one formulation (e.g.

### Antihypertensive Medications — phần 5

immediate-release, intermediate-release and sustained-release). Only immediate-release nifedipine is included on WHO’s Essential Medicines List (WHO, 2015) and hence in Tables S-13 and S-14. To avoid medication errors, it is important to specify and confirm the nifedipine formulation before administering it.

## Optimal Timing For Birth

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 1

Birth should be considered as soon as the woman’s condition has stabilized. The decision about the optimal timing of childbirth should be made on an individual basis, taking into account, among other factors, gestational age, maternal and fetal status and well-being, cervical favourability, and urgency.

Following an eclamptic convulsion, birth of the baby should occur within 12 hours of the onset of convulsions.

Gestation Less than 24 Weeks (Pre-Viable Fetus) Induction of labour is recommended for women with severe pre-eclampsia if the fetus is not viable or is unlikely to achieve viability within one or two weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-63

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the cervix (page P-19) and induce labour as per medical

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 2

management of inevitable abortion if the gestational age is less than 24 weeks (Table S-4, page S-13); or offer dilatation and evacuation (S-18) for expedited birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hysterotomy (incision of the uterus through the abdominal wall at less

than 24 weeks of gestation) should be avoided. Note: Before performing a hysterotomy, ensure that:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; coagulopathy has been ruled out;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; safe general or regional anaesthesia is available. Spinal

anaesthesia is associated with a risk of hypotension. This risk can be reduced if adequate IV fluids (500–1000 mL) are infused prior to administration of the spinal anaesthesia (page P-11). Do not use local anaesthesia or ketamine in women with pre-eclampsia or eclampsia.

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 3

Gestation between 24 and 34 Weeks In women with severe pre-eclampsia and a viable fetus before 34 weeks of gestation, expectant management is recommended, provided that uncontrolled maternal hypertension, maternal danger signs (e.g. severe headache, visual changes and abdominal pain) and fetal distress are absent and can be monitored. When laboratory services are available, it is advisable to monitor the maternal laboratory values outlined in Table

(page S-52) (creatinine, liver transaminases and platelets). If it is not possible to monitor maternal and fetal well-being, transfer to a tertiary care hospital is recommended. If referral to a tertiary hospital is not possible, manage severe pre-eclampsia as eclampsia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give antenatal corticosteroids to accelerate fetal lung maturation.

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 4

Antenatal corticosteroid therapy is recommended for women with pregnancies at a gestational age of 24–34 weeks for whom preterm birth is considered imminent (due to severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia), if the following conditions are met:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gestational age assessment can be accurately undertaken.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; There is no clinical evidence of maternal infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Adequate childbirth care is available (including the capacity to

recognize and safely manage preterm labour and birth), and the preterm newborn can receive adequate care if needed (including

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-64; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

resuscitation, thermal care, feeding support, infection treatment and safe oxygen use). Corticosteroids dosing and timing of birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give two doses of betamethasone 12 mg IM, 24 hours apart, or

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 5

four doses of dexamethasone 6 mg IM, 12 hours apart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the maternal or fetal condition is rapidly deteriorating,

expedite birth after the first dose of antenatal corticosteroids. Do not wait to complete the course of antenatal corticosteroids before the woman gives birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman and fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fetal growth by symphysis-fundal height (weekly) or

ultrasound, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give magnesium sulfate (page S-58).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the clinical picture is not worsening, premonitory signs of

eclampsia (e.g. increased reflexes associated with clonus, severe headache or visual disturbance) are absent and signs of severe pre-eclampsia (diastolic BP is 110 mmHg or higher, systolic BP is 160 mmHg or higher, elevated liver transaminases, elevated serum creatinine, and low platelets) are not persistent, stop magnesium sulfate after 24 hours.

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure at least every four hours and give

antihypertensive medications if systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or higher and/or the diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or higher (page S-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia persists (e.g. uncontrolled

hypertension despite antihypertensive medications, deterioration in blood tests or non-reassuring fetal status), expedite the birth (page S-62).

Gestation 34 to 36 6/7 Weeks Note: After 34 completed weeks of gestation, corticosteroids are not recommended for the indication of fetal lung maturation.

In women with severe pre-eclampsia and a viable fetus that is between 34 and 36 + 6/7 weeks of gestation, a policy of expectant management may be recommended, provided that uncontrolled maternal hypertension,

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-65

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 7

worsening maternal status and fetal distress are absent and can be closely monitored.

If any features of worsening severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia are present, or if close monitoring of the woman and fetus is not feasible, transfer to a higher- level facility. If transfer is not possible, the birth should be expedited.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman and fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fetal growth by symphysis-fundal height (weekly) or

ultrasound, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give magnesium sulfate (page S-58).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the clinical picture is not worsening, premonitory signs of

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 8

eclampsia (e.g. increased reflexes associated with clonus, severe headache or visual disturbance) are absent and signs of severe pre-eclampsia (diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or higher, systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or more, elevated liver transaminases, elevated creatinine, low platelets) are not persistent, stop magnesium sulfate after 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure at least every four hours and give

antihypertensive medications if systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or higher and/or the diastolic blood pressure is 110 mg Hg or higher (page S-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia persists (e.g. uncontrolled

hypertension despite antihypertensive medications, deterioration in blood tests or non-reassuring fetal status), expedite the birth (page S-62).

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 9

Gestation after 37 + 0/7 Completed Weeks For women with pre-eclampsia at term (37 + 0/7 weeks), regardless of pre-eclampsia severity, giving birth is recommended.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the cervix (page P-19) and induce labour (page P-17).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vaginal birth is not anticipated within 12 hours (eclampsia) or

24 hours (severe pre-eclampsia), perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute), perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If safe anaesthesia is not available for caesarean or if the fetus is

dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-66; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Aim for vaginal birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is unfavourable (firm, thick, closed), ripen the

### Optimal Timing For Birth — phần 10

cervix (page P-21). Note: Before performing a caesarean, ensure that:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; coagulopathy has been ruled out;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; safe general or regional anaesthesia is available. Spinal anaesthesia is

associated with a risk of hypotension. This risk can be reduced if adequate IV fluids (500–1000 mL) are infused prior to administration of the spinal anaesthesia (page P-11).

Do not use local anaesthesia or ketamine in women with pre-eclampsia or eclampsia.

## Referral For Tertiary Level Care
