### Referral For Tertiary Level Care — phần 1

Consider referral of women who have:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; HELLP-syndrome (haemolysis, elevated liver enzymes and low

platelets) coagulopathy (page S-24);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; persistent coma lasting more than 24 hours after convulsion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe pre-eclampsia and maternal and fetal well-being cannot be

adequately monitored;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; uncontrolled hypertension despite treatment with antihypertensives;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; oliguria that persists for 48 hours after giving birth.

## Chronic Hypertension

### Chronic Hypertension — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; High levels of blood pressure maintain renal and placental perfusion in

chronic hypertension; reducing blood pressure will result in diminished perfusion. Note: Blood pressure should not be lowered below its pre-pregnancy level.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman was on an antihypertensive medication before

pregnancy and her blood pressure is well-controlled, continue the same medication if acceptable in pregnancy or transfer to medication safely used in pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-67

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the systolic blood pressure is 160 mmHg or more or the

diastolic blood pressure is 110 mmHg or more, treat with antihypertensive medications (pages S-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If proteinuria or other signs and symptoms of pre-eclampsia

are present, consider superimposed pre-eclampsia and manage as pre-eclampsia (page S-56).

### Chronic Hypertension — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fetal growth and condition.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no complications, induce labour at term (page P-17).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If pre-eclampsia develops, manage as mild pre-eclampsia (page

S-56) or severe pre-eclampsia (page S-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If fetal growth restriction is severe and pregnancy dating is

accurate, assess the cervix (page P-19) and induce labour (page P-17). Note: Assessment of gestation by ultrasound in late pregnancy is not accurate. Use a first trimester ultrasound scan, if available, to date the pregnancy. • Observe for complications, including abruptio placentae (page S-23) and superimposed pre-eclampsia (see page S-56).

## Tetanus

### Tetanus — phần 1

Clostridium tetani may enter the uterine cavity on unclean instruments or hands, particularly during non-professional abortions or noninstitutional births. The newborn is usually infected from unclean instruments used in cutting the cord or from contaminated substances applied as traditional cord dressings. Begin treatment as soon as possible. • Control spasms with diazepam 10 mg IV slowly over two minutes. If spasms are severe, the woman may have to be paralyzed and put on a

ventilator. This may be possible only at a tertiary care centre. • Provide general care: - Nurse in a quiet room but monitor closely; - Avoid unnecessary stimuli; - Maintain hydration and nutrition; - Treat secondary infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-68; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give tetanus antitoxin 3000 units IM to neutralize absorbed toxin.

### Tetanus — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prevent further production of toxin:

- Remove the cause of sepsis (e.g. remove infected tissue from uterine cavity in a septic abortion); - Give benzylpenicillin 2 million units IV every four hours for 48 hours; then give ampicillin 500 mg by mouth three times per day for 10 days.

BOX S-6. Tetanus immunization When the mother has active immunity, the antibodies pass through the placenta, protecting the newborn. A woman is considered protected when she has received two vaccine doses at least four weeks apart, with an interval of at least four weeks between the last vaccine dose and pregnancy termination. Women who last received a vaccination series (five injections) more than 10 years before the present

pregnancy should be given a booster. In most women a booster is recommended in every pregnancy.

### Tetanus — phần 3

If an immunized woman has had an unsafe abortion or unhygienic birth (or birth attended by an unskilled attendant), give her a booster injection of tetanus toxoid 0.5 mL IM. If she has not been immunized before, give her antitetanus serum 1500 units IM and a booster injection of tetanus toxoid 0.5 mL IM after four weeks.

## Epilepsy

### Epilepsy — phần 1

Women with epilepsy can present with convulsions in pregnancy. Like many chronic diseases, epilepsy worsens in some women during pregnancy but improves in others. In the majority of women, however, epilepsy is unaffected by pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the woman closely. In general, pregnant women with epilepsy

have an increased risk of:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pregnancy-induced hypertension;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; preterm labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; infants with low birth weights;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; infants with congenital malformations;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; perinatal mortality.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aim to control epilepsy with the smallest dose of a single drug. In

early pregnancy avoid drugs that are associated with congenital malformations (e.g. valproic acid).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-69

### Epilepsy — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is convulsing, give diazepam 10 mg IV slowly over two

minutes. Repeat if convulsions recur after 10 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If convulsions continue (status epilepticus), infuse phenytoin 1 g

(approximately 18 mg/kg body weight) in 50-100 mL normal saline over 30 minutes (final concentration not to exceed 10 mg per mL): Note: Only normal saline can be used to infuse phenytoin. All other IV fluids will cause crystallization of phenytoin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Flush IV line with normal saline before and after infusing

phenytoin;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not infuse phenytoin at a rate exceeding 50 mg per minute due

to the risk of irregular heartbeat, hypotension and respiratory depression;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Complete administration within one hour of preparation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is known to be epileptic, give her the same medication

### Epilepsy — phần 3

that she had been taking. Follow up with her regularly and adjust the dose of medication according to the response.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is known to be epileptic but cannot recall details of

her medication, give her phenytoin 100 mg by mouth three times per day. Follow up with her regularly and adjust the dose of medication according to her response.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Folic acid deficiency may be caused by anticonvulsive drugs. Give

folic acid 600 mcg by mouth once daily along with antiepileptic treatment in pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Phenytoin can cause neonatal deficiency of vitamin K-dependent

clotting factors. This can be minimized by giving vitamin K 1 mg IM to the newborn.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evaluation for underlying causes of convulsions is indicated if

convulsions are of recent onset. This may be possible only at the tertiary care level.

### Epilepsy — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-70; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

## Postpartum Care

### Postpartum Care — phần 1

It is important to continue to monitor blood pressure and remain vigilant for new pre-eclampsia danger signs or worsening pre-eclampsia in the postpartum period because: • While most women with pre-eclampsia and eclampsia recover within the first 24 hours of giving birth, a minority will remain unstable or deteriorate. • Some women will present with pre-eclampsia or eclampsia for the first time after giving birth.

## Management After Childbirth

### Management After Childbirth — phần 1

• In women being treated with magnesium sulfate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If magnesium sulfate was started before the birth, continue

anticonvulsive therapy for 24 hours after childbirth or the last convulsion, whichever occurs last.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If magnesium sulfate was started in the postpartum period,

continue anticonvulsive therapy for 24 hours after the last convulsion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood pressure in all postpartum women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue antihypertensive therapy postpartum, if it was started

before the woman gave birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Blood pressures sometimes increase three to seven days after

giving birth, so it is important to re-evaluate women one to two weeks after discharge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Start antihypertensive therapy if the woman’s systolic blood

pressure is 160 mmHg or higher, or if the diastolic pressure is 110 mmHg or higher, during the postpartum period.

### Management After Childbirth — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor urine output to ensure that it normalizes if it has been low.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If oliguria persists for 48 hours after giving birth, transfer to a

tertiary care facility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman and her family to watch for danger signs of severe

pre-eclampsia and seek care immediately if they occur.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness; S-71

## Pregnancies

### Pregnancies — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel all women treated for pre-eclampsia or eclampsia about the

risk of recurrence in future pregnancies and the interventions that may reduce risk of future disease (Box S-7).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel women on the importance of initiating early antenatal care in

all future pregnancies and avoiding unwanted pregnancies.

## Postpartum Family Planning Services

### Postpartum Family Planning Services — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Due to the increased risk of recurrence of pre-eclampsia and eclampsia

in future pregnancies, it is very important that women are appropriately counselled and offered an effective postpartum contraceptive method.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women treated for severe pre-eclampsia or eclampsia should have

access to long-acting reversible (implants and intrauterine devices) and permanent (tubal ligation and vasectomy) contraceptive methods, which can be given postpartum prior to discharge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide women with the contraceptive method of their choice (page

C-106) or link them with follow-up contraception services before discharge.

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel women treated for pre-eclampsia and eclampsia about their

increased risk of future cardiovascular disease (e.g. hypertension, stroke).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess and address the woman’s risk factors for cardiovascular disease

(e.g. smoking, obesity, lack of physical activity, hyperlipidaemia) before she is discharged from the facility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Emphasize the importance of regular medical follow-up and link

women to follow-up primary care services before discharge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-72; Elevated blood pressure, headache, blurred vision, convulsions or loss of consciousness

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-7.; Prevention of pre-eclampsia and eclampsia in subsequent

pregnancies

### Complications — phần 2

• In areas where dietary calcium intake is low, calcium supplementation during pregnancy (at doses of 1.5–2.0 g elemental calcium/day) is recommended for the prevention of pre-eclampsia for all women, but particularly those at high risk of pre-eclampsia. • Low-dose acetylsalicylic acid (aspirin, 75 mg) should be initiated before 20 (and, if possible, as early as at 12) weeks of gestation for women at high risk of developing pre-eclampsia if they have one or more of the following risk factors:

### Complications — phần 3

previous severe pre-eclampsia, diabetes, chronic hypertension, obesity, renal disease, autoimmune disease and multiple pregnancies. This list can be complemented based on local epidemiology. • In women with risk factors, early detection and management is critical to the management of hypertensive disorders in pregnancy and the prevention of convulsions. These women should be followed up regularly and given clear instructions on when to return to their health care provider. Education of

### Complications — phần 4

immediate family members is equally important, not only so that they understand the significance of signs of progression of hypertensive disorders in pregnancy, but also to increase social support when hospitalization and changes in work activities are needed. Restriction of calories, fluids or dietary salt intake; vitamin D, C or E supplementation; and rest at home or strict bed rest are not recommended for primary prevention of hypertensive disorders of pregnancy; and some of these

interventions may even be harmful to the fetus.

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The cervix is not dilated beyond 4 cm after eight hours of regular

contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cervical dilatation is to the right of the alert line on the partograph.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman has been experiencing labour pains for 12 hours or more

without giving birth (prolonged labour).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The cervix is fully dilated and the woman has the urge to push, but

there is no descent.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Carefully explain the situation to the woman and talk to her about how

you will evaluate her. Listen to the woman and be sensitive to her feelings.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

listen or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, suspect fetal death (page

S-156).

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evaluate the cause of unsatisfactory progress of labour (Table

S-15, page S-74):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Review the partograph (page C-91), especially to rule out

inadequate uterine activity (Figure S-10, page S-80).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Test urine for ketones. If ketotic, encourage the woman to eat or

drink; otherwise, treat with IV fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess if the woman is anxious, fearful or distressed by pain:

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-74; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the woman is distressed by pain, encourage breathing

techniques and a warm bath or shower; if necessary, give analgesic (page C-55).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the woman is anxious or fearful, provide supportive care

(page C-86).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check for malposition, malpresentation and macrosomia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Talk to the woman and any support person she would like present

### General Management — phần 3

about findings and the proposed plan, and obtain consent before initiating treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide specific care for the identified cause.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide supportive care (page C-86).

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-15.; Diagnosis of unsatisfactory progress of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: Findings; Diagnosis

Cervix not dilated; no palpable contractions or False labour, page infrequent contractions

- Dòng dữ liệu: Cervix not dilated beyond 4 cm after eight hours of; Prolonged latent phase,

- Dòng dữ liệu: regular contractions; page S-81

- Dòng dữ liệu: Cervical dilatation to the right of the alert line on the; Prolonged active phase,

- Dòng dữ liệu: partograph (Fig. S-8, page S-76); page S-82

- Dòng dữ liệu: Secondary arrest of cervical dilatation and descent of; Cephalopelvic

- Dòng dữ liệu: presenting part in presence of good contractions; disproportion, page S-82

### Diagnosis — phần 2

Secondary arrest of cervical dilatation and descent of presenting part with large caput, third degree moulding, cervix poorly applied to presenting part, oedematous Obstruction, page cervix, ballooning of lower uterine segment, formation of retraction band, or maternal and fetal distress (Fig. S-9, page S-78)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Two contractions or fewer in 10 minutes, each lasting; Inadequate uterine activity,

- Dòng dữ liệu: less than 40 seconds (Fig. S-10, page S-80); page S-83

Malpresentation or Presentation other than vertex with occiput anterior malposition, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: Cervix fully dilated and woman has urge to push, but; Prolonged expulsive phase,

- Dòng dữ liệu: no descent; page S-84

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour; S-75

### Diagnosis — phần 3

Figure S-8, page S-76, is a sample partograph for prolonged active phase of labour. Note that the partograph was not adequately filled out and that this example demonstrates inappropriate management of prolonged labour. The diagnosis of prolonged labour was evident at 14:00 and labour should have been augmented with oxytocin at that time.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman was admitted in active labour at 10:00 with:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head 5/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 4 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; inadequate uterine contractions (two in 10 minutes, each lasting

less than 20 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 14:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head still 5/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 4 cm and to the right of the alert line;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; membranes have ruptured spontaneously and amniotic fluid is

clear;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; inadequate uterine contractions (one in 10 minutes, lasting less

### Diagnosis — phần 4

than 20 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 18:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head still 5/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 6 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; contractions still inadequate (two in 10 minutes, each lasting less

than 20 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 21:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal heart rate 80 per minute;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; amniotic fluid stained with meconium;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; no further progress in labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A caesarean is performed at 21:20 due to fetal distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-76; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-8.; Partograph showing prolonged active phase of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour; S-77

### Diagnosis — phần 5

Figure S-9, page S-78, is a sample partograph showing arrest of dilatation and descent in the active phase of labour. Fetal distress and third degree moulding, together with arrest of dilatation and descent in the active phase of labour in the presence of adequate uterine contractions, indicate obstructed labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman was admitted in active labour at 10:00 with:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head 3/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 4 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting 20–40 seconds;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clear amniotic fluid draining;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; first degree moulding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 14:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head still 3/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 6 cm and to the right of the alert line;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; slight improvement in contractions (three in 10 minutes, each

lasting 45 seconds);

### Diagnosis — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; second degree moulding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 17:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head still 3/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix still dilated 6 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; third degree moulding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal heart rate 92 per minute;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; amniotic fluid stained with meconium.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A caesarean is performed at 17:30 due to fetal distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-78; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE S-9.; Partograph showing obstructed labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour; S-79

Figure S-10, page S-80, is a sample partograph for poor progress of labour due to inadequate uterine contractions corrected with oxytocin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman was admitted in active labour at 10:00 with:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head 5/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 4 cm;

### Diagnosis — phần 7

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; two contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting less than 20 seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 12:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head still 5/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix still dilated 4 cm and to the right of the alert line;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; no improvement in contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 14:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; poor progress of labour due to inefficient uterine contractions

diagnosed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; augmented labour with oxytocin 10 units in 1 L IV fluids at

15 drops per minute;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; escalated oxytocin until a good pattern of contractions was

established.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At 19:00:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head 1/5 palpable;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix dilated 10 cm;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; four contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting 45 seconds.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Spontaneous vaginal birth occurred at 20:10.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-80; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

### Diagnosis — phần 8

FIGURE S-10. Partograph showing inadequate uterine contractions corrected with oxytocin

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour; S-81

## False Labour

### False Labour — phần 1

Examine for urinary tract or other infection (Table S-17, page S-114) or ruptured membranes (page S-159) and treat accordingly. If none of these are present, discharge the woman and encourage her to return if signs of labour recur or she has danger signs.

## Prolonged Latent Phase

### Prolonged Latent Phase — phần 1

Misdiagnosing false labour or prolonged latent phase leads to unnecessary induction or augmentation, which may fail. This can lead to unnecessary caesarean and amnionitis.

The diagnosis of prolonged latent phase is made retrospectively. When contractions cease, the woman is said to have had false labour. When contractions become regular and dilatation progresses beyond 4 cm, the woman is said to have been in the latent phase.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman has been in the latent phase for more than eight hours and

there is little sign of progress, reassess the situation by assessing the cervix:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there has been no change in cervical effacement or dilatation

and there is no fetal distress, review the diagnosis. The woman might not be in labour. First explore less invasive interventions that could lead to vaginal birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there has been a change in cervical effacement or dilatation,

### Prolonged Latent Phase — phần 2

augment labour using oxytocin (page P-23). Note: Do not use oral misoprostol for labour augmentation.

Do not perform amniotomy as a sole intervention for treatment of confirmed delay in labour, especially in settings with high HIV prevalence.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue to monitor the woman and fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassess cervical dilatation every four hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman has not entered the active phase after eight hours of

oxytocin infusion, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal discharge):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Augment labour immediately with oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-82; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give a combination of antibiotics until the baby is born:

ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

### Prolonged Latent Phase — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman gives birth vaginally, continue antibiotics until

resolution of clinical signs and symptoms (e.g. fever, uterine tenderness, purulent lochia) for 24–48 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the woman has a caesarean, continue antibiotics until

resolution of clinical signs and symptoms (e.g. fever, uterine tenderness, purulent lochia) for 24–48 hours.

Once labour augmentation has begun, the woman should never be left alone.

## Prolonged Active Phase

### Prolonged Active Phase — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess uterine contractions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If contractions are inefficient (less than three contractions in 10

minutes, each lasting less than 40 seconds), suspect inadequate uterine activity (page S-83).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If contractions are efficient (three or more contractions in

10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds), suspect cephalopelvic disproportion (page S-82), obstruction (page S-83), malposition or malpresentation (page S-85).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of cephalopelvic disproportion or obstruction

and the membranes are intact, augment labour using oxytocin (page P-23). Do not rupture the membranes, especially in settings with high HIV prevalence.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide general methods of labour support, which may improve

contractions and accelerate progress (page C-86).

## Cephalopelvic Disproportion

### Cephalopelvic Disproportion — phần 1

Cephalopelvic disproportion occurs because the fetus is too large or the maternal pelvis is too small. If labour persists with cephalopelvic disproportion, it may become arrested or obstructed. The best test to determine if a pelvis is adequate is a trial of labour. Clinical pelvimetry is of limited value.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour; S-83

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If cephalopelvic disproportion is confirmed (Table S-15, page

S-74):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetus is alive, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetus is dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Perform a craniotomy (page P-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the operator is not proficient in craniotomy, perform a

caesarean (page P-53).

## Obstruction

### Obstruction — phần 1

Note: Rupture of an unscarred uterus is usually caused by obstructed labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is alive, the cervix is fully dilated and the fetal head is

not more than 1/5 above the symphysis pubis, or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is at 0 station or below, assist the birth of the baby using an obstetric vacuum (page P-33).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is alive and the head is between 1/5 and 3/5 above the

symphysis pubis or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is at −2 station, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is alive but the cervix is not fully dilated or if the fetal

head is too high for vacuum-assisted birth (the head is more than 3/5 above the symphysis pubis or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is above −2 station), perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform a craniotomy (page P-65).

### Obstruction — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the operator is not proficient in craniotomy, perform a

caesarean (page P-53).

## Inadequate Uterine Activity

### Inadequate Uterine Activity — phần 1

If contractions are inefficient (two contractions or less in 10 minutes, each lasting less than 40 seconds) and cephalopelvic disproportion and obstruction have been excluded, the most probable cause of prolonged labour is inadequate uterine activity. Augment labour using oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-84; Unsatisfactory Progress of Labour

Inefficient contractions are less common in a multigravida than in a primigravida. Hence, every effort should be made to rule out disproportion in a multigravida before augmenting with oxytocin.

## Prolonged Expulsive Phase

### Prolonged Expulsive Phase — phần 1

Maternal expulsive efforts increase fetal risk by reducing the delivery of oxygen to the placenta. Encourage spontaneous maternal “pushing” during contractions; avoid telling the woman to take a deep breath and hold it (closed-glottis pushing) while she makes prolonged pushing efforts. Assist the woman in assuming a position of her choice, including upright positions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If malpresentation and obvious obstruction have been excluded,

augment labour with oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is no descent after augmentation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assist the birth of the baby using an obstetric vacuum (page P-33)

or forceps (page P-41) if the fetal head is not more than 1/5 above the symphysis pubis or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is at 0 station.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform a caesarean (page P-53) if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the fetal head is between 1/5 and 3/5 above the symphysis

### Prolonged Expulsive Phase — phần 2

pubis or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is between 0 station and −2 station; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; the fetal head is more than 3/5 above the symphysis pubis or

the leading bony edge of the fetal head is above −2 station.

## Malpositions And Malpresentations S-85

### Malpositions And Malpresentations S-85 — phần 1

Malpositions are abnormal positions of the vertex of the fetal head (with the occiput as the reference point) relative to the maternal pelvis. Malpresentations are all presentations of the fetus other than vertex.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The fetus is in an abnormal position or presentation that may result in

prolonged or obstructed labour.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the general condition of the woman,

including vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, volume of blood loss, whether bleeding is accompanied by pain, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the fetal condition:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Listen to the fetal heart rate immediately after a contraction:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Count the fetal heart rate for a full minute at least once every

30 minutes during the active phase and every five minutes during the second stage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or

more than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the membranes have ruptured, note the colour of the draining

amniotic fluid:

### General Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; The presence of thick meconium indicates the need for close

monitoring and possible intervention for management of fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; The absence of fluid draining after rupture of the membranes

is an indication of reduced volume of amniotic fluid, which may be associated with fetal distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide encouragement and supportive care (page C-86).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-86; Malpositions and malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review progress of labour using a partograph (page C-91).

Note: Observe the woman closely. Malpresentations increase the risk for uterine rupture because of the potential for obstructed labour.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The most common presentation is the vertex of the fetal head. If the

vertex is not the presenting part, see Table S-16, page S-88.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the vertex is the presenting part, use landmarks of the fetal skull to

determine the position of the fetal head (Fig. S-11).

FIGURE S-11. Landmarks of the fetal skull

## Determine The Position Of The Fetal Head

### Determine The Position Of The Fetal Head — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The fetal head normally engages in the maternal pelvis in an occiput

transverse position, with the fetal occiput transverse in the maternal pelvis (Fig. S-12).

FIGURE S-12. Occiput transverse positions

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-87

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With descent, the fetal head rotates so that the fetal occiput is anterior

in the maternal pelvis (Fig. S-13). Failure of an occiput transverse position to rotate to an occiput anterior position should be managed as an occiput posterior position (page S-88).

FIGURE S-13. Occiput anterior positions

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; An additional feature of a normal presentation is a well-flexed vertex

(Fig. S-14), with the fetal occiput lower in the vagina than the sinciput.

FIGURE S-14. Well-flexed vertex

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-88; Malpositions and malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetal head is well-flexed with occiput anterior or occiput

### Determine The Position Of The Fetal Head — phần 2

transverse (in early labour), proceed with birth of the baby (page C-98).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetal head is not occiput anterior, identify and manage the

malposition (Table S-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetal head is not the presenting part or the fetal head is not

well-flexed, identify and manage the malpresentation (Table S-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-16.; Diagnosis of malpositions

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Figure

- Dòng dữ liệu: Occiput posterior position occurs when the; FIGURE S-15

fetal occiput is posterior in relation to the maternal pelvis (Fig. S-15 and Fig. S-16).

On abdominal examination, the lower part of the abdomen is flattened, fetal limbs are palpable anteriorly and the fetal heart may be heard in the flank.

On vaginal examination, the posterior

- Dòng dữ liệu: fontanelle is towards the sacrum and the; FIGURE S-16

anterior fontanelle may be easily felt if the head is deflexed.

### Determine The Position Of The Fetal Head — phần 3

For management, see page S-91.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Occiput transverse position occurs when; FIGURE S-17

the fetal occiput is transverse to the maternal pelvis (Fig. S-17). If an occiput transverse position persists into the later part of the first stage of labour, it should be managed as an occiput posterior position (page S-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-89

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Figure

- Dòng dữ liệu: Brow presentation is caused by partial; FIGURE S-18

extension of the fetal head so that the occiput is higher than the sinciput (Fig. S-18).

On abdominal examination, more than half the fetal head is above the symphysis pubis and the occiput is palpable at a higher level than the sinciput.

On vaginal examination, the anterior fontanelle and the orbits are felt.

For management, see page S-92.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Face presentation is caused by; FIGURE S-19

### Determine The Position Of The Fetal Head — phần 4

hyperextension of the fetal head so that neither the occiput nor the sinciput is palpable on vaginal examination (Fig. S-19 and Fig. S-20).

On abdominal examination, a groove may be felt between the occiput and the back.

On vaginal examination, the face is palpated, the examiner’s finger enters the mouth easily and the bony jaws are felt.

## Figure S-20

### Figure S-20 — phần 1

For management, see page S-92.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-90; Malpositions and malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Figure

- Dòng dữ liệu: Compound presentation occurs when; FIGURE S-21

an arm prolapses alongside the presenting part. Both the prolapsed arm and the fetal head present in the pelvis simultaneously (Fig. S-21).

For management, see page S-94.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breech presentation occurs when the; FIGURE S-22

buttocks and/or the feet are the presenting parts.

On abdominal examination, the head is felt in the upper abdomen and the breech in the pelvic brim. Auscultation locates the fetal heart higher than expected with a vertex presentation.

On vaginal examination during labour, the buttocks and/or feet are felt;

- Dòng dữ liệu: thick, dark meconium is normal.; FIGURE S-23

For management, see page S-95.

Complete (flexed) breech presentation occurs when both legs are flexed at the hips and knees (Fig. S-22).

### Figure S-20 — phần 2

Frank (extended) breech presentation occurs when both legs are flexed at the

- Dòng dữ liệu: hips and extended at the knees; FIGURE S-24

(Fig. S-23).

Footling breech presentation occurs when a leg is extended at the hip and the knee (Fig. S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-91

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Figure

- Dòng dữ liệu: Transverse lie and shoulder; FIGURE S-25

presentation occur when the long axis of the fetus is transverse (Fig. S-25). The shoulder is typically the presenting part.

On abdominal examination, neither the head nor the buttocks can be felt at the symphysis pubis and the head is usually felt in the flank.

On vaginal examination, a shoulder may be felt, but not always. An arm may prolapse, and the elbow, arm or hand may be felt in the vagina.

For management, see page S-97.

## Occiput Posterior Positions

### Occiput Posterior Positions — phần 1

Spontaneous rotation to the anterior position occurs in 90% of cases. Arrested labour may occur when the head does not rotate and/or descend. Birth of the baby may be complicated by perineal tears or extension of an episiotomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of obstruction but the fetal heart rate is normal,

allow the woman to walk around or change position to encourage spontaneous rotation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of obstruction and the fetal heart rate is abnormal

(less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute) at any stage, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not fully dilated and there are no signs of

obstruction, augment labour with oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is fully dilated but there is no descent in the expulsive

phase of the second stage of labour, assess for signs of obstruction (Table S-15, page S-74):

### Occiput Posterior Positions — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of obstruction, augment labour with

oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-92; Malpositions and malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is fully dilated:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal head is no more than 2/5 above the symphysis pubis,

or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is at 0 station, assist birth of the baby using an obstetric vacuum (page P-33) or forceps (page P-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Otherwise, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

## Brow Presentation

### Brow Presentation — phần 1

In brow presentation, engagement is usually impossible and arrested labour is common. Spontaneous conversion to either vertex presentation or face presentation can rarely occur, particularly when the fetus is small or when there is fetal death with maceration. It is unusual for spontaneous conversion to occur with an average-sized live fetus once the membranes have ruptured.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is alive, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is not fully dilated, perform a caesarean (page

P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is fully dilated:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Perform a craniotomy (page P-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the operator is not proficient in craniotomy, perform a

caesarean (page P-53).

Do not use an obstetric vacuum or outlet forceps with brow presentation.

## Face Presentation

### Face Presentation — phần 1

The chin serves as the reference point in describing the position of the head. It is necessary to distinguish chin-anterior positions, in which the chin is anterior in relation to the maternal pelvis (Fig. S-26 A, page S-93), from chin-posterior positions (Fig. S-26 B, page S-93).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-93

FIGURE S-26. Face presentation

Prolonged labour is common. Descent and birth of the head by flexion may occur in the chin-anterior position. In the chin-posterior position, however, the fully extended head is blocked by the sacrum. This prevents descent and labour is arrested.

## Chin-Anterior Position

### Chin-Anterior Position — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is fully dilated:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Allow normal childbirth to proceed (page C-98).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is slow progress and no sign of obstruction (Table S-15,

page S-74), augment labour with oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If descent is unsatisfactory, assist the birth of the baby using

forceps (page P-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not fully dilated and there are no signs of

obstruction, augment labour using oxytocin (page P-23). Review progress as with vertex presentation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-94; Malpositions and malpresentations

## Chin-Posterior Position

### Chin-Posterior Position — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is fully dilated, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is not fully dilated, monitor descent, rotation and

progress. If there are signs of obstruction, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform a craniotomy (page P-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the operator is not proficient in craniotomy, perform a

caesarean (page P-53).

Do not use an obstetric vacuum for face presentation.

## Compound Presentation

### Compound Presentation — phần 1

Spontaneous vaginal birth can occur only when the fetus is very small or dead and macerated. Arrested labour occurs in the expulsive phase of the second stage of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Replacement of the prolapsed arm is sometimes possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assist the woman in assuming the knee-chest position (Fig. S-27).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Push the arm above the pelvic brim and hold it there until a

contraction pushes the head into the pelvis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Proceed with management for normal childbirth (page C-98).

FIGURE S-27. Knee-chest position

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the procedure fails or if the cord prolapses, perform a caesarean

(page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-95

## Breech Presentation

### Breech Presentation — phần 1

Prolonged labour with breech presentation is an indication for an urgent caesarean. Failure of labour to progress must be considered a sign of possible cephalopelvic disproportion (Table S-15, page S-74).

The frequency of breech presentation is high in preterm labour.

## Early Labour

### Early Labour — phần 1

Ideally, every breech birth should take place in a hospital with the ability to perform an emergency caesarean.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Attempt external cephalic version (page P-15) if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; breech presentation is present at or after 37 weeks (before

37 weeks, a successful version is more likely to spontaneously revert back to breech presentation);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; vaginal birth is possible;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; facilities for emergency caesarean are available;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; membranes are intact and amniotic fluid is adequate;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; there are no complications (e.g. fetal growth restriction, uterine

bleeding, previous caesarean birth, fetal abnormalities, twin pregnancy, hypertension, fetal death).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If external version is successful, proceed with normal childbirth

(page C-98).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If external version fails, proceed with vaginal breech birth (below) or

### Early Labour — phần 2

caesarean (page P-53).

## Vaginal Breech Birth

### Vaginal Breech Birth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A vaginal breech birth (page P-45) by a skilled health care provider is

safe and feasible under the following conditions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; complete or frank breech (Fig. P-15, page P-45);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; adequate clinical pelvimetry;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetus is not too large;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; no previous caesarean for cephalopelvic disproportion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head is flexed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-96; Malpositions and malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the woman regularly and record progress on a partograph

(page C-91).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the membranes rupture, examine the woman immediately to

exclude cord prolapse.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note: Do not rupture the membranes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cord prolapses and birth is not imminent, perform a caesarean

(page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than

### Vaginal Breech Birth — phần 2

180 beats per minute) or prolonged labour, perform a caesarean (page P-53). Note: Meconium is common with breech labour and is not a sign of fetal distress if the fetal heart rate is normal.

The woman should not push until the cervix is fully dilated. Full dilatation should be confirmed by vaginal exam.

## Caesarean Birth For Breech Presentation

### Caesarean Birth For Breech Presentation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A caesarean birth (page P-53) is safer than vaginal breech birth and

recommended in cases of:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; double footling breech;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a small or malformed pelvis;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a very large fetus;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a previous caesarean for cephalopelvic disproportion; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a hyperextended or deflexed head.

Note: Elective caesarean does not improve the outcome in preterm breech birth.

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

Fetal complications of breech presentation include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; cord prolapse;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; birth trauma as a result of extended arm or head, incomplete dilatation

of the cervix, or cephalopelvic disproportion;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Malpositions and malpresentations; S-97

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; asphyxia from cord prolapse, cord compression, placental detachment

or entrapped head;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; damage to abdominal organs; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; broken neck.

## Transverse Lie And Shoulder Presentation

### Transverse Lie And Shoulder Presentation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman is in early labour and the membranes are intact,

attempt external version (page P-15):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If external version is successful, proceed with normal childbirth

(page C-98).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If external version fails or is not advisable, perform a caesarean

(page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor for signs of cord prolapse. If the cord prolapses and birth is

not imminent, perform a caesarean (page P-53). Note: Ruptured uterus may occur if the woman is left unattended (page S-24).

In modern practice, persistent transverse lie in labour is managed by performing a caesarean whether the fetus is alive or dead.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-98; Malpositions and malpresentations

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The fetal head has been born but the shoulders are stuck and cannot be

delivered.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Be prepared for shoulder dystocia at all births, especially if a large

baby is anticipated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have several persons available to help.

Shoulder dystocia cannot be predicted.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The fetal head is born but remains tightly applied to the vulva.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The chin retracts and depresses the perineum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Traction on the head fails to deliver the shoulder, which is caught

behind the symphysis pubis.

## Management

### Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; SHOUT FOR HELP. Urgently mobilize all available personnel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make an adequate episiotomy (page P-85) to reduce soft tissue

obstruction and to allow space for manipulation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the woman on her back, ask her to flex both thighs, bringing her

knees as far up as possible towards her chest (Fig. S-28, page S-100). Ask two assistants to push her flexed knees firmly up onto her chest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves:

### Management — phần 2

- Apply firm, continuous traction downwards on the fetal head to move the shoulder that is anterior under the symphysis pubis. Note: Avoid excessive traction on the fetal head as this may result in brachial plexus injury. - Have an assistant simultaneously apply suprapubic pressure downwards to assist delivery of the shoulder. Note: Do not apply fundal pressure. This will further impact the shoulder and can result in uterine rupture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the shoulder has still not been born:

- Insert a hand into the vagina along the baby’s back.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-100; Shoulder dystocia

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply pressure to the shoulder that is anterior in the direction of

the baby’s sternum to rotate the shoulder and decrease the diameter of the shoulders. - If needed, apply pressure to the shoulder that is posterior in the direction of the sternum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the shoulder still is not born despite the above measures:

### Management — phần 3

- Insert a hand into the vagina. FIGURE S-28. Assistant pushing flexed knees firmly towards chest

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Grasp the humerus of the arm that is posterior and, keeping the

arm flexed at the elbow, sweep the arm across the chest. This will provide room for the shoulder that is anterior to move under the symphysis pubis (Fig. S-29).

FIGURE S-29. Grasping the humerus of the arm that is posterior and sweeping the arm across the chest

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If all of the above measures fail to deliver the shoulder, other

options include: - Fracture the clavicle to decrease the width of the shoulders and free the shoulder that is anterior. - Apply traction with a hook in the axilla to extract the arm that is posterior.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman in labour has an overdistended uterus or symphysis-fundal

height more than expected for the period of gestation.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prop up the woman.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm accuracy of calculated gestational age, if possible.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If only one fetus is felt on abdominal examination, consider wrong

dates, a single large fetus (page S-102) or an excess of amniotic fluid (page S-102).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If multiple fetal poles and parts are felt on abdominal examination,

suspect multiple pregnancy. Other signs of multiple pregnancy include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head small in relation to the uterus;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; uterus larger than expected for gestation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; more than one fetal heart heard with Doppler fetal stethoscope.

Note: An acoustic fetal stethoscope cannot be used to confirm the diagnosis, as one heart may be heard in different areas.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use ultrasound examination, if available, to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; identify the number, presentations and sizes of fetuses;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; assess the volume of amniotic fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If ultrasound service is not available, perform radiological

### Diagnosis — phần 2

examination (anteroposterior view) for number of fetuses and presentations.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-102; Labour with an overdistended uterus

## Single Large Fetus

### Single Large Fetus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Manage as for normal labour (page C-77).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Anticipate and prepare for prolonged and obstructed labour (page

S-83), shoulder dystocia (page S-99), and PPH (page S-29).

## Excess Amniotic Fluid

### Excess Amniotic Fluid — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow labour to progress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the condition of the woman and the fetus (page C-88).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor progress of labour (page C-89); use a partograph in the active

phase of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is uncomfortable because of uterine distension,

aspirate excess amniotic fluid:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Palpate for the location of the fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prepare the skin with an antiseptic (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Under aseptic conditions, insert a 20-gauge spinal needle through

the abdominal and uterine walls and withdraw the stylet.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Aspirate the fluid using a large syringe. Alternatively, attach an

infusion set to the needle and allow the fluid to slowly drain into a container.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When the woman is no longer uncomfortable because of

overdistension, replace the stylet and remove the needle.

### Excess Amniotic Fluid — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If rupture of membranes is indicated for other reasons, rupture the

membranes with an amniotic hook or a Kocher clamp (page P-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for cord prolapse when membranes rupture. If the cord

prolapses and birth is not imminent, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

## First Baby

### First Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and slowly infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fetuses by intermittent auscultation of the fetal heart rates. If

there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Labour with an overdistended uterus; S-103

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check presentation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For a vertex presentation, allow labour to progress as for a single

vertex presentation (page C-82), and:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; monitor the condition of the woman and the fetuses (page

C-88);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; monitor progress in labour (page C-89); use a partograph in

the active phase of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For a breech presentation, apply the same guidelines as for a

singleton breech presentation (page S-95), and:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; monitor the condition of the woman and the fetuses (page

C-88);

### First Baby — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; monitor progress in labour (page C-89); use a partograph in

the active phase of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For a transverse lie, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

Leave a clamp on the maternal end of the umbilical cord and do not attempt to deliver the placenta until the last baby is born.

## Second Or Additional Baby(S)

### Second Or Additional Baby(S) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Immediately after the first baby is born:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; palpate the abdomen to determine the lie of the unborn baby(s);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; correct to longitudinal lie by external version (page P-15);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; check fetal heart rate(s).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a vaginal examination to determine:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if the cord has prolapsed (page S-111);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; whether the membranes are intact or ruptured; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the presentation of the other baby(s).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-104; Labour with an overdistended uterus

Vertex Presentation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetal head is not engaged, manoeuvre the head into the pelvis

manually (hands on abdomen), if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the membranes are intact, rupture the membranes with an amniotic

hook or a Kocher clamp.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate between contractions.

### Second Or Additional Baby(S) — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If contractions are inadequate after birth of the first baby,

augment labour with oxytocin using rapid escalation (Table P-8, page P-26) to produce good contractions (three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If spontaneous vaginal birth does not occur within two hours of

good contractions, or if there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), perform a caesarean (page P-53).

Breech Presentation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is estimated to be no larger than the first baby, and if

the cervix has not contracted, consider assisted breech birth or breech extraction (page P-45) or vaginal breech birth (page S-95):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are inadequate or no contractions after birth of the

### Second Or Additional Baby(S) — phần 3

first baby, escalate oxytocin infusion at a rapid but controlled rate (Table P-8, page P-26) to produce good contractions (three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the membranes are intact and the breech has descended,

rupture the membranes with an amniotic hook or a Kocher clamp (page P-29).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the fetal heart rate between contractions. If there are fetal

heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), perform breech extraction (page P-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If vaginal birth is not possible, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

Transverse Lie

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the membranes are intact, attempt external version (page P-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If external version fails and the cervix is fully dilated and

membranes are still intact, attempt internal podalic version:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Labour with an overdistended uterus; S-105

### Second Or Additional Baby(S) — phần 4

Note: Do not attempt internal podalic version if the provider is untrained, if the membranes have ruptured and the amniotic fluid has drained, or if the uterus is scarred. Do not persist if the baby does not turn easily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wearing sterile gloves, insert a hand into the uterus and grasp the

baby’s foot.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently rotate the baby down.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Proceed with breech extraction (page P-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check fetal heart rate between contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If external version fails and internal podalic version is not

advisable or fails, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxytocin 10 units IM or give ergometrine 0.2 mg IM within one

minute after birth of the last baby and continue active management of the third stage to reduce postpartum blood loss (page C-102).

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maternal complications of multiple pregnancy include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; anaemia

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abortion

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; gestational hypertension and pre-eclampsia

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; excess amniotic fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; poor contractions during labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; retained placenta

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; postpartum haemorrhage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Placental/fetal complications include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; placenta praevia

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; abruptio placentae

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; placental insufficiency

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; preterm birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; low birth weight

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; malpresentations

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cord prolapse

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; congenital anomalies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-106; Labour with an overdistended uterus

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman in labour has a scarred uterus from a previous uterine

surgery.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If possible, identify the reason for the uterine scar. Caesarean and

other uterine surgeries (e.g. repair of a previous uterine rupture or excision of an ectopic pregnancy implanted in the cornua) leave a scar in the uterine wall. This scar can weaken the uterus, leading to uterine rupture during labour (Box S-8).

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-8.; Rupture of uterine scars

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Vertical scars from a previous caesarean can rupture before labour or during

the latent phase.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transverse scars typically rupture during active labour or during the expulsive

phase of second stage of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The rupture may extend only a short distance into the myometrium with little

### General Management — phần 2

pain or bleeding. The fetus and placenta may remain in the uterus and the fetus may survive for minutes or hours.

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

Studies have shown that in more than 50% of cases with low transverse caesarean scars, the baby can be born vaginally. The frequency of rupture of low transverse scars during a carefully monitored trial of labour is reported as less than 1%.

## Trial Of Labour

### Trial Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that conditions are favourable for trial of labour, including the

following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The previous surgery involved a low transverse caesarean incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-108; Labour with a scarred uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The fetus is in a normal vertex presentation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Emergency caesarean can be carried out immediately if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If these conditions are not met, or if the woman has a history of

two lower uterine segment caesarean scars or ruptured uterus, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the condition of the woman and the fetus (page C-88).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the progress of labour (page C-89); use a partograph in the

active phase of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If cervical dilatation crosses to the right of the alert line of the

### Trial Of Labour — phần 2

partograph, diagnose the cause of slow progress and take appropriate action (Table S-15, page S-74):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is slow progress in labour due to inefficient uterine

contractions (Table S-15, page S-74), augment labour using oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of cephalopelvic disproportion or obstruction

(Table S-15, page S-74), perform a caesarean immediately (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of impending uterine rupture (rapid maternal

pulse, persistent abdominal pain and suprapubic tenderness, fetal distress), perform a caesarean immediately (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If uterine rupture is suspected, perform a caesarean immediately

(page P-53) and repair the uterus (page P-113) or perform a hysterectomy (page P-121).

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Abnormal fetal heart rate (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Thick meconium-stained amniotic fluid

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prop up the woman or place her on her left side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Stop oxytocin if it is being administered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen 4–6 L by mask or nasal cannulae.

## Abnormal Fetal Heart Rate

### Abnormal Fetal Heart Rate — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-9.; Abnormal fetal heart rate

• A normal fetal heart rate may slow during a contraction but usually recovers to normal as soon as the uterus relaxes. • A very slow fetal heart rate in the absence of contractions or persisting after contractions is suggestive of fetal distress. • A rapid fetal heart rate may be a response to maternal fever, drugs causing rapid maternal heart rate (e.g. terbutaline or ritodrine), hypertension or amnionitis. In the absence of a rapid maternal heart rate, a rapid fetal heart rate

should be considered a sign of fetal distress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a maternal cause is identified (e.g. maternal fever, bleeding,

drugs), initiate appropriate management:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is bleeding with intermittent or constant pain, suspect

abruptio placentae (page S-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

### Abnormal Fetal Heart Rate — phần 2

discharge), give antibiotics as for amnionitis (page S-163).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is heard but is depressed and the mother

has had sedatives, wait for the effect of the drugs to wear off and then recheck.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-110; Fetal distress in labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a maternal cause is not identified and the fetal heart rate remains

abnormal throughout at least three contractions, perform a vaginal examination (page C-90) to check for explanatory signs of distress:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cord is below the presenting part or in the vagina,

manage as prolapsed cord (page S-111).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If fetal heart rate abnormalities persist or there are additional signs

of distress (thick meconium-stained fluid), plan for the woman to give birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is fully dilated and the fetal head is not more than

### Abnormal Fetal Heart Rate — phần 3

1/5 above the symphysis pubis, or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is at 0 station, assist the birth of the baby using an obstetric vacuum (page P-33) or forceps (page P-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is not fully dilated, or the fetal head is more than

1/5 above the symphysis pubis, or the leading bony edge of the fetal head is above 0 station, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

## Meconium

### Meconium — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Meconium staining of amniotic fluid is seen frequently as the fetus

matures, and by itself it is not an indicator of fetal distress. A slight degree of meconium without fetal heart rate abnormalities is a warning of the need for vigilance.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Thick meconium suggests passage of meconium in reduced amniotic

fluid and may indicate the need for expedited childbirth and management of the neonatal upper airway at birth to prevent meconium aspiration (page S-167).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In breech presentation, meconium is passed in labour because of

compression of the fetal abdomen. This is not a sign of distress unless it occurs in early labour.

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The umbilical cord lies in the birth canal below the fetal presenting

part.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The umbilical cord is visible at the vagina following rupture of the

membranes.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen at 4–6 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae.

## Pulsating Cord

### Pulsating Cord — phần 1

If the cord is pulsating, the fetus is alive.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Diagnose the stage of labour by an immediate vaginal examination

(Table C-8, page C-78).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is in the first stage of labour, perform the following in

all cases:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wearing sterile gloves, insert a hand into the vagina. Push the

presenting part up to decrease pressure on the cord and dislodge the presenting part from the pelvis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the other hand on the abdomen in the suprapubic region to

keep the presenting part out of the pelvis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the presenting part is firmly held above the pelvic brim,

remove the other hand from the vagina. Keep the hand on the abdomen until a caesarean can be performed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If available, give tocolytics (page S-146) to reduce contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform immediate caesarean (page P-53).

### Pulsating Cord — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is in the second stage of labour:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Expedite birth with obstetric vacuum (page P-33) or forceps

(page P-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-112; Prolapsed cord

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For breech presentation, perform breech extraction (page P-45)

and apply Piper or long forceps to the after-coming head (page P-49).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prepare for resuscitation of the newborn (page S-167).

## Cord Not Pulsating

### Cord Not Pulsating — phần 1

If the cord is not pulsating, the fetus is dead. Proceed with birth of the baby in the manner that is safest for the woman.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman has a fever (temperature of 38°C or more) during pregnancy

or labour.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even

if signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further, because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate and ask about fetal movements, depending

on gestational age:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

### Immediate Management — phần 2

listen or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal movements are not felt or the fetal heart cannot be

heard, suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-114; Fever during pregnancy and labour
