## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage rest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage increased fluid intake by mouth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a fan or tepid sponge, and if necessary, open a window to help

decrease temperature.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider paracetamol 500–1000 mg every six to eight hours

(maximum of 4000 mg in 24 hours) to help decrease temperature.

## Tests

### Tests — phần 1

Urine dipstick, microscopy and culture tests can be used to evaluate for a urinary tract infection, but they will not differentiate between cystitis and acute pyelonephritis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A dipstick leukocyte esterase test can be used to detect white blood

cells, protein or blood; a nitrate reductase test can be used to detect nitrites.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Microscopy of a urine specimen may show white cells in clumps,

bacteria and, sometimes, red cells.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-116; Fever during pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Urine culture and sensitivity tests should be done, if available, to

identify the organism and its antibiotic sensitivity. Note: Urine examination requires a clean-catch midstream specimen to minimize the possibility of contamination. Contamination may be indicated by presence of many epithelial cells noted on microscopy.

## Cystitis

### Cystitis — phần 1

Cystitis is infection of the bladder (lower urinary tract).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat with an antibiotic (page C-49). Antibiotic options include the

following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; amoxicillin 500 mg by mouth every eight hours for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; nitrofurantoin 100 mg by mouth every eight hours for three days.

Note: Avoid nitrofurantoin at term as it can cause neonatal haemolysis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If treatment fails or if infection recurs two or more times during

pregnancy, check urine culture and sensitivity, if available, and treat with an antibiotic appropriate for the organism.

## Acute Pyelonephritis

### Acute Pyelonephritis — phần 1

Acute pyelonephritis is an infection of the upper urinary tract, mainly of the renal pelvis, which may also involve the renal parenchyma. Acute pyelonephritis can cause significant illness in pregnant women and should be promptly investigated and treated in every pregnant woman with fever, urinary symptoms and flank pain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is present or suspected, initiate immediate treatment (page

S-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids at 150 mL per hour (page

C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check urine dipstick and urine culture if possible and begin empiric

antibiotic treatment promptly (pending results of urine culture, if available).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat with an IV antibiotic until the woman is fever-free for 48 hours

(page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

### Acute Pyelonephritis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever during pregnancy and labour; S-117

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the woman is fever-free for 48 hours, is drinking well and is

not vomiting, switch from an IV to an oral antibiotic, for a total of 14 days treatment. Oral antibiotic options include, among others:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; amoxicillin 1 g by mouth every eight hours to complete 14 days

of treatment. Note: Clinical response is expected within 48 hours. If there is no clinical response in 48 hours, re-evaluate urine culture results (if available) and antibiotic coverage. Also re-evaluate diagnosis to be sure that there is not another source of infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure adequate hydration by mouth or IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give paracetamol 500–1000 mg by mouth three to four times daily as

### Acute Pyelonephritis — phần 3

needed for pain and to lower temperature (4000 mg maximum in 24 hours). Note: If there are palpable contractions and blood-stained mucus discharge, suspect preterm labour (page S-144).

## Malaria

### Malaria — phần 1

In malaria-endemic areas in Africa, for women accessing care for malaria, either uncomplicated or severe:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat and then follow up with intermittent preventive treatment in

pregnancy (IPTp):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide IPTp with sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine (three tablets, each

tablet containing 500 mg/25 mg) to all pregnant women as part of routine antenatal care, ideally as directly observed therapy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Start treatment early in the second trimester (from 13 weeks

gestational age).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give IPTp at each antenatal care visit, as long as visits are at least

one month apart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that the woman receives at least three doses during

pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Promote the use of insecticide-treated nets.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Promote the use of insecticide-treated nets.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-118; Fever during pregnancy and labour

### Malaria — phần 2

Note: • Folic acid at a daily dose of 5 mg or more should not be given together with sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine as this counteracts the efficacy of the antimalarial. However, folic acid doses of 0.4 mg daily during pregnancy, as recommended by WHO, can be safely administered. • Women infected with HIV and taking co-trimoxazole prophylaxis should not be given IPTp due to a higher risk of adverse events.

Source: WHO, WHO Policy Brief for the Implementation of Intermittent Preventive Treatment of Malaria in Pregnancy Using Sulfadoxine-Pyrimethamine (IPTp-SP), April 2013.

## Uncomplicated Malaria

### Uncomplicated Malaria — phần 1

There are five important malaria parasites: Plasmodium (P.) falciparum, P. vivax, P. ovale, P. malariae and P. knowlesi. Symptomatic falciparum malaria in pregnant women may cause severe disease and death if it is not recognized and treated early. When malaria presents as an acute illness with fever, it cannot be reliably distinguished from many other causes of fever on clinical grounds. Malaria should be considered the most likely diagnosis in a pregnant woman with fever who has been exposed to

malaria. In the presence of fever in high transmission areas, a rapid test should be performed to rule out malaria comorbidity

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women without pre-existing immunity to malaria (living in non-

malarial areas) are susceptible to the more severe complications of malaria (page S-121).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women with acquired immunity to malaria are at high risk for

### Uncomplicated Malaria — phần 2

developing severe anaemia and giving birth to low birth weight babies. Note: If the malaria species is not known with certainty, treat as uncomplicated P. falciparum malaria.

## Tests

### Tests — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If facilities for testing are not available, begin therapy with

antimalarial drugs based on clinical suspicion (e.g. headache, fever, joint pain).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever during pregnancy and labour; S-119

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In areas of high transmission, always treat for malaria if malaria

cannot be ruled out, even though there might be another cause of fever.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where available, the following tests will confirm the diagnosis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; microscopy of a thick and thin blood film:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; thick blood film is more sensitive at detecting parasites

(absence of parasites does not rule out malaria);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; thin blood film helps to identify the parasite species; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; rapid antigen detection tests.

If suspicion of malaria remains, repeat the test in six hours.

## Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria

### Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria — phần 1

Chloroquine-resistant falciparum malaria is widespread. Resistance to other medications (e.g. quinine, sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine, mefloquine) also occurs. It is therefore important to follow national treatment guidelines. The antimalarials considered safe in the first trimester of pregnancy are quinine, chloroquine, clindamycin, mefloquine and proguanil. Medications contraindicated in pregnancy include primaquine, tetracycline, doxycycline and halofantrine.

First Trimester

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give quinine salt (dihydrochloride or sulfate) 10 mg/kg body weight

by mouth three times daily PLUS clindamycin 300 mg every six hours for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If clindamycin is not available, treat with quinine monotherapy:

quinine salt (dihydrochloride or sulfate) 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth three times daily for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; An artemisinin-based combination therapy (ACT) can be used if

### Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria — phần 2

quinine is not available, or if quinine plus clindamycin fails, or if adherence to seven-day treatment with quinine cannot be guaranteed. Second and Third Trimesters

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat orally based on national policy with any of the ACTs (assuming

a body weight of 50 kg or more):

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-120; Fever during pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; artemether (80 mg) PLUS lumefantrine (480 mg) twice daily for

three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; artesunate (200 mg) PLUS amodiaquine (540 mg) once daily for

three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; artesunate (200 mg) PLUS mefloquine (440 mg) once daily for

three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; dihydroartemisinin (160mg) PLUS piperaquine (1280 mg) once

daily for three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; artesunate (200 mg) once daily for three days PLUS

### Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria — phần 3

sulfadoxine/pyrimethamine (1500/75 mg) single dose only on day one. Note: Quinine is associated with an increased risk of hypoglycaemia in late pregnancy. It should be used, in combination with clindamycin, only if effective alternatives are not available.

## P. Vivax, Ovale, Malariae, Knowlesi Malaria

### P. Vivax, Ovale, Malariae, Knowlesi Malaria — phần 1

First Trimester Areas with Chloroquine-Sensitive P. Vivax Parasites Chloroquine is the treatment of choice in areas with chloroquine-sensitive vivax malaria.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give chloroquine 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily for two

days followed by 5 mg/kg body weight by mouth on day three. Areas with Chloroquine-Resistant P. Vivax Parasites Chloroquine-resistant P. vivax has been reported in several countries. Before considering second-line drugs for treatment failure with chloroquine, clinicians should exclude poor patient compliance and a new infection with P. falciparum. If diagnostic testing is not available, treat as for falciparum malaria. The treatment option for confirmed chloroquine-

resistant vivax malaria is quinine salt (dihydrochloride or sulfate) 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth three times a day for seven days. Note: The dose of quinine is the same for all species of malaria.

### P. Vivax, Ovale, Malariae, Knowlesi Malaria — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever during pregnancy and labour; S-121

Second and Third Trimesters Areas with Chloroquine-Sensitive P. Vivax Parasites ACT and chloroquine alone are the two treatment options in areas with chloroquine-sensitive vivax malaria (see dosage under First Trimester and Second and Third Trimesters in the Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria, page S-119). Areas with Chloroquine-Resistant P. Vivax Parasites Treat with ACT (see Second and Third Trimester in the Acute, Uncomplicated P. Falciparum Malaria, page S-119).

## Anti-Relapse Treatment

### Anti-Relapse Treatment — phần 1

Primaquine is contraindicated in pregnant women and mothers who are breastfeeding an infant less than six months of age. To prevent relapse in P. vivax or P. ovale malaria, consider weekly chemoprophylaxis with chloroquine until childbirth and breastfeeding are completed. Then, on the basis of G6PD (glucose-6-phosphate dehydrogenase) status, treat with primaquine to prevent future relapse.

## Treatment Of Liver Stages Of Vivax And Ovale Malaria

### Treatment Of Liver Stages Of Vivax And Ovale Malaria — phần 1

Vivax and ovale malaria may remain dormant in the liver. From time to time, these dormant stages are released into the blood, causing a new, symptomatic vivax or ovale infection. A 14-day course of primaquine (0.25–0.5 mg/kg body weight daily) should be used to clear the liver stages, but primaquine should not be given to pregnant women or women breastfeeding an infant less than six months of age.

## Severe Malaria

### Severe Malaria — phần 1

Women in the second and third trimesters of pregnancy are more likely to have severe malaria than other adults. Severe malaria in pregnancy may be misdiagnosed as eclampsia. If a pregnant woman living in a malarial area has fever, headaches or convulsions, and malaria cannot be excluded, treat the woman for both malaria and eclampsia. Pregnant women with severe malaria are particularly prone to hypoglycaemia, pulmonary oedema, anaemia and coma. Fetal death and premature labour are common.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-122; Fever during pregnancy and labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Severe malaria may also present immediately after giving birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women with severe malaria have a higher risk of a bacterial infection;

therefore, co-morbid bacterial infections should be ruled out or managed appropriately.

## Antimalarial Drugs

### Antimalarial Drugs — phần 1

Parenteral antimalarial drugs should be given to pregnant women with severe malaria in full doses without delay. Mortality from untreated severe malaria (particularly cerebral malaria) approaches 100%. With prompt, effective antimalarial treatment and supportive care, the rate falls to 10–20% overall.

Parenteral Artesunate

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Parenteral artesunate is the treatment of choice for severe malaria in

all trimesters:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Begin treatment with IV or IM route for at least 24 hours and until

the woman can tolerate oral medications.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Then give a complete oral treatment with ACT for three days.

Loading Dose Give artesunate 2.4 mg/kg body weight IV every 12 hours for at least 24 hours, until the woman can tolerate oral medications.

Maintenance Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give artesunate 1.2 mg/kg body weight IV as a single bolus once daily

beginning on the second day of treatment.

### Antimalarial Drugs — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue the maintenance dosing schedule until the woman is

conscious and able to swallow; then give artesunate 2 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily to complete seven days of treatment. If artesunate is not available, give intramuscular artemether:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever during pregnancy and labour; S-123

## Intramuscular Artemether

### Intramuscular Artemether — phần 1

Loading Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give artemether 3.2 mg/kg body weight IM as a single dose on the

first day of treatment Maintenance Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give artemether 1.6 mg/kg body weight IM once daily beginning on

the second day of treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue the maintenance dosing schedule until the woman is

conscious and able to tolerate oral medications; then give a complete dose of ACT. If artemether is unavailable, parenteral quinine should be started immediately and continued until artemether is obtained.

## Quinine Dihydrochloride

### Quinine Dihydrochloride — phần 1

Loading Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse quinine dihydrochloride 20 mg/kg body weight in IV fluids (5%

dextrose) over four hours:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Never give an IV bolus injection of quinine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If it is definitely known that the woman has taken an adequate

dose of quinine (1.2 g) within the preceding 12 hours, do not give the loading dose. Proceed with the maintenance dose (see below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the history of treatment is not known or is unclear, give the

loading dose of quinine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use 100–500 mL 5% dextrose, depending on the woman’s fluid

balance state.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wait four hours (eight hours from the start of the first dose) before

giving the maintenance dose.

Maintenance Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse quinine dihydrochloride 10 mg/kg body weight over four hours.

### Quinine Dihydrochloride — phần 2

Repeat every eight hours from the start of the last dose (i.e. quinine infusion for four hours, no quinine for four hours, quinine infusion for four hours, etc.).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-124; Fever during pregnancy and labour

Note: Monitor blood glucose levels for hypoglycaemia every hour while the woman is receiving quinine IV (page S-55).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue the maintenance dosing schedule until the woman is

conscious and able to tolerate oral medication. Then give:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; quinine dihydrochloride or quinine sulfate 10 mg/kg body weight

by mouth every eight hours to complete seven days of treatment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR ACT (page S-119).

### Quinine Dihydrochloride — phần 3

Women with severe malaria require intensive nursing care, preferably in an intensive care unit. Clinical observations should be made as frequently as possible and should include monitoring of vital signs, coma score, urine output and fetal well-being. Blood glucose should be monitored every four hours, if possible, particularly in unconscious patients.

Note: The antipyretic of choice, if body temperature is higher than 38°C, is paracetamol. Aspirin or ibuprofen should not be given because of the risks of gastrointestinal bleeding and renal impairment.

## Convulsions

### Convulsions — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If convulsions occur, maintain airways, place patient on her side and

give diazepam 10 mg IV slowly over two minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If eclampsia is diagnosed in addition to malaria, prevent subsequent

convulsions with magnesium sulfate (Box S-4, page S-59).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If eclampsia is excluded, prevent subsequent convulsions with

phenytoin (see below).

## Phenytoin

### Phenytoin — phần 1

Loading Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse phenytoin 1 g (approximately 18 mg/kg body weight) in

50–100 mL normal saline over 30 minutes (final concentration not to exceed 10 mg per mL):

Note: Only normal saline can be used to infuse phenytoin. All other IV fluids will cause crystallization of phenytoin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Flush IV line with normal saline before and after infusing

phenytoin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever during pregnancy and labour; S-125

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Do not infuse phenytoin at a rate exceeding 50 mg per minute due

to the risks of irregular heartbeat, hypotension and respiratory depression.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Complete administration within one hour of preparation.

Maintenance Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give phenytoin 100 mg IV slowly over two minutes or by mouth

every eight hours beginning at least 12 hours after the loading dose.

## Fluid Balance

### Fluid Balance — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain a strict fluid balance chart and monitor the amount of fluids

administered and urine output to ensure that there is no fluid overload. Assess clinical status regularly. Note: Women with severe malaria are prone to fluid overload.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If pulmonary oedema develops:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prop up the woman;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; give oxygen at 4 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; give furosemide 40 mg IV as a single dose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If urine output is poor (less than 30 mL per hour):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; measure serum creatinine; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; rehydrate with IV fluids (normal saline, Ringer’s lactate).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If urine output does not improve, give furosemide 40 mg IV as a

single dose and continue to monitor urine output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If urine output is still poor (less than 30 mL per hour over four

### Fluid Balance — phần 2

hours) and the serum creatinine is more than 2.9 mg/dL, refer the woman to a tertiary care centre, if possible, for management of renal failure.

## Hypoglycaemia

### Hypoglycaemia — phần 1

Hypoglycaemia in severe malaria is common and can occur at any time during the illness, especially after initiation of quinine therapy. There sometimes are no symptoms.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor blood glucose levels using a stick test every four hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-126; Fever during pregnancy and labour

Note: If the woman is receiving quinine IV, monitor blood glucose levels every hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If hypoglycaemia is detected, give 50% dextrose 50 mL IV followed

by dextrose (5% or 10%) 500 mL infused over eight hours. Note: Monitor blood glucose levels and adjust infusion accordingly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fluid balance carefully (page S-125).

## Anaemia

### Anaemia — phần 1

Severe malaria is often accompanied by anaemia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor haemoglobin levels daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor fluid balance (page S-125).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give furosemide 20 mg IV or by mouth with each unit of blood.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prescribe ferrous sulfate or ferrous fumarate 60 mg by mouth PLUS

folic acid 400 mcg by mouth once daily upon discharge.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman has a fever (temperature 38°C or more) more than 24 hours

after giving birth.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

When managing the woman’s problem, apply basic principles when providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment

(page S-1). Even if signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further, because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-18.; Differential diagnosis of fever after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs; Probable

Other Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Sometimes Present; Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fever/chills; • Light vaginal bleedinga; Postpartum

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Lower abdominal pain; • Shock; endometritis,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Purulent, foul-smelling lochia; page S-130

• Tender uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Persistent spiking fever/chills; • Poor response to; Pelvic abscess,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Lower abdominal pain and; antibiotics; page S-131

• Decreased breath sounds (may occur postoperatively)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fever; • Enlarged spleen; Uncomplicated

• Coma • Anaemia

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage rest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure adequate hydration by mouth or IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a fan or tepid sponge and, if necessary, open a window to help

decrease temperature.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider paracetamol 500–1000 mg (oral tablets or rectal

suppositories) every six to eight hours to help decrease temperature (maximum 4000 mg in 24 hours).

## Postpartum Endometritis

### Postpartum Endometritis — phần 1

Postpartum endometritis is a major cause of maternal death. Delayed or inadequate treatment of postpartum endometritis may result in pelvic abscess, peritonitis, septic shock, deep vein thrombosis, pulmonary embolism, chronic pelvic infection with recurrent pelvic pain and dyspareunia, tubal blockage, or infertility.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary. Use packed cells, if available (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman a combination of antibiotics for 24–48 hours after

complete resolution of clinical signs and symptoms (fever, uterine tenderness, purulent lochia, leucocytosis) (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

If clindamycin is not available administer:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ampicillin 2 g IV every 6 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

### Postpartum Endometritis — phần 2

When available, clindamycin (in combination with gentamycin) is more effective than ampicillin or a penicillin antibiotic for the treatment of postpartum endometritis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If fever is still present 72 hours after starting antibiotics,

re-evaluate and revise diagnosis. Note: Oral antibiotics are not necessary after stopping IV antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If retained placental fragments are suspected, perform a digital

exploration of the uterus to remove clots and large pieces. Use ovum forceps or a wide curette if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever after childbirth; S-131

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is no improvement with conservative measures and there are

signs of general peritonitis (fever, rebound tenderness, abdominal pain), perform a laparotomy to drain the pus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the uterus is necrotic and septic, perform subtotal hysterectomy

(page P-122).

## Pelvic Abscess

### Pelvic Abscess — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a combination of antibiotics before draining the abscess;

continue antibiotics until the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (page C- 50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS metronidazole 500 mg IV every eight hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the abscess is fluctuant in the cul-de-sac, drain the pus through the

cul-de-sac (page P-81). If the spiking fever continues, perform a laparotomy.

## Peritonitis

### Peritonitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide nasogastric suction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C- 34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for

48 hours (page C- 50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS metronidazole 500 mg IV every eight hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify and treat the underlying cause of the peritonitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform additional diagnostics such as X-ray or ultrasound to assist in

identifying the underlying cause.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The type of surgical intervention needed depends on the diagnosis of

the cause of the peritonitis. For example, closure may need to be performed for an intestinal or uterine perforation, whereas an abscess may need to be drained.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-132; Fever after childbirth

## Breast Engorgement

### Breast Engorgement — phần 1

Breast engorgement is an exaggeration of the lymphatic and venous engorgement that occurs before lactation. It is not the result of overdistension of the breast with milk.

## Breastfeeding

### Breastfeeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is breastfeeding and the baby is able to suckle:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage the woman to breastfeed more frequently, without

restrictions, using both breasts at each feeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Show the woman how to hold the baby and help the baby attach.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is breastfeeding and the baby is not able to suckle,

encourage the woman to express milk by hand or with a pump.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Relief measures before feeding or expression may include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Applying warm compresses to the breasts just before

breastfeeding, or encouraging the woman to take a warm shower;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Massaging the woman’s neck and back; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Having the woman express some milk manually before

breastfeeding, and wetting the nipple area to soften the areola to help the baby latch on properly and easily.

### Breastfeeding — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Relief measures after feeding or expression may include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Supporting breasts with a binder or bra;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Applying cold compresses to the breasts between feedings to

reduce swelling and pain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ibuprofen 200–400 mg every six to eight hours orally (maximum

dose of 1200 mg in 24 hours);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR paracetamol 500–1000 mg every six to eight hours orally as

an appropriate alternative (maximum 4000 mg in 24 hours).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in three days to ensure response.

## Not Breastfeeding

### Not Breastfeeding — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is not breastfeeding:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Encourage her to support breasts with a binder or bra.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply cold compresses to the breasts to reduce swelling and pain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoid massaging or applying heat to the breasts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever after childbirth; S-133

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Avoid stimulating the nipples.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give ibuprofen 200–400 mg every six to eight hours (maximum

dose 1200 mg in 24 hours);

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR paracetamol 500–1000 mg every six to eight hours orally

as an appropriate alternative (maximum dose 4000 mg in 24 hours).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up in three days to ensure response.

## Mastitis

### Mastitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat with antibiotics (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cloxacillin 500 mg by mouth every six hours for 10 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR erythromycin 250 mg every eight hours for 10 days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; continue breastfeeding;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; support the breasts with a binder or bra; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; apply cold compresses to the breasts between feedings to reduce

swelling and pain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman ibuprofen 200–400 mg every six to eight hours

(maximum dose 1200 mg in 24 hours);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR paracetamol 500–1000 mg every six to eight hours as an

appropriate alternative (maximum dose 4000 mg in 24 hours).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in three days to ensure response.

## Breast Abscess

### Breast Abscess — phần 1

Antibiotic Treatment

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat with antibiotics (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cloxacillin 500 mg by mouth every six hours for 10 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR erythromycin 250 mg every eight hours for 10 days.

Surgical Treatment Pus must be drained either by incision and drainage or ultrasound-guided needle aspiration (which may need to be repeated).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-134; Fever after childbirth

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Drain the abscess:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; General anaesthesia (e.g. ketamine, page P- 13) is usually

required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make the incision radially, extending from near the areolar

margin toward the periphery of the breast, to avoid injury to the milk ducts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wearing sterile gloves, use a finger or tissue forceps to break up

the pockets of pus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Loosely pack the cavity with gauze.

### Breast Abscess — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Remove the gauze pack after 24 hours and replace with a smaller

gauze pack.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is still pus in the cavity:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a gauze pack in the cavity and bring the edge out through

the wound as a wick, to facilitate drainage of any remaining pus;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR perform ultrasound-guided aspiration for abscesses in which

overlying skin is intact and the abscess is less than 5 cm in diameter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Local anaesthesia is generally sufficient.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; This can often be done as an outpatient procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If laboratory capacity is available, send drained or aspirated pus for

culture and sensitivity testing. Note: A large surgical incision should be avoided because it could damage the areola and milk ducts and interfere with subsequent breastfeeding.

Supportive Treatment

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to:

### Breast Abscess — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; continue breastfeeding even when there is collection of pus;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; support breasts with a binder or bra;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; apply cold compresses to the breasts between feedings to reduce

swelling and pain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ibuprofen 200–400 mg by mouth every six to eight hours as

needed (maximum dose 1200 mg in 24 hours);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR paracetamol 500–1000 mg by mouth every six to eight hours

(maximum dose 4000 mg in 24 hours).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Fever after childbirth; S-135

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassess in two to three days to determine the woman’s clinical status.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review danger signs with the woman (fever, etc.).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For women with a breast abscess, more frequent assessment and

wound dressing is necessary.

## Wound Abscess, Wound Seroma And Wound Haematoma

### Wound Abscess, Wound Seroma And Wound Haematoma — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is pus or fluid, open and drain the wound.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove infected skin or subcutaneous sutures and debride the wound.

Do not remove fascial sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is an abscess without cellulitis, antibiotics are not required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a damp dressing on the wound and have the woman return to

change the dressing every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman to practice good hygiene and to wear clean pads or

cloths that she changes often.

## Wound Cellulitis And Necrotizing Fasciitis

### Wound Cellulitis And Necrotizing Fasciitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is fluid or pus, open and drain the wound.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove infected skin or subcutaneous sutures and debride the wound.

Do not remove fascial sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If infection is superficial and does not involve deep tissues, monitor

for development of an abscess and give a combination of antibiotics (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 500 mg by mouth every six hours for five days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the infection is deep, involves muscles and is causing necrosis

(necrotizing fasciitis), give a combination of antibiotics until necrotic tissue has been removed and the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; penicillin G 2 million units IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS metronidazole 500 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-136; Fever after childbirth

### Wound Cellulitis And Necrotizing Fasciitis — phần 2

Note: Necrotizing fasciitis requires wide surgical debridement. Perform delayed primary closure two to four weeks later, depending on resolution of infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman has a severe infection or necrotizing fasciitis, admit

her to the hospital for management, and change the wound dressing twice daily.

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman is experiencing abdominal pain in the first

22 weeks of pregnancy. Abdominal pain might be the first presentation in serious complications such as abortion or ectopic pregnancy.

## General Management

### General Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition,

including vital signs (pulse, blood pressure and respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1). Note: Appendicitis should be suspected in any woman having abdominal pain. Appendicitis can be confused with other more common problems in pregnancy that cause abdominal pain (e.g. ectopic pregnancy, abruptio placentae, twisted ovarian cysts, pyelonephritis).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even if

signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate, depending on gestational age.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-19.; Diagnosis of abdominal pain in early pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs Sometimes; Probable

Other Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Present; Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abdominal pain; • Palpable, tender discrete mass Ovarian cyst,b

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Adnexal mass on vaginal; in lower abdomen; page S-138

- Dòng dữ liệu: examination; • Lighta vaginal bleeding

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-138; Abdominal Pain in Early Pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: Symptoms and Signs Sometimes; Probable

Other Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Present; Diagnosis

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Lower abdominal pain; • Abdominal distension; Appendicitis,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Low-grade fever; • Anorexia; page S-139

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Rebound tenderness; • Nausea/vomiting

• Paralytic ileus • Increased white blood cells • No mass in lower abdomen • Site of pain higher than expected

### Diagnosis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Dysuria; • Retropubic/suprapubic pain; Cystitis, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Increased frequency and; S-116

urgency of urination • Abdominal pain

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Dysuria; • Retropubic/suprapubic pain; Acute

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Spiking fever/chills; • Loin pain/tenderness; pyelonephritis,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Increased frequency and; • Tenderness in rib cage; page S-116

- Dòng dữ liệu: urgency of urination; • Anorexia

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abdominal pain; • Nausea/vomiting

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Low-grade fever/chills; • Rebound tenderness; Peritonitis, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Lower abdominal pain; • Abdominal distension; S-131

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Absent bowel sounds; • Anorexia

• Nausea/vomiting • Shock

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abdominal pain; • Fainting; Ectopic

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Light bleeding; • Tender adnexal mass; pregnancy, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Closed cervix; • Amenorrhoea; S-15

### Diagnosis — phần 3

• Uterus slightly larger than • Cervical motion tenderness normal • Uterus softer than normal a Light bleeding: takes longer than five minutes for a clean pad or cloth to be soaked.

b Ovarian cysts can be asymptomatic and are sometimes first detected on physical

examination.

## Ovarian Cysts

### Ovarian Cysts — phần 1

Ovarian cysts in pregnancy can cause abdominal pain due to torsion or rupture. Ovarian cysts most commonly undergo torsion and rupture during the first trimester.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a woman is in severe pain, suspect torsion or rupture. Perform

immediate laparotomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Abdominal Pain in Early Pregnancy; S-139

Note: If findings at laparotomy are suggestive of malignancy (solid areas in the tumour, growth extending outside the cyst wall), the specimen should be sent for immediate histological examination and the woman should be referred to a tertiary care centre for evaluation and management.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cyst is more than 10 cm and is asymptomatic:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If it is detected during the first trimester, observe for growth or

complications.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If it is detected during the second trimester, remove by

laparotomy to prevent complications.

### Ovarian Cysts — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cyst is between 5 and 10 cm, follow up. Laparotomy might be

required if the cyst increases in size or fails to regress.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cyst is less than 5 cm, it will usually regress on its own and

does not require treatment.

## Appendicitis

### Appendicitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a combination of antibiotics before surgery and continue until the

woman is postoperative and fever-free for 48 hours (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS metronidazole 500 mg IV every eight hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform an immediate surgical exploration (regardless of stage of

gestation) and perform appendectomy, if required. Note: Delaying diagnosis and treatment can result in rupture of the appendix, which may lead to generalized peritonitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of peritonitis (fever, rebound tenderness, abdominal

pain), give antibiotics as for peritonitis (page S-131). Note: The presence of peritonitis increases the likelihood of abortion or preterm labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is in severe pain, give morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight

IM.

### Appendicitis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tocolytic drugs might be needed to prevent preterm labour (page

S-146).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-140; Abdominal Pain in Early Pregnancy

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman is experiencing abdominal pain after 22 weeks of

pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The woman is experiencing abdominal pain during the first six weeks

after childbirth.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition, vital

signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration,), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, blood loss, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If shock is suspected, immediately begin treatment (page S-1). Even if

signs of shock are not present, keep shock in mind as you evaluate the woman further, because her status may worsen rapidly. If shock develops, it is important to begin treatment immediately. Note: Appendicitis should be suspected in any woman having abdominal pain. Appendicitis can be confused with other more common causes of abdominal pain in pregnancy. If appendicitis occurs in late pregnancy, the infection may be walled off by the gravid uterus. The size of the uterus

### Immediate Management — phần 2

rapidly decreases after birth, allowing the infection to spill into the peritoneal cavity. In these cases, appendicitis presents as generalized peritonitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is pregnant, check the fetal heart rate and ask about fetal

movements:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

listen, or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal movements are not felt or the fetal heart cannot be heard,

suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-142; Abdominal Pain in Later Pregnancy and After Childbirth

## Preterm Labour

### Preterm Labour — phần 1

Definitions of preterm birth: • extremely preterm (less than 28 weeks) • very preterm (28–31 weeks plus six days) • moderate to late preterm (32–36 weeks plus six days)

Preterm birth—defined as birth before 37 weeks of gestation—is the single most important determinant of adverse infant outcomes, in terms of survival and quality of life. The most beneficial maternal interventions are those that are aimed at improving outcomes for preterm infants when preterm birth is inevitable, such as administration of:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; antenatal corticosteroid therapy to improve fetal lung maturity and

chances of neonatal survival from 24 weeks to 34 weeks of gestation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; magnesium sulfate up to 32 weeks of gestation to prevent preterm

birth-related neurologic complications;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; antibiotics for women with preterm prelabour rupture of membranes

and/or clinical signs of infection.

### Preterm Labour — phần 2

Special care of the preterm newborn to prevent and treat complications of prematurity is critical to newborn survival.

Make every effort to confirm the gestational age of the fetus. The risk of harm of corticosteroid administration may outweigh benefits in late preterm births.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide information to the woman, and any accompanying family

members the woman would like to be involved in decision-making, about her diagnosis, treatment options and estimated time for in- patient care if required.

## Antenatal Corticosteroids

### Antenatal Corticosteroids — phần 1

Antenatal corticosteroids improve newborn outcomes (e.g. fewer fetal and neonatal deaths, reduced rate of respiratory distress syndrome and duration of mechanical ventilation or oxygen supplementation).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm the diagnosis of preterm labour by documenting cervical

effacement or dilatation over two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Abdominal Pain in Later Pregnancy and After Childbirth; S-145

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm gestational age of the pregnancy using all available

information (last menstrual period, physical exam in early pregnancy, ultrasound performed in the first trimester, symphysis fundal height).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide antenatal corticosteroid therapy to improve fetal lung maturity

and chances of neonatal survival for women at risk of preterm birth from 24 weeks to 34 weeks of gestation when the following conditions are met:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; gestational age assessment can be accurately undertaken;

### Antenatal Corticosteroids — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; preterm birth is considered imminent;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; there is no clinical evidence of maternal infection;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; adequate childbirth care is available (including the capacity to

recognize and safely manage preterm labour and birth); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the preterm newborn can receive adequate care if needed

(including resuscitation, thermal care, feeding support, infection treatment and safe oxygen use).

If adequate childbirth and preterm newborn care (including resuscitation, thermal care, feeding support, infection treatment and safe oxygen use) are not available in your setting, refer the woman to a hospital where adequate care is available before she gives birth, if possible. If all criteria are met to safely provide corticosteroids, with the exception of the availability of care for preterm infants, consider administration of the first dose of antenatal corticosteroids before transfer.

### Antenatal Corticosteroids — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If conditions are met, give the following course of corticosteroids to

the mother:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; betamethasone 12 mg IM, two doses 24 hours apart;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR dexamethasone 6 mg IM, four doses 12 hours apart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In case of pregestational or gestational diabetes, maintain optimal

glycaemic control.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If preterm birth does not occur within seven days after the initial

course of corticosteroids, and if subsequent clinical assessment demonstrates that there is a high risk of preterm birth in the next seven days, repeat a single course of antenatal corticosteroids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-146; Abdominal Pain in Later Pregnancy and After Childbirth

## Tocolysis

### Tocolysis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a tocolytic drug (e.g. nifedipine) to provide a window for

administration of antenatal corticosteroids and/or in-utero fetal transfer to an appropriate neonatal health care setting:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give a loading dose of 20 mg nifedipine immediate-release

capsule sublingually.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If required, give an additional 10 mg every 15 minutes up to a

maximum of 40 mg in the first hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Follow up with 20 mg sustained-release tablet orally daily for up

to 48 hours or until transfer is completed, whichever comes first.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Inform the woman to be aware of side effects of nifedipine such

as headache, flushing, dizziness, tiredness, palpitations and itching.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor maternal and fetal condition (pulse, blood pressure, signs of

### Tocolysis — phần 2

respiratory distress, uterine contractions, loss of amniotic fluid or blood, fetal heart rate, fluid balance). Note: Do not give tocolytic drugs for more than 48 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give a combination of tocolytic agents as there is no additional

benefit.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tocolytics should not be used in the following conditions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; preterm prelabour rupture of membranes (PPROM)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; chorioamnionitis

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; placental abruption

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cardiac disease.

## Magnesium Sulfate

### Magnesium Sulfate — phần 1

If gestational age is less than 32 weeks:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give magnesium sulfate to the mother as an intravenous infusion or

intramuscular injections to prevent cerebral palsy in the infant (Table S-21, page S-147).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Abdominal Pain in Later Pregnancy and After Childbirth; S-147

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-21.; Possible dosing regimens of magnesium sulfate

- Dòng dữ liệu: Route; Initial dose; Maintenance dose

4 g over 1 g per hour for 24 hours or

- Dòng dữ liệu: 20; until birth, whichever occurs

minutes earlier

- Dòng dữ liệu: IV; • IV 4 g over 30 minutes; 2 g per hour

OR • IV bolus of 4 g given as single dose and IV 6 g over 20–30 minutes

- Dòng dữ liệu: IM; 5g; 5 g every four hours for 24 hours or until the

woman has given birth, whichever occurs earlier

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor urinary output and signs of magnesium overdose or toxicity

### Magnesium Sulfate — phần 2

(respiratory rate less than 16 breaths per minute and/or absent patellar reflexes).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Withhold magnesium sulfate if there are signs of magnesium

toxicity (Box S-5, page S-60).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Restart only after signs of overdose or toxicity disappear.

## Antibiotic

### Antibiotic — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If amniotic membranes are intact and there are no clinical signs of

infection, do not give prophylactic antibiotics. If the woman has confirmed Group B streptococcal colonization, give amoxicillin 500 mg by mouth every eight hours for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If amniotic membranes are ruptured (PPROM) or there are

clinical signs of infection, give an antibiotic to reduce the risk of chorioamnionitis in the mother and the risk of neonatal infections (e.g. pneumonia, cerebral abnormality):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; oral erythromycin 250 mg every six hours for 10 days (or until

birth)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours.

Note: Do not use amoxicillin plus clavulanic acid (co-amoxiclav) in case of PPROM; it increases the risk of necrotizing enterocolitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-148; Abdominal Pain in Later Pregnancy and After Childbirth

## Management Of Labour

### Management Of Labour — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If labour continues and gestation is less than 37 weeks:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; monitor progress of labour using the partograph (page C-91).

Routine caesarean birth is not recommended to improve newborn outcomes for preterm infants, regardless of cephalic or breech presentation. Avoid vacuum-assisted birth, as the risks of intracranial bleeding in the preterm baby are high.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare for management of preterm or low birth weight baby and

anticipate the need for resuscitation (page S-167).

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A woman is short of breath during pregnancy, labour or after giving

birth.

providing care (page C-25).

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a rapid evaluation of the woman’s general condition,

including vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration), level of consciousness, presence of anxiety and/or confusion, volume of blood loss, whether bleeding is accompanied by pain, and skin colour and temperature (page C-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prop up the woman on her left side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen at 4–6 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is pregnant, check the fetal heart rate and ask about

fetal movements, depending on gestational age:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

listen, or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal movements are not felt or the fetal heart cannot be

### Immediate Management — phần 2

heard, suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Start an IV infusion and infuse IV fluids (page C-34).

Note: Obtain haemoglobin estimates using haemoglobinometer or other simple method. Or send a blood sample for haemoglobin or haematocrit and type and screen, and order blood for possible transfusion, before infusing IV fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-150; Difficulty in breathing

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-22.; Diagnosis of difficulty in breathing

Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Other Symptoms and Signs; Probable Diagnosis

Sometimes Present

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficulty in breathing; • Lethargy and fatigue; Severe anaemia,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Pallor of conjunctiva, tongue, • Flat or concave nails; page S-151

nail beds and/or palms • Haemoglobin 7g per dL or less • Haematocrit 20% or less

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Symptoms and signs of; • Oedema; Heart failure due to

- Dòng dữ liệu: severe anaemia; • Cough; anaemia, page S-151

• Rales • Swelling of legs • Enlarged liver • Prominent neck veins

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficulty in breathing; • Irregular heartbeat; Heart failure due to

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Diastolic murmur and/or; • Enlarged heart; heart disease, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Harsh systolic murmur with; • Rales; S-152

- Dòng dữ liệu: palpable thrill; • Cyanosis (blueness)

### Diagnosis — phần 2

• Cough • Swelling of legs • Enlarged liver • Prominent neck veins

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficulty in breathing; • Consolidation; Pneumonia, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fever; • Congested throat; S-153

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Cough with expectoration; • Rapid breathing

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Chest pain; • Rhonchi/rales

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficulty in breathing; • Cough with expectoration; Bronchial asthma,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Wheezing; • Rhonchi/rales; page S-153

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Difficulty in breathing; • Rales; Pulmonary oedema

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Hypertension; • Frothy cough; associated with

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Proteinuria; pre-eclampsiaa

a Withhold fluids and give furosemide 40 mg IV once (page S-125).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Difficulty in breathing; S-151

## Severe Anaemia

### Severe Anaemia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary (page C-37):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use packed cells.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If blood cannot be centrifuged, let the bag of blood hang until

the cells have settled. Infuse the cells slowly and dispose of the remaining serum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give furosemide 40 mg IV with each unit of packed cells.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If Plasmodium falciparum malaria is suspected, manage as severe

malaria (page S-121).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ferrous sulfate or ferrous fumarate 120 mg by mouth PLUS folic

acid 400 mcg by mouth once daily for six months during pregnancy. Continue for three months postpartum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where hookworm is endemic (prevalence of 20% or more), give one

of the following anthelmintic treatments:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; albendazole 400 mg by mouth once;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR mebendazole 500 mg by mouth once or 100 mg two times per

day for three days;

### Severe Anaemia — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR levamisole 2.5 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily for

three days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR pyrantel 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth once daily for three

days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If hookworm is highly endemic (prevalence of 50% or more), repeat

the anthelmintic treatment 12 weeks after the first dose.

## Heart Failure Due To Anaemia

### Heart Failure Due To Anaemia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfusion is almost always necessary in heart failure due to anaemia

(page C-37):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use packed or sedimented cells as described for severe anaemia

(above).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give furosemide 40 mg IV with each unit of packed cells.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-152; Difficulty in breathing

## Heart Failure Due To Heart Disease

### Heart Failure Due To Heart Disease — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Treat acute heart failure. Drugs used may include:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; morphine 10 mg IM as a single dose;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR furosemide 40 mg IV, repeated as necessary;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR digoxin 0.5 mg IM as a single dose;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR nitroglycerine 0.3 mg under the tongue, repeated in

15 minutes, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Refer to a higher level if needed.

## Heart Failure During Labour And Childbirth

### Heart Failure During Labour And Childbirth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prop up the woman on her left side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Limit infusion of IV fluids to decrease the risk of circulatory overload,

and maintain a strict fluid balance chart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure adequate analgesia (page C-55).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If oxytocin infusion is required, use a higher concentration at a

slower rate while maintaining a fluid balance chart (e.g. the concentration may be doubled if the number of drops per minute is decreased by half; Table P-8, page P-26). Note: Do not give ergometrine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the woman avoid sustained bearing-down efforts during the

expulsive phase of second stage of labour, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If necessary to decrease the woman’s workload, assist the birth

using an obstetric vacuum (page P-33) or forceps (page P-41).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure active management of third stage of labour (page C-102).

### Heart Failure During Labour And Childbirth — phần 2

Heart failure is not an indication for caesarean birth.

## Heart Failure During Caesarean Birth

### Heart Failure During Caesarean Birth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use local anaesthesia with conscious sedation (page P-7). Avoid

spinal anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver baby and placenta (page P-56).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Difficulty in breathing; S-153

## Pneumonia

### Pneumonia — phần 1

Inflammation in pneumonia affects the lung parenchyma and involves respiratory bronchioles and alveoli. There is loss of lung capacity that is less tolerated by pregnant women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A radiograph of the chest might be required to confirm the diagnosis

of pneumonia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give erythromycin 500 mg by mouth every six hours for seven days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give steam inhalation.

Consider the possibility of tuberculosis in areas where it is prevalent.

## Bronchial Asthma

### Bronchial Asthma — phần 1

Bronchial asthma complicates 3–4% of pregnancies. Pregnancy is associated with worsening of the symptoms in one third of affected women.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bronchospasm occurs, give bronchodilators (e.g. salbutamol 4 mg

by mouth every four hours OR 250 mcg aerosol every 15 minutes for three doses).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is no response to bronchodilators, give corticosteroids such

as hydrocortisone 2 mg/kg body weight IV every four hours as needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (bronchitis), give ampicillin 2 g IV

every six hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid the use of prostaglandins. For prevention and treatment of PPH,

give oxytocin 10 units IM or give ergometrine 0.2 mg IM.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After acute exacerbation has been managed, continue treatment with

inhaled bronchodilators and inhaled corticosteroids to prevent recurrent acute episodes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-154; Difficulty in breathing

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Fetal movements are not felt after 22 weeks of gestation or during

labour.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassure the woman and provide emotional support (page C-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the fetal heart rate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is heard but is depressed and the woman

has had sedatives, wait for the effect of the drugs to wear off and then recheck.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

listen, or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

## Diagnosis

### Diagnosis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-23.; Diagnosis of loss of fetal movements

Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Other Symptoms and Signs; Probable Diagnosis

Sometimes Present

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Decreased/absent fetal; • Shock; Abruptio placentae,

- Dòng dữ liệu: movements; • Tense/tender uterus; page S-23

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Intermittent or constant; • Fetal distress or absent

- Dòng dữ liệu: abdominal pain; fetal heart sounds

• Bleeding after 22 weeks of gestation (may be retained in the uterus)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Absent fetal movements and; • Shock; Ruptured uterus,

- Dòng dữ liệu: fetal heart sounds; • Abdominal distension/free page S-24

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Bleeding (intra-abdominal; fluid

- Dòng dữ liệu: and/or vaginal); • Abnormal uterine contour

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Severe abdominal pain (may; • Tender abdomen

- Dòng dữ liệu: decrease after rupture); • Easily palpable fetal parts

• Rapid maternal pulse

### Diagnosis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-156; Loss of fetal movements

Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Other Symptoms and Signs; Probable Diagnosis

Sometimes Present

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Decreased/absent fetal; • Thick meconium-stained; Fetal distress, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: movements; fluid; S-109

• Abnormal fetal heart rate (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute)

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Absent fetal movements and; • Symptoms of pregnancy; Fetal death, page

- Dòng dữ liệu: fetal heart sounds; cease; S-156

• Symphysis-fundal height decreases • Uterine size decreases

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

Intrauterine death can be the result of fetal growth restriction, fetal infection, a cord accident or congenital anomalies. Where syphilis is prevalent, a large proportion of fetal deaths are attributable to this disease.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If X-ray is available, confirm fetal death after five days. Signs include

overlapping skull bones, hyper-flexed spinal column, gas bubbles in heart and great vessels, and oedema of the scalp.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Alternatively, if ultrasound is available, confirm fetal death. Signs

include absent fetal heart activity, abnormal fetal head shape, reduced or absent amniotic fluid, and doubled-up fetus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain the problem to the woman and her family (page C-5). Discuss

the options of expectant or active (induction of labour) management with them:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Reassure the woman that in 90% of cases the fetus is

### Specific Management — phần 2

spontaneously expelled during the waiting period with no complications.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If expectant management is planned, await spontaneous onset of

labour during the next four weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If platelets are decreasing, four weeks have passed without

spontaneous labour, fibrinogen levels are low or the woman requests it, consider active management.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Loss of fetal movements; S-157

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If induction of labour is planned, assess the cervix

(Table P-6, page P-19):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is favourable (has a Bishop score of 6 or more,

Table P-6, page P-19), labour is usually successfully induced with oxytocin alone (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is unfavourable (has a Bishop score of 5 or less,

### Specific Management — phần 3

Table P-6, page P-19), ripen the cervix using prostaglandins (page P-21) such as misoprostol, a Foley or balloon catheter (page P-22), or oxytocin (page P-23). Note: Do not rupture the membranes because this can cause infection. Note: Sweeping membranes could reduce the need for formal induction of labour. It is suitable for non-urgent indications for pregnancy termination (page P-20).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Perform caesarean only as a last resort.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If spontaneous labour does not occur within four weeks, platelets

are decreasing and the cervix is unfavourable (has a Bishop score of 5 or less), or if the woman requests it, ripen the cervix using misoprostol (page P-21).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal discharge),

give antibiotics as for endometritis (page S-130).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a clotting test shows failure of a clot to form after seven minutes

### Specific Management — phần 4

or a soft clot that breaks down easily, suspect coagulopathy (page S-24).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-158; Loss of fetal movements

## Problem

### Problem — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Watery vaginal discharge after 22 weeks of gestation

Prelabour rupture of membranes (PROM) is rupture of the membranes before labour has begun. PROM can occur either when the fetus is immature or preterm (i.e. before 37 weeks)—also called preterm prelabour rupture of membranes (PPROM)—or when the fetus is mature (term).

It is important that the gestational age is accurately assessed (ideally confirmed by ultrasound scan in early pregnancy) and rupture of membranes confirmed in order to avoid inadvisable corticosteroid and antibiotic administration.

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

providing care (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Confirm accuracy of calculated gestational age, if possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Document time and history of the reported vaginal fluid loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform and record maternal temperature, pulse and blood pressure,

respirations and oxygen saturation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Auscultate the fetal heart rate and confirm presence of fetal

movements.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are fetal heart rate abnormalities (less than 100 or more

than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to

listen, or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If fetal movements are not felt or the fetal heart cannot be

heard, suspect fetal death (page S-156).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform an abdominal palpation noting: Symphysis fundal height, lie

### Immediate Management — phần 2

(if appropriate depending on gestational age), presentation (if appropriate depending on gestational age), uterine tenderness/ irritability and uterine contractions. If the woman complains of bleeding in later pregnancy (after 22 weeks), do not do a digital vaginal examination.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-160; Prelabour rupture of membranes

## Confirming The Diagnosis

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 1

The typical odour of amniotic fluid confirms the diagnosis. If membrane rupture is not recent, or when leakage is gradual, confirming the diagnosis might be difficult: Do not perform a digital vaginal examination; it does not help to establish the diagnosis and can introduce infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a vaginal pad over the vulva and examine the pad (visually and

by odour) one hour later.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a high-level disinfected or sterile speculum to assess vaginal

discharge (amount, colour, odour) and exclude urinary incontinence:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fluid might be seen coming from the cervix or forming a pool in

the posterior fornix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ask the woman to cough; this may cause a gush of fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Determine cervical dilatation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If available, perform tests:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The nitrazine test depends on the fact that vaginal secretions and

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 2

urine are acidic while amniotic fluid is alkaline. Hold a piece of nitrazine paper in a haemostat and touch it against the fluid pooled on the speculum blade. A change from yellow to blue indicates alkalinity (presence of amniotic fluid).

Note: Blood and some vaginal infections give false positive results.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; For the ferning test, spread some fluid on a slide and let it dry.

Examine it with a microscope. Amniotic fluid crystallizes and may leave a fern-leaf pattern. False negatives are frequent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Prelabour rupture of membranes; S-161

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE S-24.; Differential diagnosis of vaginal discharge

Symptoms and Signs

- Dòng dữ liệu: Other Symptoms and Signs; Probable Diagnosis

Sometimes Present

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Watery vaginal discharge; • Sudden gush or; Prelabour rupture of

- Dòng dữ liệu: intermittent leaking of; membranes,

- Dòng dữ liệu: fluid; page S-159

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 3

• Fluid seen at introitus • No contractions within one hour

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fever/chills; • History of loss of fluid; Amnionitis, page S-163

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Maternal tachycardia; • Foul-smelling watery

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Abdominal pain; discharge after 22 weeks

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Fetal tachycardia; • Tender uterus

• Light vaginal bleedinga

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Foul-smelling vaginal; • Itching; Vaginitis/cervicitisb

- Dòng dữ liệu: discharge; • Frothy/curd-like discharge

- Dòng dữ liệu: • No history of loss of fluid; • Abdominal pain

• Dysuria

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Bloody vaginal discharge; • Abdominal pain; Antepartum

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Loss of fetal movements; haemorrhage,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Heavy, prolonged vaginal; page S-22

bleeding

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Vaginal bleeding; Abruptio placentae,

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Intermittent or constant; page S-23

abdominal pain

### Confirming The Diagnosis — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Blood-stained mucus or; • Cervical dilatation and; Possible term labour,

- Dòng dữ liệu: watery vaginal discharge; effacement; page C-77, or possible

- Dòng dữ liệu: (show); • Contractions; preterm labour, page

a Light bleeding: takes longer than five minutes for a clean pad or cloth to be soaked b Determine cause and treat accordingly.

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide information about the woman’s diagnosis, treatment options

and estimated time for inpatient care, if required, to the woman and any accompanying family members the woman would like to be involved in decision-making.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-162; Prelabour rupture of membranes

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is vaginal bleeding with intermittent or constant

abdominal pain, suspect abruptio placentae (page S-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are palpable contractions and blood-stained mucus

discharge, suspect preterm (page S-144) or term (page C-77) labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection (fever, foul-smelling vaginal discharge),

### Specific Management — phần 2

give the woman antibiotics and manage as for amnionitis, regardless of gestational age (page S-163). Confirm gestational age of the pregnancy using all available information (last menstrual period, physical exam in early pregnancy, ultrasound performed in the first trimester, symphysis fundal height).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of infection, and the pregnancy is less than

37 weeks (when fetal lungs are more likely to be immature), give antibiotics to the mother to reduce maternal and neonatal infective morbidity (page C-49).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If gestational age is between 24 and 34 weeks and preterm birth is

considered imminent, give corticosteroids to the mother (S-144) to improve fetal lung maturity and chances of neonatal survival.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If gestational age is less than 32 weeks and preterm birth is likely

within the next 24 hours, consider magnesium sulfate for neuroprotection (page S-146).

### Specific Management — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Induce labour using oxytocin IV (page P-23) or misoprostol (orally or

vaginally, in a non-scarred uterus) (page P-21) at 37 weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are contraindications for vaginal birth, prepare for caesarean

birth at 37 weeks (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the pregnancy is 37 weeks or more:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is confirmed Group B streptococcus colonization, give

prophylactic antibiotics to help reduce Group B streptococcus infection in the neonate, even if the woman received antibiotics previously:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours until birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assess the cervix (page P-19):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the cervix is favourable (soft, thin, partly dilated), induce

labour using oxytocin IV (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Prelabour rupture of membranes; S-163

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the cervix is unfavourable (firm, thick, closed) and there is

### Specific Management — phần 4

no scar on the uterus, ripen the cervix using misoprostol (orally or vaginally) (page P-21) or oxytocin IV (page P-23). If there is a previous uterine scar, use oxytocin and monitor the woman closely.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are contraindications for vaginal birth, prepare for

caesarean (page P-53).

## Amnionitis

### Amnionitis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a combination of antibiotics until the woman gives birth

(page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman gives birth vaginally, continue treatment for 24–48

hours after the symptoms and signs of infection (e.g. fever, uterine tenderness) have subsided.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman has a caesarean, cleanse the vagina with

povidone-iodine before the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the cervix (page P-19):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is favourable (soft, thin, partly dilated), induce

labour using oxytocin (page P-23).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is unfavourable (firm, thick, closed), ripen the

cervix using oral or vaginal misoprostol, or infuse oxytocin where misoprostol use is contraindicated (page P-23); perform a caesarean (page P-53).

### Amnionitis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If postpartum endometritis is suspected (fever, foul-smelling vaginal

discharge), give the woman antibiotics (page S-130).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If newborn sepsis is suspected, arrange for a blood culture, if

possible, and antibiotics (page S-182).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-164; Prelabour rupture of membranes

## Immediate Newborn Conditions Or Problems S-165

### Immediate Newborn Conditions Or Problems S-165 — phần 1

Note: This chapter describes immediate management of newborn problems occurring in the first 24 hours after birth. For further guidance on managing the newborn with immediate or ongoing problems, please refer to WHO documents for the management of sick newborns, including WHO, Pocket Book of Hospital Care for Children, 2013.

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The newborn has serious conditions or problems that require

immediate attention:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; gasping or not breathing;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; breathing with difficulty (less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per

minute, indrawing of the chest, or grunting);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; extremely preterm (less than 28 weeks) or very preterm (28 to less

than 32 weeks);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; very low birth weight (less than 1500 g);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; central cyanosis (blueness);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pallor;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; lethargy, drowsiness or unconsciousness;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; movement only when stimulated or no movement at all;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hypothermia (cold to the touch and/or axillary temperature less

than 36.5°C);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; hyperthermia (hot to the touch and/or axillary temperature more

than 37.5°C);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; not feeding well;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; convulsions;

### Problems — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; jaundice (the face is jaundiced earlier than 24 hours after birth, or

the palms or soles are jaundiced at any time).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The newborn has other conditions or problems that require attention in

the birthing room or as soon after birth as possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; moderate to late preterm (32 weeks to 36 weeks plus six days);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; low birth weight (1500–2500 g);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; risk of bacterial infection in an apparently normal newborn

(page C-112);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; exposure to HIV, syphilis or tuberculosis (pages S-183–185).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-166; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

## Immediate Management

### Immediate Management — phần 1

Many serious conditions in newborns—bacterial infections, malformations, severe asphyxia and respiratory distress syndrome due to preterm birth— present in a similar way, with difficulty in breathing, lethargy, and poor or no feeding. It is difficult to distinguish between the conditions without diagnostic methods. Nevertheless, treatment must start immediately, even without a clear diagnosis of a specific cause. Babies with any of these

problems should be suspected to have a serious condition and should be transferred without delay to the appropriate service for the care of sick newborns. Give the first dose of both ampicillin and gentamicin IM in the thigh before referral for possible serious illness, severe umbilical infection or severe skin infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IM if at term, 4 mg/kg body weight

IM if preterm, every 24 hours;

### Immediate Management — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; PLUS ampicillin 50 mg/kg body weight IM every 12 hours.

Inform the mother and birth companion about what is happening and keep them updated as you care for the baby.

## Specific Management

### Specific Management — phần 1

Note: Observe standard infection prevention and control practices when caring for and resuscitating a newborn (pages C-113).

## Gasping Or Not Breathing

### Gasping Or Not Breathing — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dry the baby; observe the infant while drying.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the wet cloth and keep the baby warm by skin-to-skin contact

with the mother; cover with a clean, dry cloth. Where available, put a hat on the baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If, after drying, the baby is not crying/breathing or does not have good

muscle tone or vigorous movements:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Stimulate breathing by rubbing the baby’s back two or three

times.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clear the airway: remove secretions from the baby’s mouth, then

nose. Suctioning should be done only if amniotic fluid was meconium stained or the mouth or nose is full of secretions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-167

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In settings where mechanical equipment to generate negative

### Gasping Or Not Breathing — phần 2

pressure for suctioning is not available and a newly born baby requires suctioning, a bulb syringe (single-use or easy to clean) should be used.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If, after stimulation and clearing the airway, the baby is still not

crying/breathing:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Call for help.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Clamp and cut the cord.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby to the newborn resuscitation area (firm, warm

surface under a radiant heater).

## Resuscitation

### Resuscitation — phần 1

Airway: Opening the Airway

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the newborn (Fig. S-30):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby on her back.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Position the head in a slightly extended position (“sniffing”) to

open the airway.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep the baby wrapped or covered, except for the face and upper

chest.

FIGURE S-30. Correct position of the head for ventilation; note that the neck is less extended than in adults

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn has thick secretions (mouth or nose) or was delivered

through meconium-stained amniotic fluid, quickly clear the airway by suctioning the mouth and nostrils before initiating positive pressure ventilation. Note: Suction the mouth, nose and oropharynx by direct vision only. Do not suction deep in the throat as this may cause the baby’s heart to slow or

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-168; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

### Resuscitation — phần 2
