the baby to stop breathing. The only indication for tracheal suctioning is a depressed neonate born through thick meconium-stained amniotic fluid. In this case, tracheal suctioning should be done before initiating positive pressure ventilation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassess the baby:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the newborn starts crying or breathing, no further immediate

action is needed. Proceed with initial care of the newborn (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is still not breathing, start ventilating within one

minute after birth (see below). Note: If the baby is not crying/breathing well after the airway is cleared and brief stimulation, the baby needs ventilation with a bag and mask. More stimulation is unlikely to be effective. Prolonged stimulation only wastes time while the baby is becoming sicker.

### Resuscitation — phần 3

Breathing: Ventilating the Newborn Start positive pressure ventilation with a mask and self-inflating bag within one minute of birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Recheck the newborn’s position. The neck should be slightly extended

(Fig. S-30, page S-167).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the mask and check the seal (Figs. S-31 and S-32):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Stand at the baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Select a mask size that fits over the baby’s nose and mouth

(Fig. S-31): size 1 for normal weight infants, size 0 for small (less than 2.5 kg) infants.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the mask on the newborn’s face. It should cover the chin,

mouth and nose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-169

FIGURE S-31. Fitting mask over face

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Form a seal between the mask and the face.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Squeeze the bag with two fingers only or with the whole hand,

depending on the size of the bag.

### Resuscitation — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the seal by ventilating twice and observing the rise of the

chest. FIGURE S-32. Ventilation with bag and mask

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once a seal is ensured and chest movement is present, ventilate the

newborn. Maintain the correct rate (approximately 40 breaths per minute) and pressure (observe the chest for an easy rise and fall):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Count out loud: “Breathe—two—three” as you ventilate the baby

(Fig. S-33). Squeeze the bag as you say “breathe” and release the pressure on the bag as you say “two—three.” This helps you to ventilate with an even rhythm, at a rate that the newborn’s lungs are naturally adapted to.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-170; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

FIGURE S-33. Timing the rate of ventilation

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s chest is rising, ventilation pressure is probably

### Resuscitation — phần 5

adequate. Note: Make sure the chest moves up with each press on the bag; in a very small infant, make sure the chest does not move too much (danger of causing pneumothorax).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s chest is not rising:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Repeat suction of mouth and nose to remove mucus, blood or

meconium from the airway.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Recheck and correct, if necessary, the position of the

newborn (Fig. S-30, page S-167).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If you hear air escaping from the mask, reposition the mask

on the baby’s face to improve the seal between mask and face (the most common leak is between the nose and the cheeks).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Squeeze the bag harder to increase ventilation pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-171

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-10.; Use of oxygen during resuscitation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For newly born term or preterm (more than 32 weeks’ gestation) babies

### Resuscitation — phần 6

requiring positive pressure ventilation, ventilation should be initiated with air.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For preterm babies born before or at 32 weeks of gestation, start ventilation

with 30% (not 100%) oxygen. If this is not possible, ventilation should be started with air.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For newborns who continue to have a heart rate of less than 60 beats per

minute after 30 seconds of adequate ventilation with air, progressively higher concentrations of oxygen should be considered. However, if oxygen is not available, ventilation should be continued with air.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If available, and if there is more than one health worker performing

newborn resuscitation, pulse oximetry should be used to determine the need for supplemental oxygen and to monitor the needed concentration of oxygen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother of the newborn received morphine before giving birth,

### Resuscitation — phần 7

consider administering naloxone after vital signs have been established (Box S-11).

BOX S-11. Counteracting respiratory depression in the newborn caused by narcotic medications

If the mother received morphine, naloxone is the medication to counteract respiratory depression in the newborn caused by these medications. Note: Do not administer naloxone to newborns whose mothers are suspected of having recently abused narcotic medications.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of respiratory depression, begin resuscitation immediately:

- After vital signs have been established, give naloxone 0.1 mg/kg body weight IV to the newborn. - Naloxone may be given IM after successful resuscitation, if the infant has adequate peripheral circulation. Repeated doses may be required to prevent recurrent respiratory depression.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of respiratory depression, but morphine was given to the

### Resuscitation — phần 8

mother within four hours of giving birth, observe the baby expectantly for signs of respiratory depression and treat as above if they occur.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-172; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

Evaluate Resuscitation Efforts

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ventilate for one minute; then stop and quickly:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; assess if the newborn is breathing spontaneously; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; rapidly measure the heart rate (normal is more than 100 beats per

minute) by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; feeling the umbilical cord pulse;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR listening to the heartbeat with a stethoscope.

Circulation: Chest Compressions

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Decide if chest compressions are needed:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When a second skilled provider is present, and the neonate has a

### Resuscitation — phần 9

heart rate of less than 60 beats per minute after one minute of positive pressure ventilation, consider chest compressions in addition to positive pressure ventilation (see WHO, Guidelines on Basic Newborn Resuscitation, 2012). Note: In newly born babies who do not start breathing within one minute after birth, priority should be given to providing adequate ventilation rather than to performing chest compressions.

Chest compressions are of little value unless the lungs are also being ventilated sufficiently.

Continue or Stop Resuscitation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If breathing is normal (30–60 breaths per minute) and there is no

indrawing of the chest and no grunting for one minute, no further resuscitation is needed. Proceed with initial care of the newborn (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn is not breathing, or the breathing is weak, continue

ventilating until spontaneous breathing begins.

### Resuscitation — phần 10

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn starts crying, stop ventilating and continue to observe

breathing for five minutes after crying stops:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If breathing is normal (30–60 breaths per minute) and there is no

indrawing of the chest and no grunting for one minute, no further resuscitation is needed. Proceed with initial care of the newborn (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-173

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the respiratory rate is less than 30 breaths per minute,

continue ventilating.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there is severe indrawing of the chest, ventilate with oxygen,

if available (Box S-13, page S-176). Arrange to transfer the baby to the most appropriate service for the care of sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn is not breathing regularly after 20 minutes of

ventilation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby to the most appropriate service for the care of

### Resuscitation — phần 11

sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; During the transfer, keep the newborn warm and ventilated, if

necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is no gasping or breathing at all after 20 minutes of

ventilation, stop ventilating; the baby is stillborn. Provide emotional support to the mother and family (page C-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn has no detectable heart rate after 10 minutes of

effective ventilation, stop resuscitation when no advanced resuscitation care is available. Provide emotional support to the mother and family (page C-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the heart rate continues to be less than 60 beats per minute

without spontaneous breathing after 20 minutes of ventilation, stop ventilating when no advanced resuscitation care is available. Provide emotional support to the mother and family (page C-9).

Care after Successful Resuscitation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prevent heat loss:

### Resuscitation — phần 12

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby skin-to-skin on the mother’s chest and cover the

baby’s body and head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, place the baby under a radiant heater.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the newborn and count the number of breaths per minute:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty breathing

(less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, indrawing of the chest, or grunting), give oxygen by nasal catheter or prongs (Box S-13, page S-176).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure the baby’s axillary temperature:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the temperature is 36.5°C or more, keep the baby in skin-to-

skin contact on the mother’s chest and encourage breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-174; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the temperature is less than 36.5°C, rewarm the baby (page

S-179).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to begin breastfeeding. A newborn who

### Resuscitation — phần 13

required resuscitation has a higher risk of developing hypoglycaemia:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If suckling is good, the newborn is recovering well.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If suckling is not good, transfer the baby to the appropriate

service for the care of sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure frequent monitoring of the newborn during the next 24 hours.

If signs of breathing difficulties recur, arrange to transfer the baby to the most appropriate service for the care of sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-12.; Resuscitation equipment

To avoid delays during an emergency situation, it is vital to check that equipment for newborn resuscitation is available, functional and in good condition before all deliveries:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have masks available (size 1 for normal weight newborns and size 0 for small

newborns).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Test vital functions of the ventilation device:

### Resuscitation — phần 14

- Squeeze the ventilation bag and watch for the valve in the patient outlet to open as you squeeze. This shows the device is ready to deliver air to a patient. - Seal the mask tightly to the palm of your hand and squeeze hard enough to open the pressure release valve:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If you feel pressure against your hand, the bag is generating

adequate pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the valve in the patient outlet opens as you squeeze, this shows

that air that cannot be delivered through a blocked airway will escape through the pressure relief valve.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the bag reinflates when you release the grip, the bag is

functioning properly. - Check the mask rim for any damage that could prevent an airtight mask seal to the face.

## Cyanosis Or Breathing Difficulty

### Cyanosis Or Breathing Difficulty — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty breathing (less

than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, severe indrawing of the lower chest wall, or grunting), give oxygen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Suction the mouth and nose to ensure the airways are clear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give oxygen at 0.5 L per minute by nasal catheter or nasal prongs

(Box S-13, page S-176).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-175

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby to the appropriate service for the care of sick

newborns. Some newborns may have fast breathing as the only sign of severe illness.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is kept warm. Wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth;

cover with a blanket and ensure that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If respiratory distress syndrome is diagnosed in a preterm infant on the

basis of clinical or radiological criteria:

### Cyanosis Or Breathing Difficulty — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) in health

facilities that can provide quality supportive care to neonates. If oxygen therapy is to be delivered with CPAP, use low concentrations of blended oxygen and titrate upwards based on the blood oxygen saturation levels. If blenders are not available, use room air. The use of 100% oxygen is not recommended because of demonstrable harms (see Box S-10).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Provide surfactant replacement therapy for intubated and ventilated

infants in health facilities where intubation, ventilator care, blood gas analysis, newborn nursing care and monitoring are available (WHO, WHO Pocket Book of Hospital Care for Children, 2013, and WHO Recommendations on Interventions to Improve Preterm Birth Outcomes, 2015).

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-176; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-13.; Use of oxygen

### Cyanosis Or Breathing Difficulty — phần 3

Give oxygen to neonates who have any of the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; central cyanosis or gasping

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; grunting with every breath

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; difficulty feeding due to respiratory distress

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; severe lower chest wall indrawing

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; head nodding (i.e. a nodding movement of the head, synchronous with the

respiration and indicating severe respiratory distress). When using oxygen, remember:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use supplemental oxygen for cyanosis or difficulty breathing only if the baby’s

oxygen saturation is 90% or less. Oxygen flow should be regulated to maintain saturation of greater than 90% but less than 95% to avoid eye damage. Oxygen can be discontinued once the infant can maintain saturation over 90% in room air.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Nasal prongs are the preferred method for delivering oxygen, with a flow rate

### Cyanosis Or Breathing Difficulty — phần 4

of 0.5–1 L per minute, increased to 2 L per minute in severe respiratory distress to achieve oxygen saturation greater than 90% but less than 95%.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Thick secretions should be cleared from the throat by intermittent suction under

direct observation, if they are obstructing the airway and the infant is too weak to clear them. Note: Indiscriminate use of supplemental oxygen for premature infants has been associated with the risk of blindness and chronic respiratory problems (bronchopulmonary dysplasia).

## Low Birth Weight Or Preterm Baby

### Low Birth Weight Or Preterm Baby — phần 1

Newborns weighing 2000 g or less at birth should be provided kangaroo mother care as close to continuously as possible. Kangaroo mother care should be initiated in health facilities as soon as the newborns are clinically stable. The key features of kangaroo mother care for preterm infants are early, continuous and prolonged skin-to-skin contact between the mother and the baby, and exclusive breastfeeding (ideally) or feeding with breastmilk.

Unstable neonates weighing 2000 g or less at birth, or stable newborns weighing less than 2000 g, who cannot be given kangaroo mother care should be cared for in a thermoneutral environment, either under radiant warmers or in incubators.

## Very Low Birth Weight Or Very Preterm Baby

### Very Low Birth Weight Or Very Preterm Baby — phần 1

If a baby is very small (less than 1500 g or less than 32 weeks), severe health problems are likely and include difficulty in breathing, inability to

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-177

feed, severe jaundice and infection. Without special thermal protection (e.g. an incubator), the baby is susceptible to hypothermia. Very small newborns require special care. They should be transferred to the appropriate service for the care of sick and small babies as early as possible. Before and during transfer:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is kept warm. The baby can be transferred in

skin-to-skin contact with the mother.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the baby with a diaper, a hat and socks (which is enough when

### Very Low Birth Weight Or Very Preterm Baby — phần 2

the ambient temperature is 22–24°C) safely in skin-to-skin contact in an upright position between the mother’s breasts—chest to chest, with the infant’s head turned to one side. Tie the infant to the mother with a cloth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If skin-to-skin contact with the mother is not possible:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; transfer the baby in skin-to-skin contact with the father, a relative

or a willing health care provider; OR

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth; cover with a blanket and ensure

that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to begin breastfeeding or provide alternative

breastmilk feeding to prevent hypoglycaemia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If maternal history indicates possible bacterial infection, give a

first dose of antibiotics:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; gentamicin 3 mg/kg body weight IM;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS ampicillin 50 mg/kg body weight IM.

### Very Low Birth Weight Or Very Preterm Baby — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty breathing (less

than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, indrawing of the chest, or grunting), give oxygen by nasal catheter or prongs (page S-174).

## Moderately Preterm Or Low Birth Weight Baby

### Moderately Preterm Or Low Birth Weight Baby — phần 1

Moderately preterm (32–37 weeks) or low birth weight (1500–2500 g) babies may start to develop problems soon after birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has no breathing difficulty and remains adequately

warm while in skin-to-skin contact with the mother, the father or a relative:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; keep the baby with the mother, father or relative; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-178; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; if possible, encourage the mother to initiate breastfeeding within

the first hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty breathing

(less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, indrawing of the chest, or grunting), give the baby oxygen by nasal catheter or prongs (page S-174) and manage as for cyanosis or difficulty breathing (page S-174).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s axillary temperature drops below 36.5°C, rewarm the

baby (page S-179).

### Moderately Preterm Or Low Birth Weight Baby — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby develops problems, transfer the baby to the appropriate

service for the care of sick newborns as quickly as possible. The baby can be transferred in skin-to-skin contact with the mother, the father or a relative.

## Lethargy

### Lethargy — phần 1

If the baby is lethargic (low muscular tone, drowsy, does not move spontaneously or when stimulated), it is very likely that the baby has a severe illness and should be transferred to the appropriate service for the care of sick of newborns. Before transfer:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open and maintain the baby’s airway. Give oxygen by nasal prongs if

the newborn is cyanosed, in severe respiratory distress or hypoxaemic (oxygen saturation of 90% or less).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give bag and mask ventilation (page S-170) with oxygen (or room air

if oxygen is not available) if there is apnoea or gasping or if the baby’s respiratory rate is too slow (less than 20 breaths per minute).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give ampicillin and gentamicin (see S-166).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is drowsy or unconscious, check blood glucose and treat

### Lethargy — phần 2

the baby for hypoglycaemia if present. If you cannot check blood glucose quickly, assume that the baby has hypoglycaemia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the baby vitamin K (if not given before).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the infant frequently until transfer to specialized care for sick

newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-179

## Hypothermia

### Hypothermia — phần 1

Hypothermia can occur quickly in a very small baby, a baby who was not dried immediately after birth, or a baby who was resuscitated or separated from the mother. In these cases, the baby’s temperature can quickly drop below normal. Rewarm the baby as soon as possible:

## Severe Hypothermia

### Severe Hypothermia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is very sick or is severely hypothermic (axillary

temperature less than 32°C):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby as quickly as possible to the appropriate service

for the care of preterm or sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby is cyanotic (bluish) or is having difficulty breathing

(less than 30 or more than 60 breaths per minute, indrawing of the chest, or grunting), treat for breathing difficulty (page S-174).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Before transfer:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Remove cold or wet clothing, if present, and thoroughly dry the

baby. Dress the baby in warm clothes and a hat, and cover with a warm blanket.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Warm the baby immediately using available methods (e.g.

incubator, radiant heater, warm room, heated bed, warm clothes, warm blankets, skin-to-skin contact).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the first dose of antibiotics for sepsis, and keep the tubing of

### Severe Hypothermia — phần 2

the IV line under the radiant warmer to warm the fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check blood glucose level to rule out hypoglycaemia and treat

hypoglycaemia if present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby shows signs of readiness to suckle, allow the baby to

begin breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breastmilk using an

alternative feeding method.

## Moderate Hypothermia

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has moderate hypothermia (temperature higher than

32°C and less than 36.5°C):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ensure that the baby is kept warm; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; remove cold or wet clothing, if present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-180; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is present, have her rewarm the baby using skin-to-skin

contact if the baby does not have other problems.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother is not present or skin-to-skin contact cannot be used:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth; cover with a blanket and

ensure that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place the baby in an incubator or under a radiant heater (after

covering the baby’s body and head). Use another method of rewarming, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the mother to begin breastfeeding as soon as the baby is

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 2

ready. If the baby cannot be breastfed, give expressed breastmilk using an alternative feeding method.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check blood glucose level to rule out hypoglycaemia and treat

hypoglycaemia if present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s respiratory rate is more than 60 breaths per

minute or the baby has chest indrawing or grunting on expiration, treat for breathing difficulty (page S-174).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the baby’s axillary temperature hourly until normal (or for at

least three hours):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature has increased at least 0.5°C per

hour over the last three hours, rewarming has been successful; continue measuring the baby’s temperature every two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature does not rise or is rising more slowly

### Moderate Hypothermia — phần 3

than 0.5°C per hour, look for signs of sepsis (e.g. poor feeding, vomiting, breathing difficulty; and transfer the baby as quickly as possible to the appropriate service for the care of preterm or sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Once the baby’s temperature is normal, measure the temperature

every three hours for 12 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the baby’s temperature remains within the normal range,

discontinue measurements.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is feeding well and there are no other problems requiring

hospitalization, discharge the baby. Advise the mother on how to keep the baby warm at home.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-181

## Convulsions

### Convulsions — phần 1

Convulsions in the first hour of life are rare. Convulsions can be due to asphyxia, birth injury, hypoglycaemia or hypocalcaemia, and are also a sign of meningitis or neurologic problems (e.g. hypoxic-ischaemic encephalopathy, intracranial haemorrhage). Note: Treat clinically apparent seizures with phenobarbital, if the seizures last longer than three minutes or if they are brief serial seizures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is kept warm. Wrap the baby in a soft, dry cloth;

cover with a blanket and ensure that the baby’s head is covered to prevent heat loss.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfer the baby to the appropriate service for the care of sick

newborns as quickly as possible. The initial management of convulsions includes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the baby’s blood glucose level to rule out hypoglycaemia and

### Convulsions — phần 2

treat hypoglycaemia, if present, before antiepileptic medication treatment is considered. If facilities for measuring glucose are not available, consider empirical treatment with glucose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are clinical signs suggestive of associated sepsis or meningitis,

central nervous system infection should be ruled out by doing a lumbar puncture. If present, the infection should be treated with appropriate antibiotics. Note: If facilities for lumbar puncture are not available, consider empirical antibiotic treatment with ampicillin and gentamicin IV for a neonate with clinical signs of sepsis or meningitis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Measure serum calcium (if facilities are available) and treat, if

hypocalcaemia is present.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In the absence of hypoglycaemia, meningitis, hypocalcaemia or

### Convulsions — phần 3

another obvious underlying etiology such as hypoxic-ischaemic encephalopathy or intracranial haemorrhage or infarction, pyridoxine treatment in a specialized centre, where this treatment is available, may be considered before antiepileptic medication treatment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is currently having a convulsion or has had a

convulsion within the last hour, give the baby a loading dose of phenobarbital 20 mg/kg body weight IV over 15 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If an IV line has not yet been established, give phenobarbital 20

mg/kg body weight as a single IM injection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-182; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If convulsions do not stop within 30 minutes, give another dose

of phenobarbital 10 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over five minutes (or IM if an IV line still has not been established). Repeat one more time after another 30 minutes, if necessary.

### Convulsions — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In neonates who continue to have seizures despite administering

the maximal tolerated dose of phenobarbital (IV up to a maximum of 40 mg/kg body weight), either midazolam or lidocaine may be used as the second-line agent for control of seizures. (Note that the use of lidocaine requires cardiac monitoring facilities.)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Watch for apnoea and always have a bag and mask available in case

ventilation is required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby has central cyanosis (blue tongue and lips) or other

signs of breathing difficulty, treat for breathing difficulty (page S-174).

Note: Do not use diazepam for convulsions. Diazepam given in addition to phenobarbital will increase the risk of circulatory collapse and respiratory failure.

## To Infection

### To Infection — phần 1

The following suggested guidelines may be modified according to local situations:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a neonate has risk factors for infection (i.e. preterm prelabour

rupture of membranes; membranes ruptured more than 18 hours before birth; mother had fever higher than 38°C before childbirth or during labour; amniotic fluid was foul smelling or purulent; or mother has documented colonization with Group B streptococcus), the following steps should be taken, even if the mother has no clinical signs of infection:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep the baby with the mother and encourage the mother to

continue breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make arrangements with the appropriate service that cares for sick

newborns to take a blood culture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Treat the newborn with prophylactic antibiotics: ampicillin (IM or

IV) and gentamicin for at least two days.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-183

### To Infection — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Transfer the baby for further management to the appropriate

service that cares for sick newborns.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If none of the risk factors listed applies, do not treat with antibiotics.

Observe the baby for signs of infection for three days:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Keep the baby with the mother and encourage her to continue

breastfeeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If signs of infection occur within three days, make

arrangements with the appropriate service that cares for sick newborns to take a blood culture and start the newborn on antibiotics.

## Maternal-To-Child Transmission Of Syphilis

### Maternal-To-Child Transmission Of Syphilis — phần 1

Syphilis infection in a pregnant woman can lead to adverse outcomes such as early fetal loss, stillbirth, neonatal death, prematurity, low birth weight and clinical evidence of syphilis in the neonate. To prevent maternal-to- child transmission of syphilis:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; All pregnant women and their partners should be screened and, where

indicated, treated for syphilis at the first antenatal visit, preferably before 16 weeks’ gestation, and again in late pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a pregnant woman was not tested during pregnancy, efforts should

be made to test for syphilis during labour or the immediate postpartum period, before she is discharged.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother has a positive serologic test for syphilis (at any time

during pregnancy or the intrapartum period), treat the newborn regardless of whether the mother was fully or partially treated and whether the newborn or mother has signs of syphilis:

### Maternal-To-Child Transmission Of Syphilis — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the baby 37.5 mg/kg body weight (50 000 U/kg body

weight) of benzathine benzylpenicillin in a single IM dose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the mother was not treated for syphilis or she was treated

inadequately, or if her treatment status is unknown or uncertain:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give the mother and her partner(s) benzathine benzylpenicillin

1.8g IM as two injections at separate sites.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Refer the mother and her partner(s) for follow-up to a clinic that

offers services for sexually transmitted infections.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inform the mother of the importance of treatment for her, her newborn

and her partner.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-184; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up in four weeks to examine the baby for growth and signs of

congenital syphilis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Report the case to authorities, if required.

### Maternal-To-Child Transmission Of Syphilis — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the newborn shows signs of syphilis (Box S-14), administer the

first dose of antibiotics and transfer the baby to the appropriate service for the care of sick newborns (hospitalization is often indicated to ensure that the infant receives the full course of treatment):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; procaine benzylpenicillin 50 mg/kg body weight as a single dose

by deep IM injection daily for 10 days;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; OR benzylpenicillin 30 mg/kg body weight every 12 hours IV for

the first seven days of life and then 30 mg/kg body weight every eight hours IV for three more days. Note: Any suspected case of congenital syphilis should be confirmed by testing

- Dòng dữ liệu: BOX S-14.; Signs of congenital syphilis

Signs of syphilis include the following:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; low birth weight

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; palms of hands and soles of feet with red rash, grey patches, blisters or

peeling skin

### Maternal-To-Child Transmission Of Syphilis — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; “snuffles”: highly infectious rhinitis with nasal obstruction

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; abdominal distension due to enlarged liver and/or spleen

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; jaundice

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; pallor, anaemia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; generalized oedema

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; anal condylomata

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; paralysis of one limb

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; spirochetes seen on dark field examination of lesion, body fluid or

cerebrospinal fluid Some very low birth weight infants with syphilis have signs of severe sepsis with lethargy, respiratory distress, skin petechiae or other bleeding.

## Infants Of Mothers With Tuberculosis

### Infants Of Mothers With Tuberculosis — phần 1

If the mother has active lung tuberculosis and was on treatment for less than two months before giving birth, or if tuberculosis was diagnosed after the birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: Immediate newborn conditions or problems; S-185

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reassure the mother that it is safe for her to breastfeed her infant.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not give the tuberculosis vaccine (BCG) at birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give prophylactic isoniazid at 10 mg/kg body weight by mouth once

daily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure follow-up of the infant with the appropriate service for the care

of sick newborns.

## Infants Of Mothers With Hiv Infection

### Infants Of Mothers With Hiv Infection — phần 1

Refer to national guidelines.

- Dòng dữ liệu: S-186; Immediate newborn conditions or problems

## Paracervical Block P-1

### Paracervical Block P-1 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-1.; Indications and precautions for paracervical block

- Dòng dữ liệu: Indications; Precautions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Dilatation and curettage; • Make sure there are no known allergies

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Manual vacuum aspiration; to lidocaine or related drugs.

• Do not inject into a vessel. • Maternal complications are rare but may include haematoma.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare 20 mL 0.5% lidocaine solution without adrenaline

(page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a 3.5-cm, 22-gauge or 25-gauge needle to inject the lidocaine

solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a tenaculum to grasp the cervix, first inject 1 mL of 0.5%

### Paracervical Block P-1 — phần 2

lidocaine solution into the anterior or posterior lip of the cervix that has been exposed by the speculum. Note: With incomplete abortion, a ring (sponge) forceps is preferable, as it is less likely than the tenaculum to tear the cervix with traction and does not require the use of lidocaine for placement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the tenaculum or ring forceps on the cervix vertically (one tooth

in the external os, the other on the face of the cervix), use slight traction and movement to help identify the area between the smooth cervical epithelium and the vaginal tissue. This is the site for insertion of the needle around the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the needle just under the epithelium.

### Paracervical Block P-1 — phần 3

Tip: Some practitioners have suggested the following step to divert the woman’s attention from the insertion of the needle: Place the tip of the needle just over the site selected for insertion and ask the woman to cough. This will “pop” the needle just under the surface of the tissue. Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove

the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-2; Paracervical block

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 2 mL of lidocaine solution just under the epithelium, not deeper

than 3 mm, at 3, 5, 7 and 9 o’clock (Fig. P-1). Optional injection sites are at 2 and 10 o’clock. When correctly placed, a swelling and blanching of the tissue can be noted.

### Paracervical Block P-1 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the cervix with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-1.; Paracervical block injection sites

Optional injection sites Injection sites

## Pudendal Block P-3

### Pudendal Block P-3 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-2.; Indications and precautions for pudendal block

- Dòng dữ liệu: Indications; Precautions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Instrumental or breech birth; • Make sure there are no known allergies

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Episiotomy and repair of perineal tears; to lidocaine or related drugs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Craniotomy or craniocentesis; • Do not inject into a vessel.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare 40 mL 0.5% lidocaine solution without adrenaline

(page C-57). Note: It is best to limit the pudendal block to 30 mL of solution so that a maximum of 10 mL of additional solution may be injected into the perineum during repair of tears, if needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a 15-cm, 22-gauge needle to inject the lidocaine.

The target is the pudendal nerve as it passes through the lesser sciatic notch. There are two approaches:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; through the perineum

### Pudendal Block P-3 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; through the vagina.

The perineal approach requires no special instrument. For the vaginal approach, a special needle guide (“trumpet”), if available, provides protection for the provider’s fingers.

## Perineal Approach

### Perineal Approach — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infiltrate the perineal skin on both sides of the vagina using 10 mL of

lidocaine solution. Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, place two fingers in the vagina and guide the

needle through the perineal tissue to the tip of the woman’s left ischial spine (Fig. P-2, page P-4).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-4; Pudendal block

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-2.; Perineal approach

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 10 mL of lidocaine solution in the angle between the ischial

spine and the ischial tuberosity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pass the needle through the sacrospinous ligament and inject another

### Perineal Approach — phần 2

10 mL of lidocaine solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repeat the procedure on the opposite side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an episiotomy is to be performed, infiltrate the episiotomy site in

the usual manner at this time (page P-85).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the area with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Pudendal block; P-5

## Vaginal Approach

### Vaginal Approach — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, use the left index finger to palpate the

woman’s left ischial spine through the vaginal wall (Fig. P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-3.; Vaginal approach without a needle guide

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the right hand to advance the needle guide (“trumpet”) towards

the left spine, keeping the left fingertip at the end of the needle guide.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the needle guide just below the tip of the ischial spine.

Remember to keep the fingertip near the end of the needle guide. Do not place the fingertip beyond the end of the needle guide as needle-stick injury can easily occur.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advance a 15-cm, 22-gauge needle with attached syringe through the

guide.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Penetrate the vaginal mucosa until the needle pierces the sacrospinous

### Vaginal Approach — phần 2

ligament. Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-6; Pudendal block

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 10 mL of lidocaine solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Withdraw the needle into the guide and reposition the guide to just

above the ischial spine.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Penetrate the vaginal mucosa and aspirate again to be sure that no

vessel has been penetrated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject another 5 mL of lidocaine solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repeat the procedure on the other side, using the right index finger to

palpate the woman’s right ischial spine. Use the left hand to advance the needle and needle guide, and inject the lidocaine solution.

### Vaginal Approach — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an episiotomy is to be performed, infiltrate the episiotomy site in

the usual manner at this time (page P-85).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the area with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

## Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7

### Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7 — phần 1

Local anaesthesia is a safe alternative to general, ketamine or spinal anaesthesia when these anaesthetics or persons trained in their use are not available. The use of local anaesthesia for caesarean requires that the provider counsel the woman and reassure her throughout the procedure. The provider must keep in mind that the woman is awake and alert, and should use instruments and handle tissue as gently as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-3.; Indications and precautions for local anaesthesia for

caesarean

- Dòng dữ liệu: Indications; Precautions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Caesarean (especially in women with; • Avoid use in women with eclampsia,

- Dòng dữ liệu: heart failure); severe pre-eclampsia or previous

laparotomy. • Avoid use in women who are obese, apprehensive, or allergic to lidocaine or related drugs. • Avoid use if the provider has little experience in performing caesareans. • Do not inject lidocaine into a vessel.

### Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare 200 mL of 0.5% lidocaine solution with 1:200 000 adrenaline

(page C-58). Usually less than half this volume (approximately 80 mL) is needed in the first hour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is alive:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Wait to give morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM and

promethazine 25 mg IV to the woman until after birth of the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, morphine and promethazine can be given to the

woman before birth, but the baby might need to be given naloxone 0.1 mg/kg body weight IV at birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the fetus is dead, give morphine 0.1 mg/kg body weight IM and

promethazine 25 mg IV to the woman before infiltrating the skin and subcutaneous tissue with local anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-8; Local anaesthesia for caesarean birth

### Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7 — phần 3

Talk to the woman and reassure her throughout the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Using a 10-cm needle, infiltrate one band of skin and subcutaneous

tissue on either side of the proposed incision, two finger breadths apart (Fig. P-4). Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

FIGURE P-4. Infiltration of skin and subcutaneous tissue with local anaesthesia for caesarean

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Raise a long wheal of lidocaine solution 3–4 cm on either side of the

midline from the symphysis pubis to a point 5 cm above the umbilicus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infiltrate the lidocaine solution down through the layers of the

### Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7 — phần 4

abdominal wall. The needle should remain almost parallel to the skin. Take care not to pierce the peritoneum and insert the needle into the uterus, as the abdominal wall is very thin at term.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the incision site with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

Note: If the caesarean is performed under local anaesthesia, make a midline incision that is about 4 cm longer than when general anaesthesia is

- Dòng dữ liệu: Local anaesthesia for caesarean birth; P-9

used. A Pfannenstiel’s incision should not be used because it takes longer, retraction is poorer and more local anaesthetic is needed.

The anaesthetic effect can be expected to last about 60 minutes.

Proceed with caesarean (page P-53), keeping the following in mind:

### Local Anaesthesia For Caesarean Birth P-7 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Do not use abdominal packs. Use retractors as little as possible and

with a minimum of force.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 30 mL of lidocaine solution beneath the uterovesical peritoneum

as far laterally as the round ligaments. No additional anaesthetic is required. The peritoneum is sensitive to pain; the myometrium is not.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inform the woman that she will feel some discomfort from traction

when the baby is born. This is usually no more than occurs during vaginal birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the uterus without removing it from the abdomen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Additional local anaesthesia may be necessary to repair the abdominal

wall.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-10; Local anaesthesia for caesarean birth

## Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11

### Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-4.; Indications and precautions for spinal anaesthesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: Indications; Precautions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Caesarean; • Make sure there are no known allergies to

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Laparotomy; lidocaine or related drugs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Repair of third and fourth; • Avoid use in women with uncorrected

- Dòng dữ liệu: degree perineal tears; hypovolaemia, severe anaemia, coagulation

disorders, haemorrhage, local infection, severe pre-eclampsia, eclampsia or heart failure due to heart disease.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse 500–1000 mL of IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate) to

preload the woman and avoid hypotension. This should be done 30 minutes before anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare 1.5 mL of the local anaesthetic: 5% lidocaine in 5% dextrose.

### Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11 — phần 2

Add 0.25 mL of adrenaline (1:1000) if the anaesthetic needs to be effective for longer than 45 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman to lie on her side (or sit up), ensuring that the lumbar

spine is well flexed. Ask the woman to flex her head onto her chest and round her back as much as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify and, if required, mark the proposed site of injection. A vertical

line from the iliac crest upward will cross the woman’s vertebral column between the spines of the fourth and fifth lumbar vertebrae. Choose this space or the space just above it.

Sterility is critical. Do not touch the point or shaft of the spinal needle with your hand. Hold the needle only by its hub.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 1% lidocaine solution using a fine needle to anaesthetize the

woman’s skin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Introduce the finest spinal needle available (22- or 23-gauge) in the

### Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11 — phần 3

midline through the wheal, at a right angle to the skin in the vertical plane. Note: Fine needles tend to bend.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the needle hits bone, it might not be in the midline. Withdraw the

needle and reinsert it, directing it slightly upwards while aiming for the woman’s umbilicus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-12; Spinal (subarachnoid) anaesthesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advance the spinal needle towards the subarachnoid space. A distinct

loss of resistance will be felt as the needle pierces the ligamentum flavum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the needle is through the ligamentum flavum, push the needle

slowly through the dura. There will be another slight loss of resistance as the dura is pierced.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the stylet. Cerebrospinal fluid should flow out of the needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If cerebrospinal fluid does not come out, reinsert the stylet and

### Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11 — phần 4

rotate the needle gently. Remove the stylet to see if the fluid is flowing out. If this fails two times, try another space.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inject 1–1.25 mL of the local anaesthetic solution. For pregnant

women who have not given birth, a smaller dose of the drug is needed because the available subarachnoid space is reduced due to engorged epidural veins.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Help the woman lie on her back. Have the operating table tilted to the

left or place a pillow or folded linen under her right lower back to decrease supine hypotension syndrome.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Recheck the woman’s blood pressure. A fall in blood pressure is

likely. If there is significant hypotension, give the woman more IV fluids (500 mL quickly):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If this does not raise her blood pressure, give ephedrine

0.2 mg/kg body weight IV in 3 mg increments.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If blood pressure continues to fall after giving IV ephedrine

### Spinal (Subarachnoid) Anaesthesia P-11 — phần 5

boluses four times, give ephedrine 30 mg IM.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxygen at 6–8 L per minute by mask or nasal cannulae.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After injecting the local anaesthetic solution, wait two minutes and

then pinch the incision site with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After surgery, keep the woman flat for at least six hours, with only a

single pillow beneath her head to prevent post-spinal headache. She must not sit up or strain during this period.

## Ketamine P-13

### Ketamine P-13 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-5.; Indications and precautions for ketamine anaesthesia

- Dòng dữ liệu: Indications; Precautions

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Any procedure that is relatively short; • When used alone, ketamine can cause

- Dòng dữ liệu: (less than 60 minutes) and for which; unpleasant hallucinations. Avoid use in

- Dòng dữ liệu: muscle relaxation is not required (e.g.; women with a history of psychosis. To

- Dòng dữ liệu: repair of perineal tears or extensive; prevent hallucinations, give the woman

- Dòng dữ liệu: cervical tears, manual removal of; diazepam 10 mg IV after the baby is

- Dòng dữ liệu: placenta, caesarean, drainage of breast; born.

- Dòng dữ liệu: abscess); • By itself, ketamine does not provide

- Dòng dữ liệu: • Suitable as a back-up if inhalation; muscular relaxation, so the incision for

- Dòng dữ liệu: apparatus (or gas supply for a Boyle’s; caesarean might need to be longer.

### Ketamine P-13 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: anaesthesia machine) fails or if general; • Ketamine should not be used in

- Dòng dữ liệu: anaesthesia is used without an; women with elevated blood pressure,

- Dòng dữ liệu: inhalation apparatus; pre-eclampsia, eclampsia or heart

disease.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ketamine may be given IM or IV or by infusion. The dose of ketamine

is variable:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Most women will require 6–10 mg/kg body weight IM. Surgical

anaesthesia is reached within 10 minutes and lasts up to 30 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Alternatively, give 2 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over two

minutes (in which case the action lasts for only 15 minutes).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Infusion of ketamine is described below. This is suitable for

caesarean.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When additional pain relief is needed, give ketamine 1 mg/kg

body weight IV.

### Ketamine P-13 — phần 3

Ketamine anaesthesia should not be used in women with elevated blood pressure, pre-eclampsia, eclampsia or heart disease.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-14; Ketamine

## Premedication

### Premedication — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman atropine sulfate 0.6 mg IM 30 minutes prior to

surgery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman diazepam 10 mg IV at the time of induction to

prevent hallucinations (for caesarean, give diazepam after the baby is born).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman oxygen at 6–8 L per minute by mask or nasal

cannulae.

## Induction And Maintenance

### Induction And Maintenance — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the woman’s vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration,

temperature).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a mouth gag to prevent airway obstruction by the tongue.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Induction of anaesthesia is achieved by administering ketamine

2 mg/kg body weight IV slowly over two minutes. For short procedures lasting less than 15 minutes, this will provide adequate anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For longer procedures, infuse ketamine 200 mg in 1 L dextrose at

2 mg per minute (i.e. 20 drops per minute).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the level of anaesthesia before proceeding with the surgery.

Pinch the incision site with forceps. If the woman can feel the pinch, wait two minutes and then retest.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration,

temperature) every 10 minutes during the procedure.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discontinue ketamine infusion and administer a

postoperative analgesic suited to the type of surgery performed (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Maintain observations every 30 minutes until the woman is

fully awake; ketamine anaesthesia can take up to 60 minutes to wear off.

## External Version P-15

### External Version P-15 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications. Do not perform this procedure before

37 weeks or if facilities for emergency caesarean are not available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an ultrasound machine is available, perform an examination to

confirm breech position, determine amniotic fluid index, note placental location, and rule out congenital anomalies and the presence of a nuchal cord.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the woman lie on her back, and elevate the foot of the bed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Listen to and note the fetal heart rate. If there are fetal heart rate

abnormalities (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; do not proceed with external version;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; manage as for fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Coat the abdomen liberally with ultrasonic gel or oil to decrease

friction and lessen the chances of an overly vigorous manipulation.

### External Version P-15 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Palpate the abdomen to confirm the presentation and position of the

fetal head, back and hips.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To mobilize the breech, gently lift the lowest part of the fetus from the

pelvic inlet by grasping above the pubic bone (Fig. P-5 A, page P-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Bring the head and buttocks of the fetus closer to each other to achieve

forward rotation. Rotate the fetus slowly by guiding the head in a forward roll as the buttocks are lifted (Fig. P-5 B–C, page P-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Listen to the fetal heart rate after every attempt at external version. If

an abnormal fetal heart rate is detected:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; manage as for fetal distress (page S-109); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; reassess every 15 minutes.

If the fetal heart rate does not stabilize within the next 30 minutes, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

### External Version P-15 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the procedure is successful, have the woman remain lying down for

15 minutes. If there are no fetal heart rate abnormalities, discharge the woman. Counsel her to return to the facility for routine antenatal care or if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; bleeding or pain occurs; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; she believes the baby has returned to the previous presentation; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; she believes she is in labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the procedure is unsuccessful, try again using a backward roll

(Fig. P-5 D).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-16; External version

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the procedure is still unsuccessful and the fetal heart rate is

good, tocolytics (page S-146) might increase the chances of successful version.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the procedure is still unsuccessful, attempt version again after one

week or if the woman presents in early labour with breech or transverse lie.

### External Version P-15 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-5.; External version of a breech presentation

## Induction And Augmentation Of Labour P-17

### Induction And Augmentation Of Labour P-17 — phần 1

Induction and augmentation of labour are performed for different indications, but some of the methods used are the same regardless of the indication. They should be performed only when there is a clear medical or obstetrical indication and when the expected benefits outweigh the potential harms of uterine hyperstimulation, uterine rupture and fetal distress.

Wherever possible, induction and augmentation of labour should be carried out in facilities where caesarean can be performed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Induction of labour: artificially stimulating the uterus to start labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Augmentation of labour: stimulating the uterus during labour to

increase the frequency, duration and intensity of contractions

Whenever induction or augmentation of labour is carried out:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; facilities should be available for assessing maternal and fetal well-

being;

### Induction And Augmentation Of Labour P-17 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; personnel who are familiar with the effects of uterotonic drugs and

are able to identify and manage both maternal and fetal complications should be present during administration;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; the facility should have the capacity to manage potential adverse

effects and failure to achieve vaginal birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; supportive care should be provided continuously (pages C-86–88);

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; women receiving oxytocin, misoprostol or other prostaglandins

should never be left unattended;

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; if oxytocin is used for induction of labour, the infusion rate of

oxytocin should be monitored; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; betamimetics should be available in case of uterine

hyperstimulation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-18; Induction and augmentation of labour

## Labour

### Labour — phần 1

Note: Before beginning induction/augmentation, ensure that staff and essential equipment are available to adequately monitor the woman, the fetus, contractions and the progress of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record maternal and fetal status every 30 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the number of contractions in a 10-minute period

and their duration in seconds every 30 minutes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the progress of labour by:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; measuring changes in cervical effacement and dilatation

(Fig. C-4 A–E, page C-81) during the latent phase;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; measuring the rate of cervical dilatation and fetal descent

(Fig. C-13, page C-96, and Fig. C-3, page C-80) during the active phase and recording the findings on the partograph;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; assessing further fetal descent during the second stage of labour.

### Labour — phần 2

A vaginal examination should be carried out once every four hours, after obtaining informed consent and permission from the woman, unless there is a clinical indication to perform an exam more frequently.

## Indications

### Indications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women who are known with certainty to have reached 41 weeks

(40 weeks plus seven days or more) of gestation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with prelabour rupture of membranes at term (gestational

age of 36 weeks plus seven days or more);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with severe pre-eclampsia at a gestational age when the

fetus is not viable or unlikely to achieve viability within one or two weeks;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with pre-eclampsia or gestational hypertension at term, or

earlier as clinically indicated (page S-50);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with vaginal bleeding at term, or earlier as clinically

indicated (page S-21);

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-19

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with chorioamnionitis at term, or earlier as clinically

indicated (page S-146);

### Indications — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with fetal growth restriction at term, or earlier as clinically

indicated (page S-156);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; women with a dead or anomalous fetus (page S-156).

Note: Induction of labour is not recommended for women with an uncomplicated pregnancy and gestational age of less than 41 weeks. If gestational diabetes is the only abnormality, but it is well controlled, do not induce labour before 41 weeks of gestation. If suspected fetal macrosomia at term is the only indication, do not induce labour.

Women and, if they desire, their support persons should be counselled about induction to assist them in making an informed choice. Prior to induction of labour, the wishes and preferences of each woman should be considered.

## Assessment Of The Cervix

### Assessment Of The Cervix — phần 1

The success of induction of labour is related to the condition of the cervix at the start of induction. To assess the condition of the cervix, perform a cervical examination and assign a score based on the criteria in Table P-6.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-6.; Assessment of cervix for induction of labour

Rating

- Dòng dữ liệu: Factor; 0; 1; 2; 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: Dilatation (cm); closed; 1–2; 3–4; more than 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: Length of cervix (cm) more than 4; 3–4; 1–2; less than 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: Consistency; firm; average; soft; N/A

- Dòng dữ liệu: Position; posterior; mid; anterior; N/A

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is favourable (has a score of 6 or more), labour is usually

successfully induced with oxytocin alone.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cervix is unfavourable (has a score of 5 or less), ripen the

cervix using prostaglandins (page P-21) such as misoprostol, a Foley or balloon catheter (page P-22), or oxytocin (page P-22).

### Assessment Of The Cervix — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-20; Induction and augmentation of labour

## Sweeping Membranes

### Sweeping Membranes — phần 1

Note: Sweeping membranes could reduce the need for formal induction of labour. It is suitable for non-urgent indications for pregnancy termination.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain the purpose of the procedure to the woman and what will

happen, including that it can cause discomfort and bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

Note: In areas where HIV and/or hepatitis are highly prevalent, it is prudent to leave the membranes intact for as long as possible to reduce perinatal transmission.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Listen to and note the fetal heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman to lie on her back, displacing the uterus by performing

a left lateral tilt (e.g. rolled blanket under right hip), with her legs bent, feet together and knees apart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, use one hand to assess the cervix and note the

### Sweeping Membranes — phần 2

consistency, position and effacement, and dilatation (Table P-6, page P-19).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Using the examining hand, rotate one finger against the wall of the

uterus beyond the internal cervical os, taking care to avoid rupture of membranes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After membrane sweeping, listen to the fetal heart rate during and after

a contraction. If the fetal heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If fetal distress is not suspected and the woman is not in labour,

inform the woman, sensitively, that she is not in labour and can go home:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Advise her to watch for signs of labour (page C-77).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Advise her to return for assessment if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; she has spontaneous rupture of membranes;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; she has bleeding;

### Sweeping Membranes — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; no labour is established by 41 completed weeks’ gestation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; she is experiencing problems; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; as clinically indicated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-21

## Prostaglandins

### Prostaglandins — phần 1

Prostaglandins are highly effective in ripening the cervix during induction of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check and record the woman’s pulse, blood pressure and contractions,

and the fetal heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

Monitor uterine contractions and the fetal heart rate of all women undergoing induction of labour with prostaglandins.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; There are many prostaglandin preparations available:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prostaglandin E2 is available in several forms (3 mg pessary or

2–3 mg gel). The prostaglandin is placed high in the posterior fornix of the vagina and may be repeated after six hours if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Misoprostol is a synthetic analogue of prostaglandin E1. It is

highly effective in cervical ripening during induction of labour.

### Prostaglandins — phần 2

Misoprostol should not be used for women with previous caesarean, who are at increased risk of uterine rupture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-7.; Possible routes of misoprostol administration

- Dòng dữ liệu: Route of; If required,

Dose

- Dòng dữ liệu: administration; repeat after

25 mcg (dissolve one 200 mcg tablet in 200 mL of

- Dòng dữ liệu: Oral; water and administer 25 mL of that solution as a; 2 hours

single dose) 25 mcg (only if misoprostol is available in the form

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vaginal; of a 25 mcg tablet)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Place into the; 6 hours

- Dòng dữ liệu: posterior fornix; Do not divide or cut a 200 mcg tablet into smaller

pieces, as this is inaccurate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman, the fetus, contractions, and progress of labour

(page P-18).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discontinue prostaglandins and begin oxytocin infusion if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-22; Induction and augmentation of labour

### Prostaglandins — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; membranes rupture;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervical ripening has been achieved; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; 12 hours have passed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If any contraction lasts longer than 60 seconds, or if there are

more than five contractions in 10 minutes, manage as hyperstimulation (page P-30). Note: If induction fails, provide the woman with support and information on management options: further attempt to induce labour (the timing should depend on the clinical situation and the woman’s wishes) or caesarean birth.

## Foley/Balloon Catheter

### Foley/Balloon Catheter — phần 1

A balloon catheter alone or in combination with oxytocin is recommended as an alternative method of induction of labour if prostaglandins are not available or are contraindicated. Compared to prostaglandins, a balloon catheter is associated with a lower risk of uterine hyperstimulation and uterine rupture, and it may therefore be preferred for women with a previous caesarean birth. It should, however, be avoided in women with obvious cervicitis or vaginitis.

The Foley catheter is an effective alternative to balloon catheters sold commercially for cervical ripening and labour induction.

If there is a history of bleeding or ruptured membranes or obvious vaginal infection, do not use a balloon or Foley catheter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check and record the woman’s pulse, blood pressure and contractions,

and the fetal heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

### Foley/Balloon Catheter — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently insert a high-level disinfected or sterile speculum into the

vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the catheter just below the tip with a high-level disinfected or

sterile forceps and gently introduce it through the cervix. Ensure that the inflatable bulb of the catheter is beyond the internal cervical os.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inflate the bulb with 30–50 mL of water, depending on the size of the

balloon.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-23

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Coil the rest of the catheter and place it into the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Leave the catheter in place for at least 12 hours, or until contractions

begin.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman, the fetus, contractions and progress of labour

(page P-18).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deflate the bulb before removing the catheter.

Note: Oxytocin infusion can be started with a balloon catheter in place or after it has been removed.

### Foley/Balloon Catheter — phần 3

The combination of balloon catheter plus oxytocin is recommended as an alternative method of induction of labour when prostaglandins (including misoprostol) are not available or are contraindicated.

## Oxytocin

### Oxytocin — phần 1

If prostaglandins are not available, intravenous oxytocin alone should be used for induction of labour.

Use oxytocin with great caution, as fetal distress can occur from hyperstimulation and, rarely, uterine rupture can occur. Multiparous women are at higher risk for uterine rupture.

Carefully observe women receiving oxytocin. Women receiving oxytocin should never be left alone.

The effective dose of oxytocin varies greatly among women. Cautiously administer oxytocin in IV fluids (dextrose or normal saline), gradually increasing the rate of infusion until good labour is established (three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds). The uterus should relax between contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check and record the woman’s pulse, blood pressure and contractions,

and the fetal heart rate.

Be sure induction is indicated, as failed induction is usually followed by caesarean birth.

### Oxytocin — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-24; Induction and augmentation of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is lying on her left side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor and record the following every 30 minutes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Rate of infusion of oxytocin (see below) (note: changes in arm

position may alter the flow rate).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Duration and frequency of contractions: If there are more than

five contractions in 10 minutes, or if any contraction lasts longer than 60 seconds, stop the infusion and manage as hyperstimulation (page P-30).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Maternal pulse: If the maternal pulse is rapid (110 or more) and

weak, perform a rapid evaluation of the general condition of the woman including vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration, temperature) (page C-1).

### Oxytocin — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fetal heart rate: Listen immediately after a contraction. If the fetal

heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), stop the infusion and manage for fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse oxytocin as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Infuse 2.5 units in 500 mL of dextrose (or normal saline) at

2.5 mIU per minute (i.e. 0.5 mL per minute or 10 drops per minute if the giving set has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL) (Table P-8, page P-26 and Table P-9, page P-27).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the infusion rate by 2.5 mIU per minute (i.e. 0.5 mL per

minute or 10 drops per minute if the giving set has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL) every 30 minutes until a good contraction pattern is established (three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If any contraction lasts longer than 60 seconds, or if there are

### Oxytocin — phần 4

more than five contractions in 10 minutes, stop the infusion and manage as hyperstimulation (page P-30).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once a good contraction pattern is established (three contractions in

10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds), maintain the rate; continue to monitor the woman’s pulse, blood pressure and contractions, and the fetal heart rate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute), stop the infusion and manage for fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-25

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are more than five contractions in 10 minutes, or if any

contraction lasts longer than 60 seconds, stop the infusion and manage as hyperstimulation (page P-30).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a good contraction pattern has not been established with the

### Oxytocin — phần 5

infusion rate at 15 mIU (i.e. 3 mL per minute or 60 drops per minute with a giving set that has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the oxytocin concentration to five units in 500 mL of

dextrose (or normal saline) (i.e. 10 mIU per mL) and adjust the infusion rate to 15 mIU per minute (1.5 mL per minute or 30 drops per minute if the giving set has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Increase the infusion rate by 5 mIU (i.e. 0.5 mL per minute or

10 drops per minute if the giving set has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL) every 30 minutes until a good contraction pattern is established or the maximum rate of 30 mIU per minute (i.e. 3 mL per minute or 60 drops per minute if the giving set has a drop factor of 20 drops/mL) is reached.

Do not use oxytocin 10 units in 500 mL (i.e. 20 mIU/mL) in multigravidae and women with a previous caesarean birth.

### Oxytocin — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a good contraction pattern still has not been established using the

higher concentration of oxytocin:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In multigravidae and in women with previous caesarean scars,

induction has failed; perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In a primigravida:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Infuse oxytocin at a higher concentration (10 units in

500 mL) according to the protocol in Table P-9, page P-27.

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If good contractions are not established at the maximum

dose, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-26; Induction and augmentation of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: TABLE P-8.; Oxytocin infusion rates for induction of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: Time; Drip; Total

- Dòng dữ liệu: Approximate; Volume

- Dòng dữ liệu: since; Oxytocin; Rate:; Volume

- Dòng dữ liệu: Dose (mIU/; Infused

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction; Concentration; Drops per; Infused

- Dòng dữ liệu: minute); (mL)

### Oxytocin — phần 7

- Dòng dữ liệu: (hours); Minute; (mL)

Note: Check the drop factor (drops per mL) for the giving set being used. If the drop factor is more or less than 20 drops per mL, the drip rates need to be recalculated.

5 units in 500 mL

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.00; dextrose or normal; 30; 15; 90; 315

saline (10 mIU/mL)

10 units in 500 mL

Increase the rate of oxytocin infusion only to the point where a good contraction pattern (three contractions in 10 minutes, each lasting more than 40 seconds) is established; then maintain the infusion at that rate but continue to monitor the woman and fetus and respond immediately if there are signs of hyperstimulation or fetal distress.

### 1.50 Same 60 15 68 135 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: 2.00; 5 units in 500 mL

- Dòng dữ liệu: dextrose or normal; 30; 15; 90; 225

saline (10 mIU/mL)

### 3.00 Same 60 30 68 338 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: 3.50; 10 units in 500 mL

saline (20 mIU/mL)

### 5.00 Same 60 60 90 630 — phần 1

## Augmentation Of Labour

### Augmentation Of Labour — phần 1

This procedure is used to shorten labour to prevent complications related to undue prolongation and to avert caesarean birth.

## Indications

### Indications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Findings are suggestive of unsatisfactory progress or delay of

labour (page C-97).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; False labour has been ruled out.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; There are no signs of cephalopelvic disproportion or obstruction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Inadequate contractions are the most likely cause of unsatisfactory

progress of labour.

Note: Use of augmentation prior to confirmation of delay of labour is not recommended.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; The following practices are not recommended:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; active management of labour for prevention of delay in labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the use of early amniotomy (artificial rupture of membranes) with

early oxytocin augmentation for prevention of delay in labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the use of oxytocin for prevention of delay in labour in women

receiving epidural analgesia;

### Indications — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the use of amniotomy alone for prevention of delay in labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the use of antispasmodic agents for prevention of delay in labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; pain relief for preventing delay and reducing the use of

augmentation in labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the use of intravenous fluids with the aim of shortening the

duration of labour (could cause maternal fluid overload); and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; administration of enema for reducing the use of labour

augmentation.

The use of oral misoprostol for labour augmentation is not recommended.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-29

## Augmentation Of Labour With Oxytocin

### Augmentation Of Labour With Oxytocin — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Diagnose the stage and phase of labour (page C-77).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse oxytocin as described for induction of labour (page P-23).

Note: Do not use rapid escalation for augmentation of labour.
