- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider performing an amniotomy (page P-29) together with

administering oxytocin for treatment of confirmed delay in labour. Do not perform amniotomy as a sole intervention for augmentation of labour, especially in settings with high HIV prevalence.

## Artificial Rupture Of Membranes/Amniotomy

### Artificial Rupture Of Membranes/Amniotomy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rupture of membranes, whether spontaneous or artificial, often sets

off the following chain of events:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Amniotic fluid is expelled.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Uterine volume is decreased.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Prostaglandins are produced, stimulating labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Uterine contractions begin (if the woman is not in labour) or

become stronger (if she is already in labour).

Amniotomy alone is not recommended either for induction of labour or for the treatment of delay in labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If membranes are intact, amniotomy can be considered for

augmentation in combination with oxytocin infusion. Note: In areas where HIV and/or hepatitis are highly prevalent, it is prudent to leave the membranes intact for as long as possible to reduce perinatal transmission.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Obtain oral informed consent from the woman before proceeding.

### Artificial Rupture Of Membranes/Amniotomy — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Listen to and note the fetal heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask the woman to lie on her back, displacing the uterus by performing

a left lateral tilt (e.g. rolled blanket under right hip), with her legs bent, feet together and knees apart.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-30; Induction and augmentation of labour

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, use one hand to assess the cervix and note the

consistency, position and effacement, and dilatation (Table P-6, page P-19).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the other hand to insert an amniotic hook or a Kocher clamp into

the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Guide the hook or clamp towards the membranes along the fingers in

the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place two fingers against the membranes and gently rupture the

membranes with the instrument in the other hand. Allow the amniotic fluid to drain slowly around the fingers.

### Artificial Rupture Of Membranes/Amniotomy — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note: Rupture membranes between contractions to prevent splashing

of amniotic fluid.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note the colour of the fluid (clear, meconium-stained, blood-stained,

greenish, purulent, foul smelling). If thick meconium is present, suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After amniotomy, listen to the fetal heart rate during and after a

contraction. If the fetal heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than 180 beats per minute), suspect fetal distress (page S-109).

## Augmentation Of Labour

### Augmentation Of Labour — phần 1

If hyperstimulation occurs (any contraction lasts longer than 60 seconds) or if there are more than five contractions in 10 minutes:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Stop the infusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remain with the woman until normal uterine activity is achieved.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Position the woman on her left side (left lateral position).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assess the fetal heart rate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is normal (between 100 and 180 beats per

minute), observe for improvement in uterine activity and monitor the fetal heart rate.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate is abnormal (less than 100 or more than

180 beats per minute), manage for fetal distress (page S-109) and relax the uterus using betamimetics: terbutaline 250 mcg IV slowly over five minutes OR salbutamol 10 mg in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate) at 10 drops per minute.

### Augmentation Of Labour — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: Induction and augmentation of labour; P-31

Note: Betamimetics should not be used in women with cardiac disease. If the woman has cardiac disease or betamimetics are not available, non- betamimetics (e.g. nifedipine 20 mg orally) may be used.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe for improvement in uterine activity, and monitor the fetal

heart rate:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If normal activity is not established within 20 minutes and

betamimetics have not been administered, relax the uterus using betamimetics (see above).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetal heart rate becomes reassuring or normal and

normal uterine activity is established for a period of at least 30 minutes, cautiously recommence oxytocin infusion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-32; Induction and augmentation of labour

## Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33

### Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33 — phần 1

Figure P-6 shows the essential components of the obstetric vacuum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-6.; Obstetric vacuum

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review and apply basic principles for procedures when conducting an

obstetric vacuum–assisted birth (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for conditions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; vertex presentation;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; term fetus;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix fully dilated;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head at least at 0 station or no more than 2/5 palpable above

symphysis pubis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check all connections and test the vacuum on a gloved hand.

Routine antibiotic prophylaxis is not recommended for women undergoing an obstetric vacuum–assisted birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. If necessary, use a

pudendal block (page P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, assess the position of the fetal head by feeling

### Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33 — phần 2

the sagittal suture line and the fontanelles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the posterior fontanelle (Fig. P-7, page P-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-34; Vacuum-assisted birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-7.; Landmarks of the fetal skull

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Locate the flexion point, 3 cm anterior to the posterior fontanelle

(Figs. P-8 and P-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-8.; Locating the flexion point (F)

FIGURE P-9. Locating the flexion point (F), 3 cm anterior to the posterior fontanelle, and calculating cup insertion distance

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vacuum-assisted birth; P-35

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply the largest cup that will fit, with the centre of the cup over the

flexion point. The edge of the cup should be placed about 1 cm anterior to the posterior fontanelle. This placement will promote flexion, descent and autorotation with traction (Fig. P-10).

FIGURE P-10. Applying the cup A. Holding the cup, retracting the perineum

### Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33 — phần 3

B. Inserting the cup through the introitus

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-36; Vacuum-assisted birth

C. Manoeuvring the cup towards and over the flexion point

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Consider episiotomy if the perineum interferes with the axis of

traction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the application. Ensure that there is no maternal soft tissue

(cervix or vagina) within the rim of the cup.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the pump, create a vacuum of 0.2 kg/cm2 negative pressure and

check the application.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Increase the vacuum to 0.8 kg/cm2 and check the application.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After maximum negative pressure, start traction in the line of the

pelvic axis and perpendicular to the cup. If the fetal head is tilted to one side or not flexed well, traction should be directed in a line that will try to correct the tilt or deflexion of the head (i.e. to one side or the other, not necessarily in the midline).

### Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply traction at the onset of each contraction and maintain it

throughout the contraction. Apply traction in a line perpendicular to the plane of the cup rim (Fig. P-11, page P-37). Place a finger on the scalp next to the cup during traction to assess potential slippage and descent of the vertex.

In between contractions, do not apply traction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to assist descent with expulsive efforts.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the head slowly and protect the perineum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vacuum-assisted birth; P-37

FIGURE P-11. Applying traction and delivering the head

A. Applying traction

B. Axis for traction changes according to the pelvic curve

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-38; Vacuum-assisted birth

C. Delivering the head (hand protects perineum while completing delivery of the head)

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Between contractions check:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal heart rate

### Vacuum-Assisted Birth P-33 — phần 5

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; application of the cup.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Removal of cup. As soon as the head is crowned or after delivery of

the head, release the vacuum, remove the cup, and complete delivery of the baby.

TIPS • Never use the cup to actively rotate the baby’s head. Rotation of the baby’s head will occur with traction. • The first pulls help to find the proper direction for pulling. • Do not continue to pull between contractions and expulsive efforts. • With progress, and in the absence of fetal distress, continue the “guiding” pulls for a maximum of 30 minutes.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform active management of the third stage of labour.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the uterus is well contracted and that blood loss is not

excessive.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for genital tract trauma and repair any lacerations or tears that

may have occurred.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair episiotomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Vacuum-assisted birth; P-39

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor the woman’s uterine tone, vaginal bleeding, pulse,

temperature and blood pressure every 15 minutes for the first two hours, every 30 minutes for the third hour after birth, and then hourly for three hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the baby is dry and warm, that the cord is securely tied,

and that the baby is put to the mother’s breast as soon as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the baby’s scalp and note any injuries. Explain to the parents

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

the reason for the large swelling “chignon” and assure them that it will disappear within a few hours and will not damage the baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the woman and the baby to rest comfortably where their

recovery can be monitored.

## Post-Procedure Tasks

### Post-Procedure Tasks — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove gloves and discard them in a leakproof container.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash hands and forearms thoroughly with soap and water and dry

them with a clean, dry cloth (or air-dry hands).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Decontaminate and clean equipment according to recommended

practices.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assemble the equipment and check the vacuum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; High-level disinfect or sterilize the equipment.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Document the following information:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; indication for vacuum birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; date and time of the procedure;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; name of the clinician performing the procedure and the names of

personnel who assisted;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; length of the procedure and the number of pulls;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; position of the fetal head prior to application of the cup (occipito-

anterior, occipito-lateral, occipito-posterior);

### Post-Procedure Tasks — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; birth position (occipito-anterior or occipito-posterior);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; condition of the baby at birth, colour, whether breathing and any

resuscitation needed as well as position of “chignon” and any bruising;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; details of the third stage of labour;

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-40; Vacuum-assisted birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; details of any medications used;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; maternal condition following the procedure;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; any complications affecting the mother or baby.

## Failure

### Failure — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Vacuum-assisted birth has failed if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetal head does not advance with each pull;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; there is no descent of the baby’s head after three pulls with no

descent;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the cup slips off the head twice at the proper direction of pull with

maximum negative pressure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Every application should be considered as a test of the obstetric

vacuum. Do not persist if there is no descent after three pulls.

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

Complications usually result from not observing the conditions of application or from continuing efforts beyond the time limits stated above.

## Fetal Complications

### Fetal Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Localized scalp oedema (caput succedaneum or chignon) under the

vacuum cup is harmless and disappears in a few hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cephalohaematoma requires observation and usually will clear in three

to four weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Scalp abrasions (common and harmless) and lacerations may occur.

Clean and examine lacerations to determine if sutures are necessary. Necrosis is extremely rare.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Intracranial bleeding is extremely rare and requires immediate

intensive neonatal care.

## Maternal Complications

### Maternal Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tears of the genital tract may occur. Examine the woman carefully and

repair any tears to the cervix (page P-95) or vagina (page P-97) or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

## Forceps-Assisted Birth P-41

### Forceps-Assisted Birth P-41 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review and apply general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for conditions:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; vertex presentation or face presentation with chin-anterior or

entrapped after-coming head in breech vaginal birth (page P-49);

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; cervix fully dilated;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal head at +2 or +3 station or 0/5 palpable above the symphysis

pubis. At a minimum, the sagittal suture should be in the midline and straight, guaranteeing an occiput anterior or occiput posterior position.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement (page C-9). If

necessary, use a pudendal block (page P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assemble the forceps before application. Ensure that the parts fit

together and lock well.

Routine antibiotic prophylaxis is not recommended for women undergoing forceps-assisted birth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lubricate the blades of the forceps.

### Forceps-Assisted Birth P-41 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, insert two fingers of the right hand into the

vagina on the side of the fetal head. Slide the left blade gently between the head and fingers to rest on the side of the head (Fig. P-12).

A biparietal, bimalar application is the only safe application.

FIGURE P-12. Applying the left blade of the forceps

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-42; Forceps-assisted birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repeat the same manoeuvre on the other side, using the left hand and

the right blade of the forceps (Fig. P-13).

FIGURE P-13. Applying the right blade of the forceps

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Depress the handles and lock the forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Difficulty in locking usually indicates that the application is incorrect.

In this case, remove the blades and recheck the position of the head. Reapply only if rotation is confirmed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After locking, apply steady traction inferiorly and posteriorly with

### Forceps-Assisted Birth P-41 — phần 3

each contraction (Fig. P-14).

FIGURE P-14. Locking and applying traction

- Dòng dữ liệu: Forceps-assisted birth; P-43

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Between contractions check:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal heart rate

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; application of forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the head crowns, make an episiotomy, if necessary (page P-85).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lift the head slowly out of the vagina between contractions.

The head should descend with each pull. Only two or three pulls should be necessary.

## Failure

### Failure — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Forceps have failed if:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the fetal head does not advance with each pull;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; there is no descent of the baby’s head after three pulls.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Every application should be considered a test of use of the forceps. Do

not persist if the head does not descend after three pulls.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If forceps fails, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

## Fetal Complications

### Fetal Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Injury to facial nerves requires observation. This injury usually

resolves spontaneously.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lacerations of the face and scalp may occur. Clean and examine

lacerations to determine if sutures are necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Fractures of the face and skull require observation.

## Maternal Complications

### Maternal Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Tears of the genital tract may occur. Examine the woman carefully and

repair any tears to the cervix (page P-95) or vagina (page P-97) or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Uterine rupture may occur and requires immediate treatment

(page P-113).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-44; Forceps-assisted birth

## Breech Birth P-45

### Breech Birth P-45 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications. Ensure that all conditions for safe vaginal

breech birth are met.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. If necessary, use a

pudendal block (page P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform all manoeuvres gently and without undue force.

## Complete Or Frank Breech

### Complete Or Frank Breech — phần 1

FIGURE P-15. Breech presentation

## Birth Of The Buttocks And Legs

### Birth Of The Buttocks And Legs — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the buttocks have entered the vagina and the cervix is fully

dilated, tell the woman she can bear down with the contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In the presence of physical obstruction due to lesions or scar tissue in

the perineum, a decision to perform an episiotomy may be made (page P-85).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Let the buttocks deliver until the lower back and then the shoulder

blades are seen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently hold the buttocks in one hand, but do not pull.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the legs do not deliver spontaneously, deliver one leg at a time:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Push behind the knee to bend the leg.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-46; Breech birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Grasp the ankle and deliver the foot and leg.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat for the other leg.

Do not pull the baby while the legs are being delivered.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the baby by the hips, as shown in Fig. P-16. Do not hold the

### Birth Of The Buttocks And Legs — phần 2

baby by the flanks or abdomen as this may cause kidney or liver damage.

FIGURE P-16. Hold the baby at the hips, but do not pull

## Arms Are Felt On Chest

### Arms Are Felt On Chest — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the arms to disengage spontaneously one by one. Only assist if

necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After spontaneous delivery of the first arm, lift the buttocks towards

the mother’s abdomen to enable the second arm to deliver spontaneously.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the arm does not spontaneously deliver, place one or two fingers

in the elbow and bend the arm, bringing the hand down over the baby’s face.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breech birth; P-47

## Arms Are Stretched Above The Head Or Folded Around The

### Arms Are Stretched Above The Head Or Folded Around The — phần 1

NECK Use the Lovset’s manoeuvre (Fig. P-17):

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the baby by the hips and turn half a circle, keeping the back

uppermost and applying downward traction at the same time, so that the arm that was posterior becomes anterior and can be delivered under the pubic arch.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Assist delivery of the arm by placing one or two fingers on the upper

## part of the arm. Draw the arm down over the chest as the elbow is

### part of the arm. Draw the arm down over the chest as the elbow is — phần 1

flexed, with the hand sweeping over the face.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To deliver the second arm, turn the baby back half a circle, keeping

the back uppermost and applying downward traction, and deliver the second arm in the same way under the pubic arch.

FIGURE P-17. Lovset’s manoeuvre

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-48; Breech birth

## Baby’S Body Cannot Be Turned

### Baby’S Body Cannot Be Turned — phần 1

If the baby’s body cannot be turned to deliver the arm that is anterior first, deliver the shoulder that is posterior (Fig. P-18):

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold and lift the baby up by the ankles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Move the baby’s chest towards the woman’s inner leg. The shoulder

that is posterior should deliver.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the arm and hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lay the baby back down by the ankles. The shoulder that is anterior

should now deliver.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the arm and hand.

FIGURE P-18. Delivery of the shoulder that is posterior

## Birth Of The Head

### Birth Of The Head — phần 1

Deliver the head by the Mauriceau-Smellie-Veit manoeuvre (Fig. P-19, page P-49) as follows:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lay the baby face down with the length of its body over your hand and

arm.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the first and third fingers of this hand on the baby’s cheekbones

and place the second finger in the baby’s mouth to pull the jaw down and flex the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breech birth; P-49

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the other hand to grasp the baby’s shoulders.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With fingers of the first hand, gently flex the baby’s head towards the

chest while continuing to pull on the jaw to bring the baby’s head down until the hairline is visible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pull gently to deliver the head.

Note: Ask an assistant to push above the woman’s pubic bone as the head delivers. This helps to keep the baby’s head flexed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Raise the baby, still astride the arm, until the mouth and nose are free.

### Birth Of The Head — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-19.; The Mauriceau-Smellie-Veit manoeuvre

## Entrapped (Stuck) Head

### Entrapped (Stuck) Head — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant available to hold the baby while applying Piper or

long forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Be sure the cervix is fully dilated.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wrap the baby’s body in a cloth or towel and hold the baby up.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the left blade of the forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the right blade and lock handles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the forceps to flex and deliver the baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If unable to use forceps, apply firm pressure above the mother’s

pubic bone to flex the baby’s head and push it through the pelvis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-50; Breech birth

## Footling Breech

### Footling Breech — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; A footling breech baby (Fig. P-20) will usually require caesarean birth

(page P-53).

FIGURE P-20. Single footling breech presentation, with one leg extended at hip and knee

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Limit vaginal birth of a footling breech baby to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; advanced labour with a fully dilated cervix;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a preterm baby not likely to survive after birth;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; delivery of additional baby(s) in multiple gestation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To deliver the baby vaginally:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Grasp the baby’s ankles with one hand.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If only one foot presents, insert a hand into the vagina and gently

pull the other foot down.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently pull the baby downwards by the ankles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Deliver the baby until the back and shoulder blades are seen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Proceed with delivery of the arms (page P-46).

## Completing The Birth Of The Baby

### Completing The Birth Of The Baby — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics after birth of the baby

(page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Breech birth; P-51

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Suction the baby’s mouth and nose.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clamp and cut the cord.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a uterotonic drug (oxytocin 10 units IM is the uterotonic drug of

choice) within one minute of birth, and continue active management of the third stage (page C-102).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the woman carefully and repair any tears to the cervix

(page P-95) or vagina (page P-97) or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-52; Breech birth

## Caesarean Birth P-53

### Caesarean Birth P-53 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications. Ensure that vaginal birth is not possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for fetal life by listening to the fetal heart rate, and examine for

fetal presentation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the fetus is dead:

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; Perform a craniotomy (page P-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; If the provider is not proficient in craniotomy, perform a

caesarean (page P-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65), and start an IV infusion (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If an intracaesarean insertion of an IUD or a post-caesarean tubal

ligation is planned, ensure that the woman is still eligible and has provided informed consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use spinal anaesthesia (page P-11), local infiltration with lidocaine

(page P-7), ketamine (page P-13) or general anaesthesia:

### Caesarean Birth P-53 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Local anaesthesia is a safe alternative to general, ketamine or

spinal anaesthesia when these anaesthetics (or persons trained in their use) are not available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; When using a local anaesthesia for caesarean, the provider must

counsel the woman and reassure her throughout the procedure. The provider must keep in mind that the woman is awake and alert, and must use instruments and handle tissue as gently as possible. Note: In the case of heart failure, use local infiltration anaesthesia with conscious sedation. Avoid spinal anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-49)

15–60 minutes prior to skin incision:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Determine if a high vertical incision (page P-62) is indicated.

Indications include the following:

### Caesarean Birth P-53 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; an inaccessible lower segment due to dense adhesions from

previous caesarean;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; transverse lie (with baby’s back down) for which a lower uterine

segment incision cannot be safely performed;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fetal malformations (e.g. conjoined twins);

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-54; Caesarean birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; large fibroids over the lower segment;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; a highly vascular lower segment due to placenta praevia;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; carcinoma of the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s head is deep down in the pelvis, as in obstructed

labour, prepare the vagina for a caesarean birth (page C-53).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have the operating table tilted to the left, or place a pillow or folded

linen under the woman’s right lower back, to decrease supine hypotension syndrome.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cleanse the vagina with povidone-iodine immediately before the

### Caesarean Birth P-53 — phần 4

caesarean to prevent post-caesarean endometritis.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the skin with an antiseptic agent (e.g. chlorhexidine,

povidone-iodine) prior to incision to prevent surgical site infections. Note: The choice of an antiseptic agent and its method of application for skin preparation before caesarean should be based primarily on the clinician’s experience with that particular antiseptic agent and method of application and the antiseptic agent’s cost and local availability.

## Opening The Abdomen

### Opening The Abdomen — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic hair,

through the skin and to the level of the fascia (Fig. P-21). Note: If the caesarean is performed under local anaesthesia, make a midline incision that is about 4 cm longer than the incision made when general anaesthesia is used. A Pfannenstiel’s incision should not be used, as it takes longer, retraction is poorer and it requires more local anaesthetic.

FIGURE P-21. Site of abdominal incision

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean birth; P-55

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up and

down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal wall

muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use fingers to make an opening in the peritoneum near the umbilicus.

### Opening The Abdomen — phần 2

Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down in order to see the entire uterus. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use forceps to pick up the loose peritoneum covering the anterior

surface of the lower uterine segment, and incise with scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Extend the incision by placing the scissors between the uterus and the

loose serosa and cutting about 3 cm on each side in a transverse fashion.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use two fingers to push the bladder downwards off of the lower

uterine segment. Replace the bladder retractor over the pubic bone and bladder.

## Opening The Uterus

### Opening The Uterus — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a scalpel to make a 3 cm transverse incision in the lower segment

of the uterus. It should be about 1 cm below the level where the vesico-uterine serosa was incised to bring the bladder down.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Widen the incision by placing a finger at each edge and gently pulling

upwards and laterally at the same time (Fig. P-22, page P-56).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the lower uterine segment is thick and narrow, extend the

incision in a crescent shape, using scissors instead of fingers to avoid extension of the uterine vessels.

It is important to make the uterine incision big enough to deliver the head and body of the baby without tearing the incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-56; Caesarean birth

FIGURE P-22. Enlarging the uterine incision

## Birth Of The Baby And Delivery Of The Placenta

### Birth Of The Baby And Delivery Of The Placenta — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To deliver the baby, place one hand inside the uterine cavity between

the uterus and the baby’s head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the fingers, grasp and flex the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently lift the baby’s head through the incision (Fig. P-23, page

P-57), taking care not to extend the incision down towards the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the other hand, gently press on the abdomen over the top of the

uterus to help deliver the head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s head is deep down in the pelvis or vagina, ask an

assistant (wearing sterile gloves) to reach into the vagina and push the baby’s head up through the vagina. Then lift and deliver the head (Fig. P-24, page P-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean birth; P-57

FIGURE P-23. Delivering the baby’s head

FIGURE P-24. Delivering the deeply engaged head

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Suction the baby’s mouth and nose when the head is delivered.

### Birth Of The Baby And Delivery Of The Placenta — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the shoulders and body.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) at 60 drops per minute for two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-58; Caesarean birth

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clamp and cut the umbilical cord.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hand the baby to the assistant for initial care (page C-107).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep gentle traction on the cord and massage (rub) the uterus through

the abdomen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the placenta and membranes. Use ring forceps to ensure that

all membranes are removed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is medically eligible, has been appropriately

counselled and has chosen postpartum insertion of an IUD, proceed to intracaesarean insertion of the IUD.

## Closing The Uterine Incision

### Closing The Uterine Incision — phần 1

Note: If a Couvelaire uterus (swollen and discoloured by blood) is seen during caesarean, close it in the normal manner. Observe for bleeding and assess uterine tone. Be prepared to manage coagulopathy (page S-24) or atonic uterus (page S-32).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the corners of the uterine incision with clamps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the edges of the incision with clamps. Make sure the uterus is

separate from the bladder.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look carefully for any extensions of the uterine incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the incision and any extensions with a continuous locking stitch

of 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture (Fig. P-25, page P-59).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is any further bleeding from the incision site, close with

figure-of-eight sutures. There is no need for a routine second layer of sutures in the uterine incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean birth; P-59

### Closing The Uterine Incision — phần 2

FIGURE P-25. Closing the uterine incision

## Closing The Abdomen

### Closing The Abdomen — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Look carefully at the uterine incision before closing the abdomen.

Make sure there is no bleeding and the uterus is firm. Use a sponge to remove any clots inside the abdomen.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine carefully for injuries to the bladder and repair any found

(page P-115).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic)

suture. Note: There is no need to close the bladder peritoneum or the abdominal peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with gauze

and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical mattress

sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently push on the abdomen over the uterus to remove clots from the

### Closing The Abdomen — phần 2

uterus and vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-60; Caesarean birth

## Bleeding Is Not Controlled

### Bleeding Is Not Controlled — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Massage the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the uterus is atonic, continue to infuse oxytocin and give

ergometrine 0.2 mg IM and prostaglandins, if available. These drugs can be given together or sequentially (Table S-11, page S-32).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Have an assistant press fingers over the aorta to reduce the bleeding

until the source of bleeding can be found and stopped.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding is not controlled, perform uterine and utero-ovarian artery

ligation (page P-117) or hysterectomy (page P-121).

## Baby Is Breech

### Baby Is Breech — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby is breech, grasp a foot and deliver it through the incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Complete the delivery as in a vaginal breech birth (page P-45):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Deliver the legs and the body up to the shoulders; then deliver the

arms.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Flex (bend) the head using the Mauriceau-Smellie-Veit

manoeuvre (page P-49).

## The Baby’S Back Is Up

### The Baby’S Back Is Up — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s back is up (near the top of the uterus), reach into the

uterus and find the baby’s ankles.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the ankles and pull gently through the incision to deliver the

legs and complete the delivery as for a breech birth (page P-45).

## The Baby’S Back Is Down

### The Baby’S Back Is Down — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the baby’s back is down, a high vertical uterine incision is the

preferred incision (page P-62).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean birth; P-61

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the incision is made, reach into the uterus and find the feet. Pull

them through the incision and complete the delivery as for a breech birth (page P-45).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; To repair the vertical incision, you will need several layers of suture

(page P-62).

## Placenta Praevia

### Placenta Praevia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a low anterior placenta is encountered, incise through it and

deliver the baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After birth of the baby, if the placenta cannot be detached manually,

the diagnosis is placenta accreta, a common finding at the site of a previous caesarean scar. Perform a hysterectomy (page P-121).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Women with placenta praevia are at high risk of postpartum

haemorrhage. If there is bleeding from the placental site, under-run the bleeding sites with chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Watch for bleeding in the immediate postpartum period and take

appropriate action (page S-29).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If bleeding occurs:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Massage the uterus to expel blood and blood clots. The presence

of blood clots will inhibit effective uterine contractions.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Give oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) at 60 drops per minute, ergometrine 0.2 mg IM and prostaglandins (Table S-11, page S-32). These drugs can be given together or sequentially.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or if the woman currently has a

fever, give a combination of antibiotics for 24–48 hours after complete resolution of clinical signs and symptoms (fever, uterine tenderness, purulent lochia, leucocytosis) (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-62; Caesarean birth

## High Vertical (“Classical”) Incision

### High Vertical (“Classical”) Incision — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen through a midline incision skirting the umbilicus.

Approximately one-third of the incision should be above the umbilicus and two-thirds below.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a scalpel to make the incision:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Check the position of the round ligaments and ensure that the

incision is in the midline (the uterus may have twisted to one side).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make the uterine incision in the midline over the fundus of the

uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The incision should be approximately 12–15 cm in length, and the

lower limit should not extend to the utero-vesical fold of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant (wearing sterile gloves) to apply pressure on the cut

edges to control the bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Cut down to the level of the membranes and then extend the incision

using scissors.

### High Vertical (“Classical”) Incision — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After rupturing the membranes, grasp the baby’s foot and deliver the

baby.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the placenta and membranes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the edges of the incision with Allis or Green Armytage forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the incision using at least three layers of sutures:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the first layer closest to the cavity, but avoid the decidua,

with a continuous 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the second layer of uterine muscle using interrupted

1 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the superficial fibres and the serosa using a continuous

0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture with an atraumatic needle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen as for lower segment caesarean (page P-59).

The woman should not labour with future pregnancies.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Caesarean birth; P-63

## Tubal Ligation At Caesarean

### Tubal Ligation At Caesarean — phần 1

Tubal ligation can be performed immediately after caesarean if the woman requested the procedure before labour began (during prenatal visits). Adequate counselling and informed decision-making and consent must precede voluntary sterilization procedures; these are often not possible during labour and childbirth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for patient consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the least vascular, middle portion of the fallopian tube with a

Babcock or Allis forceps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold up a loop of tube 2.5 cm in length (Fig. P-26 A, page P-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Crush the base of the loop with artery forceps and ligate it with 0 plain

catgut suture (Fig. P-26 B, page P-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Excise the loop (a segment 1 cm in length) from between the crushed

areas (Fig. P-26 C–D, page P-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repeat the procedure on the other side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-64; Caesarean birth

FIGURE P-26. Tubal ligation

## Craniotomy And Craniocentesis P-65

### Craniotomy And Craniocentesis P-65 — phần 1

In certain cases of obstructed labour with fetal death, reduction in the size of the fetal head by craniotomy makes vaginal birth possible and avoids the risks associated with caesarean. Craniocentesis can be used to reduce the size of a hydrocephalic head to make birth possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. If necessary, give

diazepam IV slowly or use a pudendal block (page P-3).

## Craniotomy (Skull Perforation)

### Craniotomy (Skull Perforation) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications. Verify that fetal death has occurred: If the

fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to listen or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and apply antiseptic

solution to the vagina (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional and psychological support (page C-9) before,

during and after the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform an episiotomy, if required (page P-85).

## Cephalic Presentation

### Cephalic Presentation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make a cruciate (cross-shaped) incision on the scalp (Fig. P-27).

FIGURE P-27. Cruciate incision on scalp

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-66; Craniotomy and craniocentesis

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the cranial vault at the lowest and most central bony point with a

craniotome (or large pointed scissors or a heavy scalpel). In face presentation, perforate the orbits.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the craniotome into the fetal cranium and fragment the

intracranial contents.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the edges of the skull with several heavy-toothed forceps

(e.g. Kocher) and apply traction in the axis of the birth canal (Fig. P-28).

FIGURE P-28. Applying scalp traction

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; As the head descends, pressure from the bony pelvis will cause the

skull to collapse, decreasing the cranial diameter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the head is not delivered easily, perform a caesarean (page P-53).

## Breech Presentation With Entrapped Head

### Breech Presentation With Entrapped Head — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make an incision through the skin at the base of the neck.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a craniotome (or large pointed scissors or heavy scalpel) through

the incision and tunnel subcutaneously to reach the occiput.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perforate the occiput and open the gap as widely as possible.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply traction on the trunk to collapse the skull as the head descends.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Craniotomy and craniocentesis; P-67

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After birth of the baby, examine the woman carefully and repair any

tears to the cervix (page P-95) or vagina (page P-97), or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Leave a self-retaining catheter in place until it is confirmed that there

is no bladder injury.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure adequate fluid intake and urinary output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional and psychological support (page C-9).

## Craniocentesis (Skull Puncture)

### Craniocentesis (Skull Puncture) — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications. Verify that fetal death has occurred: If the

fetal heart cannot be heard, ask several other persons to listen or use a Doppler stethoscope, if available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and apply antiseptic

solution to the vagina (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional and psychological support (page C-9) before,

during and after the procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make an episiotomy, if required (page P-85).

## Fully Dilated Cervix

### Fully Dilated Cervix — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pass a large-bore spinal needle through the dilated cervix and through

the sagittal suture line or fontanelles of the fetal skull (Fig. P-29, page P-68).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate the cerebrospinal fluid until the fetal skull has collapsed, and

allow vaginal birth to proceed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-68; Craniotomy and craniocentesis

FIGURE P-29. Craniocentesis with a dilated cervix

## Closed Cervix

### Closed Cervix — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Palpate for the location of the fetal head.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the suprapubic skin (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pass a large-bore spinal needle through the abdominal and uterine

walls and through the hydrocephalic skull.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate the cerebrospinal fluid until the fetal skull has collapsed, and

allow vaginal birth to proceed.

## After-Coming Head During Breech Birth

### After-Coming Head During Breech Birth — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the rest of the body has been delivered, insert a large-bore spinal

needle through the dilated cervix and foramen magnum (Fig. P-30, page P-69).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate the cerebrospinal fluid and deliver the after-coming head as

in a breech birth (page P-48).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Craniotomy and craniocentesis; P-69

FIGURE P-30. Craniocentesis of the after-coming head

## During Caesarean

### During Caesarean — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After the uterine incision is made, pass a large-bore spinal needle

through the hydrocephalic skull.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate the cerebrospinal fluid until the fetal skull has collapsed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the baby and placenta as in caesarean (page P-56).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; After birth of the baby, examine the woman carefully and repair any

tears to the cervix (page P-95) or vagina (page P-97), or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Leave a self-retaining catheter in place until it is confirmed that there

is no bladder injury.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure adequate fluid intake and urinary output.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional and psychological support (page C-9).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-70; Craniotomy and craniocentesis

## Dilatation And Curettage P-71

### Dilatation And Curettage P-71 — phần 1

The preferred method of evacuation of the uterus is manual vacuum aspiration (page P-75). Dilatation and curettage should be used only if manual vacuum aspiration is not available.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications (page P-75).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. Give morphine IM or

IV before the procedure or use a paracervical block (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Administer oxytocin 10 units IM or ergometrine 0.2 mg IM before the

procedure to make the myometrium firmer and reduce the risk of perforation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a bimanual pelvic examination to assess the size and position

of the uterus and the condition of the fornices.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a speculum or vaginal retractor into the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the vagina and cervix (especially the os)

(page C-35).

### Dilatation And Curettage P-71 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the cervix for tears or protruding products of conception. If

products of conception are present in the vagina or cervix, remove them using ring or sponge forceps. Note: With incomplete abortion, a ring or sponge forceps is preferable to grasp the cervix, as it is less likely than the tenaculum to tear the cervix with traction and does not require the use of lidocaine for placement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently grasp the anterior or posterior lip of the cervix with a vulsellum

or single-toothed tenaculum (Fig. P-31, page P-72).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a tenaculum to grasp the cervix, first inject 1 mL of

0.5% lidocaine solution into the anterior or posterior lip of the cervix that has been exposed by the speculum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dilatation is needed only when the pregnancy has not passed at all or

when some retained products of conception have remained in the uterus for several days:

### Dilatation And Curettage P-71 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently introduce the widest gauge cannula or curette.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use graduated dilators only if the cannula or curette will not pass.

Begin with the smallest dilator and end with the largest dilator that ensures adequate dilatation (usually 10–12 mm) (Fig. P-32, page P-72).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Take care not to tear the cervix or to create a false opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-72; Dilatation and curettage

FIGURE P-31. Inserting a retractor and holding the anterior lip of the cervix

FIGURE P-32. Dilating the cervix

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently pass a uterine sound through the cervix to assess the length and

direction of the uterus.

The uterus is very soft in pregnancy and can be easily injured during this procedure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evacuate the contents of the uterus with ring forceps or a large curette

(Fig. P-33, page P-73). Gently curette the walls of the uterus until a grating sensation is felt.

### Dilatation And Curettage P-71 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Dilatation and curettage; P-73

FIGURE P-33. Curetting the uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the speculum or retractors and perform a bimanual pelvic

examination to check the size and firmness of the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the evacuated material (page P-78). Send material for

histopathologic examination, if required.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give paracetamol 500 mg by mouth as needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to eat, drink and walk about as she wishes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible, including tetanus prophylaxis,

counselling and a family planning method (page S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discharge uncomplicated cases in one to two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise women to watch for symptoms and signs requiring immediate

attention:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prolonged cramping (more than a few days)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prolonged bleeding (more than two weeks)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; bleeding more than normal menstrual bleeding

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; severe or increased pain

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fever, chills or malaise

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fainting.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-74; Dilatation and curettage

## Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications for manual vacuum aspiration (inevitable

abortion before 16 weeks, incomplete abortion, molar pregnancy or delayed postpartum haemorrhage due to retained placental fragments).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement, and give paracetamol

30 minutes before the procedure. Use a paracervical block if necessary (page P-1).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare the manual vacuum aspiration syringe:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Assemble the syringe.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the pinch valve.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Pull back on the plunger until the plunger arms lock.

Note: For molar pregnancy, when the uterine contents are likely to be copious, have three syringes ready for use.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Even if bleeding is slight, give oxytocin 10 units IM or ergometrine

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 2

0.2 mg IM before the procedure to make the myometrium firmer and reduce the risk of perforation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Perform a bimanual pelvic examination to assess the size and position

of the uterus and the condition of the fornices.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a speculum or vaginal retractor into the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the vagina and cervix (especially the os)

(page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check the cervix for tears or protruding products of conception. If

products of conception are present in the vagina or cervix, remove them using ring or sponge forceps. Note: With incomplete abortion, a ring or sponge forceps is preferable to grasp the cervix, as it is less likely than the tenaculum to tear the cervix with traction and does not require the use of lidocaine for placement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently grasp the anterior or posterior lip of the cervix with a vulsellum

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 3

or single-toothed tenaculum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If using a tenaculum to grasp the cervix, first inject 1 mL of

0.5% lidocaine solution into the anterior or posterior lip of the cervix that has been exposed by the speculum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dilatation is needed only when the pregnancy has not passed at all or

when products of conception have remained in the uterus for several days:

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-76; Manual vacuum aspiration

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently introduce the widest gauge suction cannula.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use graduated dilators only if the cannula will not pass. Begin

with the smallest dilator and end with the largest dilator that ensures adequate dilatation (usually 10–12 mm) (Fig. P-32, page P-72).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Take care not to tear the cervix or to create a false opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; While gently applying traction to the cervix, insert the cannula through

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 4

the cervix into the uterine cavity just past the internal os (Fig. P-34). (Rotating the cannula while gently applying pressure often helps the tip of the cannula pass through the cervical canal.)

FIGURE P-34. Inserting the cannula

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Slowly push the cannula into the uterine cavity until it touches the

fundus, but not more than 10 cm. Measure the depth of the uterus by dots visible on the cannula and then withdraw the cannula slightly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Attach the prepared manual vacuum aspiration syringe to the cannula

by holding the vulsellum (or tenaculum) and the end of the cannula in one hand and the syringe in the other.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Release the pinch valve(s) on the syringe to transfer the vacuum

through the cannula to the uterine cavity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Manual vacuum aspiration; P-77

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Evacuate remaining uterine contents by gently rotating the syringe

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 5

from side to side (10 to 12 o’clock) and then moving the cannula gently and slowly back and forth within the uterine cavity (Fig. P-35). Note: To avoid losing the vacuum, do not withdraw the cannula opening past the cervical os. If the vacuum is lost or if the syringe is more than half full, empty it and then re-establish the vacuum. Note: Avoid grasping the syringe by the plunger arms while the vacuum is established and the cannula is in the uterus. If the plunger arms become

unlocked, the plunger may accidentally slip back into the syringe, pushing material back into the uterus.

FIGURE P-35. Evacuating the contents of the uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for signs of completion:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Red or pink foam but no more tissue is seen in the cannula.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A grating sensation is felt as the cannula passes over the surface

of the evacuated uterus.

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 6

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The uterus contracts around (grips) the cannula.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Withdraw the cannula. Detach the syringe and place the cannula in

decontamination solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-78; Manual vacuum aspiration

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; With the valve open, empty the contents of the MVA syringe into a

strainer by pushing on the plunger. Note: Place the empty syringe on a high-level disinfected or sterile tray or container until you are certain the procedure is complete.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the speculum or retractors and perform a bimanual

examination to check the size and firmness of the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Quickly inspect the tissue removed from the uterus to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; assess quantity and presence of products of conception;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ensure complete evacuation; and

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; check for a molar pregnancy (rare).

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 7

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If necessary, strain and rinse the tissue to remove excess blood clots,

then place in a container of clean water, saline or weak acetic acid (vinegar) to examine. Tissue specimens may also be sent for histopathologic examination, if required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If no products of conception are seen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; All of the products of conception may have been passed before

the MVA was performed (complete abortion).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The uterine cavity may appear to be empty but may not have been

emptied completely. Repeat the evacuation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The vaginal bleeding may been due to something other than an

incomplete abortion (e.g. breakthrough bleeding, which is sometimes seen with hormonal contraceptives or uterine fibroids).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The uterus may be abnormal (e.g. cannula may have been inserted

### Manual Vacuum Aspiration P-75 — phần 8

in the nonpregnant side of a double uterus). Note: The absence of products of conception in a woman with symptoms of pregnancy raises the strong possibility of ectopic pregnancy (page S-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently insert a speculum into the vagina and examine for bleeding. If

the uterus is still soft and not smaller, or if there is persistent, brisk bleeding, repeat the evacuation.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give the woman paracetamol 500 mg by mouth as needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Encourage the woman to eat, drink and walk about as she wishes.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Manual vacuum aspiration; P-79

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible, including tetanus prophylaxis,

counselling and a family planning method (page S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Discharge women without complications in one to two hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise women to watch for symptoms and signs requiring immediate

attention:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prolonged cramping (more than a few days)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; prolonged bleeding (more than two weeks)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; bleeding more than normal menstrual bleeding

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; severe or increased pain

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fever, chills or malaise

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; fainting.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-80; Manual vacuum aspiration

## Culdocentesis

### Culdocentesis — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and apply antiseptic

solution to the vagina (especially the posterior fornix) (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. If necessary, use local

infiltration with lidocaine (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently grasp the posterior lip of the cervix with a tenaculum and

gently pull to elevate the cervix and expose the posterior vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a long needle (e.g. spinal needle) on a syringe and insert it

through the posterior vagina, just below the posterior lip of the cervix (Fig. P-36).

FIGURE P-36. Diagnostic puncture of the cul-de-sac

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pull back on the syringe to aspirate the cul-de-sac (the space behind

the uterus).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If non-clotting blood is obtained, suspect ectopic pregnancy

(page S-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If clotting blood is obtained, a vein or artery may have been

### Culdocentesis — phần 2

aspirated. Remove the needle, reinsert it and aspirate again.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-82; Culdocentesis and colpotomy

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If clear or yellow fluid is obtained, there is no blood in the

peritoneum. The woman might, however, still have an unruptured ectopic pregnancy. Further observations and tests may be needed (page S-16).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If no fluid is obtained, remove the needle, reinsert it and aspirate

again. If no fluid is obtained, the woman might have an unruptured ectopic pregnancy (page S-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If pus is obtained, keep the needle in place and proceed to

colpotomy (see below).

## Colpotomy

### Colpotomy — phần 1

If pus is obtained on culdocentesis, keep the needle in place and make a stab incision at the site of the puncture:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the needle and insert blunt forceps or a finger through the

incision to break loculi in the abscess cavity (Fig. P-37).

FIGURE P-37. Colpotomy for pelvic abscess

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Allow the pus to drain.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert a high-level disinfected or sterile soft rubber corrugated drain

through the incision.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Culdocentesis and colpotomy; P-83

Note: A drain can be prepared by cutting off the fingertips of a sterile examination glove.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If required, use a stitch through the drain to anchor it in the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove the drain when there is no more drainage of pus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If no pus is obtained, the abscess may be higher than the pouch of

Douglas. A laparotomy will be required for peritoneal lavage (wash-out).

### Colpotomy — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-84; Culdocentesis and colpotomy

## Episiotomy P-85

### Episiotomy P-85 — phần 1

Episiotomy should not be performed routinely.

In the presence of physical obstruction due to lesions or scar tissue in the perineum, a decision to perform an episiotomy may be made.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and apply antiseptic

solution to the perineal area (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Make sure there are no known allergies to lidocaine or related drugs.

Use local infiltration with lidocaine (page C-57) or a pudendal block (page P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infiltrate beneath the vaginal mucosa, beneath the skin of the perineum

### Episiotomy P-85 — phần 2

and deeply into the perineal muscle (Fig. P-38, page P-86), using about 10 mL 0.5% lidocaine solution (page C-57). Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the incision site with forceps. If the woman feels the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-86; Episiotomy

FIGURE P-38. Infiltration of perineal tissue with local anaesthetic

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wait to perform episiotomy until:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the perineum is thinned out; and

### Episiotomy P-85 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; 3–4 cm of the baby’s head is visible during a contraction.

Performing an episiotomy will cause bleeding. It should not, therefore, be done too early.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, place two fingers between the baby’s head and

the perineum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use scissors to cut the perineum about 3–4 cm in the mediolateral

direction (Fig. P-39, page P-87).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use scissors to cut 2–3 cm up the middle of the posterior vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Control the baby’s head and shoulders as they are born, ensuring that

the shoulders have rotated to the midline to prevent an extension of the episiotomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Carefully examine for extensions and other tears, and repair (page

P-88).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Episiotomy; P-87

FIGURE P-39. Making the incision while inserting two fingers to protect the baby’s head

### Episiotomy P-85 — phần 4

Note: It is important that absorbable sutures be used for closure. Polyglycolic sutures are preferred over chromic catgut for their tensile strength, non-allergenic properties, and lower probability of infectious complications and episiotomy breakdown. Chromic catgut is an acceptable alternative, but it is not ideal.

## Repair Of Episiotomy

### Repair Of Episiotomy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the area around the episiotomy

(page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the episiotomy is extended through the anal sphincter or rectal

mucosa, manage as third or fourth degree tears (page P-104), including giving prophylactic antibiotics.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the vaginal mucosa using continuous 2-0 suture (Fig. P-40 A,

page P-88):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Start the repair about 1 cm above the apex (top) of the episiotomy.

Continue the suture to the level of the vaginal opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; At the opening of the vagina, bring together the cut edges of the

vaginal opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Bring the needle under the vaginal opening and out through the

incision, and tie.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-88; Episiotomy

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the perineal muscle using interrupted 2-0 sutures (Fig. P-40 B).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the skin using interrupted (or subcuticular) 2-0 sutures

### Repair Of Episiotomy — phần 2

(Fig. P-40 C).

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-40.; Repair of episiotomy

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a haematoma occurs, open and drain it. If there are no signs of

infection and bleeding has stopped, reclose the episiotomy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection, open and drain the wound. Remove

infected sutures and debride the wound:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is mild, antibiotics are not required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is severe but does not involve deep tissues, give

a combination of antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is deep, involves muscles and is causing

necrosis (necrotizing fasciitis), give a combination of antibiotics until necrotic tissue has been removed and the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: Episiotomy; P-89

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

### Complications — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

Note: Necrotizing fasciitis requires wide surgical debridement. Perform delayed primary closure in two to four weeks (depending on resolution of the infection).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-90; Episiotomy

## Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91

### Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review for indications:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the placenta has not expelled within 30 minutes after the

birth of the baby, especially in cases of heavy bleeding, manual removal is recommended.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In the absence of haemorrhage, the woman can be observed for

another 30 minutes before manual removal of the placenta is attempted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; A conservative approach is advised. However, if the woman is

bleeding heavily, manual removal of the placenta should be attempted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Where feasible, ensure that the woman has a companion of her choice

with her while she is being cared for.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement (page C-9) to the

woman. Explain the procedure in simple terms. Obtain her verbal consent to perform the procedure.

### Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give morphine and diazepam IV slowly (do not mix in the same

syringe) or use ketamine (page P-13).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; In cases of haemorrhagic shock, avoid the administration of

medications that could cause mental status changes in the woman (i.e. narcotic pain medication and sedatives).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Catheterize the bladder or ensure that it is empty.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wash and dry hands. Put on sterile gloves (use long gloves if

available).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the umbilical cord with a clamp. Pull the cord gently with one

hand until it is parallel to the floor.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Insert the other hand into the vagina and up into the uterus (Fig. P-42,

page P-92).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-92; Manual removal of placenta

### Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91 — phần 3

FIGURE P-41. Introducing one hand into the vagina along cord

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Let go of the cord and move the hand up over the abdomen in order to

support the fundus of the uterus and provide counter-traction during removal, to prevent inversion of the uterus (Fig. P-42). Note: If uterine inversion occurs, reposition the uterus (page P-109).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Move the fingers of the hand in the uterus laterally until the edge of

the placenta is located.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the cord has been detached previously, insert a hand into the

uterine cavity. Explore the entire cavity until a line of cleavage is identified between the placenta and the uterine wall.

FIGURE P-42. Supporting the fundus while detaching the placenta

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Detach the placenta from the implantation site by keeping the fingers

tightly together and using the edge of the hand to gradually make a space between the placenta and the uterine wall.

### Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91 — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: Manual removal of placenta; P-93

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Proceed slowly all around the placental bed until the whole placenta is

detached from the uterine wall.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; When the placenta is completely separated:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Palpate the inside of the uterine cavity to ensure that all placental

tissue has been removed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Slowly withdraw the hand from the uterus, bringing the placenta

with it.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Continue to provide counter-traction to the fundus by pushing it in

the opposite direction of the hand that is being withdrawn (Fig. P-43).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the placenta does not separate from the uterine surface by gentle

lateral movement of the fingertips at the line of cleavage, remove placental fragments (page S-44). If the tissue is very adherent, suspect placenta accreta and proceed to laparotomy and possible subtotal hysterectomy (page P-122).

### Manual Removal Of Placenta P-91 — phần 5

FIGURE P-43. Withdrawing the hand from the uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) at 60 drops per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant to massage the fundus of the uterus to encourage a

tonic uterine contraction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is continued heavy bleeding, give ergometrine 0.2 mg IM or

prostaglandins (Table S-11, page S-32).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-94; Manual removal of placenta

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the uterine surface of the placenta to ensure that it is

complete. If any placental lobe or tissue is missing, explore the uterine cavity to remove it.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the cervix, vagina and perineum carefully and repair any

tears to the cervix (page P-95) or vagina and perineum (page P-97), or repair episiotomy (page P-87).

## Problems

### Problems — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the placenta is retained due to a constriction ring, or if hours or

days have passed since the woman gave birth, it might not be possible to get the entire hand into the uterus. Extract the placenta in fragments using two fingers, ovum forceps or a wide curette (page S-44).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe the woman closely until the effect of IV sedation has worn

off.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor vital signs (pulse, blood pressure, respiration) every

15 minutes for two hours and then every 30 minutes for the next six hours or until stable.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Palpate the uterine fundus to ensure that the uterus remains contracted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Check for excessive lochia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Continue infusion of IV fluids.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfuse as necessary (page C-37).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Document procedure and post-procedure care, including any

medications given.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Counsel the woman and ensure that she understands what the

procedure was and why it was done.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide postnatal care in the facility for at least 24 hours after

childbirth.

## Repair Of Cervical Tears P-95

### Repair Of Cervical Tears P-95 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and apply antiseptic

solution to the vagina and cervix (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. Anaesthesia is not

required for most cervical tears. For tears that are high and extensive, give morphine and diazepam IV slowly (do not mix in the same syringe) or use ketamine (page P-13).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant to gently provide fundal pressure to help push the

cervix into view.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use vaginal retractors as necessary to expose the cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Gently grasp the cervix with ring or sponge forceps. Apply the forceps

on both sides of the tear, and gently pull in various directions to see the entire cervix. There may be several tears.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the cervical tears with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

### Repair Of Cervical Tears P-95 — phần 2

polyglycolic) suture starting at the apex (upper edge of tear), which is often the source of bleeding (Fig. P-44).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a long section of the rim of the cervix is tattered, under-run it

with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the apex is difficult to reach and ligate, grasp it with artery or ring

forceps. Leave the forceps in place for four hours. Do not persist in attempts to ligate the bleeding points as such attempts may increase the bleeding. Then:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After four hours, open the forceps partially but do not remove.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; After another four hours, remove the forceps completely.

Note: A laparotomy may be required to repair a cervical tear that has extended deep beyond the vaginal vault.

FIGURE P-44. Repair of a cervical tear

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-96; Repair of cervical tears

## Repair Of Vaginal And Perineal Tears P-97

### Repair Of Vaginal And Perineal Tears P-97 — phần 1

Because all women who give birth vaginally are at risk of genital tears, a routine inspection of the vagina, perineum and cervix should be conducted for all women immediately after vaginal birth. An unrepaired laceration can lead to haemorrhage, anaemia, abscess formation, wound breakdown, anal incontinence and rectovaginal fistula. These consequences of unrepaired or poorly repaired tears can affect patients not only physically but also psychologically.

## Examination And Classification Of Tears

### Examination And Classification Of Tears — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Explain to the woman what you are about to do and obtain her verbal

consent.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure good lighting.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is in a suitable position to enable visualization

of the entire genital tract.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Visually assess the extent of perineal trauma to include the structures

involved, the apex of the injury and any bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use instruments or gauze pads to assist with visualization to ensure

that you see the apex of any tear or laceration.

Note: A woman can have significant vaginal tears without experiencing tears of the perineum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the tear is long and deep through the perineum, inspect it to be

sure that there is no third or fourth degree tear:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a gloved finger in the anus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Gently lift the finger and identify the sphincter, or lack of it.

### Examination And Classification Of Tears — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Feel for the tone or tightness of the sphincter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Feel the surface of the rectum and look carefully for protrusion of

the finger into the vagina, which shows a tear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Change gloves after the rectal exam, carefully wash and dry

hands, and put on a new pair of sterile gloves to repair any identified tears.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman is bleeding and there is suspicion of a cervical tear,

ask an assistant to press down firmly on the woman’s uterus. This will move the cervix lower in the vagina so that you can examine it more easily.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-98; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the type of trauma: There are four degrees of tears that can

occur during a vaginal birth:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; First degree tears (Fig. P-45) involve the vaginal mucosa and

connective tissue.

### Examination And Classification Of Tears — phần 3

FIGURE P-45. First degree tear (involves fourchette only; vaginal and perineal skin torn, muscles intact)

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of vaginal and perineal tears; P-99

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Second degree tears (Fig. P-46) involve the vaginal mucosa,

connective tissue and underlying muscles.

FIGURE P-46. Second degree tear (involves fourchette and superficial perineal muscles; vaginal tear often extends up both sides)

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-100; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Third degree tears (Fig. P-47) involve complete transection of

the anal sphincter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: FIGURE P-47.; Third degree tear (involves fourchette

and superficial perineal muscles and anal sphincter)

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fourth degree tears involve the rectal mucosa.

### Examination And Classification Of Tears — phần 4

Note: It is important that absorbable sutures be used for closure. Polyglycolic sutures are preferred over chromic catgut for their tensile strength, non- allergenic properties and lower probability of infectious complications. Chromic catgut is an acceptable alternative, but it is not ideal.

## Repair Of First And Second Degree Tears

### Repair Of First And Second Degree Tears — phần 1

Most first degree tears close spontaneously without sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of vaginal and perineal tears; P-101

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. Make sure there are no

known allergies to lidocaine or related drugs. Use local infiltration with lidocaine solution (page C-57). If necessary, use a pudendal block (page P-3).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant to check the uterus at 15-minute intervals and ensure

that it is contracted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure good lighting.
