- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is in a suitable position to enable visualization

of the entire genital tract.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Expose the tear (Fig. P-48).

FIGURE P-48. Exposing a perineal tear

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the area around the tear (page C-35).

### Repair Of First And Second Degree Tears — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Note: If more than 40 mL of lidocaine solution will be needed for

the repair, add adrenaline to the solution (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infiltrate (Fig. P-49, page P-102) beneath the vaginal mucosa, beneath

the skin of the perineum and deeply into the perineal muscle using about 10 mL 0.5% lidocaine solution (page C-57).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-102; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

FIGURE P-49. Technique of infiltration of local anaesthetic before repair of a tear

Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

### Repair Of First And Second Degree Tears — phần 3

pinch the area with forceps. If the woman feels the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the vaginal mucosa using a continuous 2-0 suture (Fig. P-50,

page P-103):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Start the repair about 1 cm above the apex (top) of the vaginal

tear. Continue the suture to the level of the vaginal opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; At the opening of the vagina, bring together the cut edges of the

vaginal opening.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Bring the needle under the vaginal opening and out through the

perineal tear and tie.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of vaginal and perineal tears; P-103

FIGURE P-50. Repairing the vaginal mucosa

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the perineal muscles using interrupted 2-0 suture (Fig. P-51). If

the tear is deep, place a second layer of the same stitch to close the space.

FIGURE P-51. Repairing the perineal muscles

### Repair Of First And Second Degree Tears — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the perineal skin using interrupted (or subcuticular) 2-0 sutures,

starting at the vaginal opening (Fig. P-52, page P-104).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the tear was deep, perform a rectal examination. Make sure no

stitches are in the rectum; if there are stitches in the rectum, undo the sutures and resuture the tear, taking care to avoid stitches in the rectum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-104; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

FIGURE P-52. Repairing the skin

It is not necessary to provide prophylactic antibiotics for cases of first and second degree tears.

## Tears

### Tears — phần 1

Note: A woman may suffer loss of control over bowel movements and gas if a torn anal sphincter is not repaired correctly. If a tear in the rectum is not repaired, the woman can suffer from infection and rectovaginal fistula (passage of stool through the vagina). Repair the tear in the operating room:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics before beginning repair

(page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 500 mg by mouth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Provide emotional support and encouragement. Use a pudendal block

(page P-3), ketamine (page P-13) or spinal anaesthesia (page P-11). Rarely, if all edges of the tear can be seen, the repair can be done using local infiltration with lidocaine (Fig. P-49, page P-102) and morphine and diazepam IV slowly (do not mix in the same syringe). Make sure there are no known allergies to lidocaine or related drugs.

### Tears — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant to check the uterus at 15-minute intervals and ensure

that it is contracted.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure good lighting.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of vaginal and perineal tears; P-105

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ensure that the woman is in a suitable position to enable visualization

of the entire genital tract.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the tear and remove any faecal material, if

present (page C-35).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infiltrate beneath the vaginal mucosa, beneath the skin of the perineum

### Tears — phần 3

and deeply into the perineal muscle, using about 10 mL 0.5% lidocaine solution (Fig. P-49, page P-102). Note: Aspirate (pull back on the plunger) to be sure that no vessel has been penetrated. If blood is returned in the syringe with aspiration, remove the needle. Recheck the position carefully and try again. Never inject if blood is aspirated. The woman can suffer convulsions and death if IV injection of lidocaine occurs.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the conclusion of the set of injections, wait two minutes and then

pinch the area with forceps. If the woman feels the pinch, wait two more minutes and then retest.

Anaesthetize early to provide sufficient time for effect.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the rectum using interrupted 3-0 or 4-0 sutures 0.5 cm apart to

bring together the mucosa (Fig. P-53, page P-106). Remember: Place the suture through the muscularis (not all the way through the mucosa):

### Tears — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Cover the muscularis layer by bringing together the fascial layer

with interrupted sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Apply antiseptic solution to the area frequently.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-106; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

FIGURE P-53. Closing the muscle wall of the rectum

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the sphincter is torn:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Grasp each end of the sphincter with an Allis clamp (the sphincter

retracts when torn). The fascial sheath around the sphincter is strong and will not tear when pulled with the clamp (Fig. P-54).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repair the sphincter with two or three interrupted stitches of 2-0

suture.

FIGURE P-54. Suturing the anal sphincter

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply antiseptic solution to the area again.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of vaginal and perineal tears; P-107

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine the anus with a gloved finger to ensure the correct repair of

### Tears — phần 5

the rectum and sphincter. If there are stitches in the rectum, undo the sutures and resuture the tear, taking care to avoid stitches in the rectum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Remove gloves after the rectal exam, carefully wash and dry hands,

and put on a new pair of sterile gloves to continue the repair.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the vaginal mucosa, perineal muscles and skin (page P-103,

P-104).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman to clean the genital area, including the suture line,

with clean water twice daily and always after defecation.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Advise the woman on danger signs and when and where to seek care if

they occur.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Follow up closely for signs of wound infection (e.g. marked

inflammation, excessive swelling, pus).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Avoid giving enemas or rectal examinations for two weeks.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give stool softener by mouth for one week, if possible. Avoid bulk

laxatives as these can cause wound dehiscence.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; No dietary restrictions are needed.

## Management Of Neglected Cases

### Management Of Neglected Cases — phần 1

A perineal tear can become contaminated with faecal material. If closure is delayed more than 12 hours, infection is likely. Delayed primary closure is indicated in such cases.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For first and second degree tears, have the woman return in six days.

If there are no signs of infection, proceed with delayed primary closure.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; For third and fourth degree tears:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the rectal mucosa with some supporting tissue and

approximate the fascia of the anal sphincter with two or three sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the muscle and vaginal mucosa and the perineal skin

six days later.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-108; Repair of vaginal and perineal tears

## Complications

### Complications — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If a haematoma is observed, open and drain it. If there are no signs

of infection and the bleeding has stopped, the wound can be reclosed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection, open and drain the wound. Remove

infected sutures and debride the wound:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is mild, antibiotics are not required.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is severe but does not involve deep tissues, give

a combination of antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 500 mg by mouth every six hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the infection is deep, involves muscles and is causing

necrosis (necrotizing fasciitis), give a combination of antibiotics until the necrotic tissue has been removed and the woman is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; ampicillin 2 g IV every six hours;

### Complications — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: –; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

Note: Necrotizing fasciitis requires wide surgical debridement. Perform delayed primary closure in two to four weeks (depending on resolution of the infection).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Faecal incontinence may result from complete sphincter transection.

Many women are able to maintain control of defaecation by using other perineal muscles. When incontinence persists, reconstructive surgery must be performed three months or more after childbirth.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Rectovaginal fistula requires reconstructive surgery by a skilled

provider trained in the repair technique. Refer the woman for evaluation and follow-up.

## Correcting Uterine Inversion P-109

### Correcting Uterine Inversion P-109 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and start an IV infusion

(page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give morphine and diazepam IV slowly (do not mix in the same

syringe). If necessary, use general anaesthesia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Thoroughly cleanse the inverted uterus using antiseptic solution.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply compression to the inverted uterus with a moist, warm sterile

towel until ready for the procedure.

## Manual Correction

### Manual Correction — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Wearing sterile gloves, grasp the inverted uterus and push it through

the cervix in the direction of the umbilicus to its normal anatomic position, using the other hand to support the uterus (Fig. P-55). If the placenta is still attached, manually remove the placenta after correction.

It is important that the part of the uterus that came out last (the

## part closest to the cervix) goes in first.

### part closest to the cervix) goes in first. — phần 1

FIGURE P-55. Manual replacement of the inverted uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If correction is not achieved, proceed to hydrostatic correction (page

P-110).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-110; Uterine inversion

## Hydrostatic Correction

### Hydrostatic Correction — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the woman in deep Trendelenburg position (lower her head

about 0.5 metres below the level of the perineum).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Prepare a high-level disinfected or sterile douche system with a large

nozzle, long tubing (2 metres) and a warm water reservoir (3–5 L). Note: This can also be done using warmed normal saline and an ordinary IV administration set.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the posterior fornix. This is easily done in partial inversion

when the inverted uterus is still in the vagina. In other cases, the posterior fornix is recognized by where the rugose vagina becomes the smooth vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the nozzle of the douche in the posterior fornix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; At the same time, with the other hand hold the labia sealed over the

nozzle and use the forearm to support the nozzle.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ask an assistant to start the douche with full pressure (raise the water

### Hydrostatic Correction — phần 2

reservoir to at least 2 metres). Water will distend the posterior fornix of the vagina gradually so that it stretches. This causes the circumference of the orifice to increase, relieves cervical constriction and results in correction of the inversion.

## Manual Correction Under General Anaesthesia

### Manual Correction Under General Anaesthesia — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If hydrostatic correction is not successful, try manual repositioning

under general anaesthesia using halothane. Halothane is recommended because it relaxes the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the inverted uterus and push it through the cervix in the

direction of the umbilicus to its normal anatomic position, using the other hand to support the uterus (Fig. P-55, page P-109). If the placenta is still attached, manually remove the placenta after correction.

## Combined Abdominal-Vaginal Correction

### Combined Abdominal-Vaginal Correction — phần 1

Abdominal-vaginal correction under general anaesthesia may be required if the above measures fail.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review operative care principles (page C-65).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Uterine inversion; P-111

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic

hair, through the skin and to the level of the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up

and down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal

wall muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to make an opening in the peritoneum near

the umbilicus. Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

### Combined Abdominal-Vaginal Correction — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone and place

self-retaining abdominal retractors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dilate the constricting cervical ring digitally.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a tenaculum through the cervical ring and grasp the inverted

fundus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply gentle continuous traction to the fundus while an assistant

attempts manual correction vaginally.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If traction fails:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Incise the constricting cervical ring vertically and posteriorly

(where the incision is least likely to injure the bladder or uterine vessels).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat digital dilatation, tenaculum and traction steps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the constriction ring.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If correction is successful, close the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make sure there is no bleeding. Use a sponge to remove any clots

inside the abdomen.

### Combined Abdominal-Vaginal Correction — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture. Note: There is no need to close the bladder peritoneum or the abdominal peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with

gauze and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-112; Uterine inversion

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical

mattress sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Once the inversion is corrected, infuse oxytocin 20 units in 500 mL IV

fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s lactate) at 10 drops per minute:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If haemorrhage is suspected, increase the infusion rate to

60 drops per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the uterus does not contract after oxytocin, give ergometrine

0.2 mg or prostaglandins (Table S-11, page S-32).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics after correcting the

inverted uterus (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If combined abdominal-vaginal correction was used, see

postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or the woman currently has fever, give

a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

## Repair Of Ruptured Uterus P-113

### Repair Of Ruptured Uterus P-113 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65), and start an IV infusion (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-50):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic

hair, through the skin and to the level of the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up

and down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal

wall muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers to make an opening in the peritoneum near the

### Repair Of Ruptured Uterus P-113 — phần 2

umbilicus. Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down in order to see the entire uterus. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Examine the abdomen and the uterus for the site of rupture and

remove clots.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone and place

self-retaining abdominal retractors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Deliver the baby and placenta.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Infuse oxytocin 20 units in 1 L IV fluids (normal saline or Ringer’s

lactate) at 60 drops per minute until the uterus contracts, and then reduce to 20 drops per minute.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lift the uterus out of the pelvis in order to note the extent of the injury.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Examine both the front and the back of the uterus.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Hold the bleeding edges of the uterus with Green Armytage forceps

(or ring forceps).

### Repair Of Ruptured Uterus P-113 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Separate the bladder from the lower uterine segment by sharp or blunt

dissection. If the bladder is scarred to the uterus, use fine scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-114; Repair of ruptured uterus

## Rupture Through Cervix And Vagina

### Rupture Through Cervix And Vagina — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the uterus is torn through the cervix and vagina, mobilize the

bladder at least 2 cm below the tear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If possible, place a suture 2 cm above the lower end of the cervical

tear and keep traction on the suture to bring the lower end of the tear into view as the repair continues.

## Rupture Laterally Through Uterine Artery

### Rupture Laterally Through Uterine Artery — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the rupture extends laterally to damage one or both uterine

arteries, ligate the injured artery.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the arteries and ureter prior to ligating the uterine vessels

(Fig. P-56, page P-118).

## Rupture With Broad Ligament Haematoma

### Rupture With Broad Ligament Haematoma — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the rupture has created a broad ligament haematoma

(Fig. S-2, page S-25), clamp, cut and tie off the round ligament.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the anterior leaf of the broad ligament.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Drain off the haematoma manually, if necessary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Inspect the area carefully for injury to the uterine artery or its

branches. Ligate any bleeding vessels.

## Repairing The Uterine Tear

### Repairing The Uterine Tear — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the tear with a continuous locking stitch of 0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture. If bleeding is not controlled or if the rupture is through a previous classical or vertical incision, place a second layer of suture. Ensure that the ureter is identified and exposed to avoid including it in a stitch.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the rupture is too extensive for repair, proceed with hysterectomy

(page P-121).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Control bleeding by clamping with long artery forceps and ligating. If

the bleeding points are deep, use figure-of-eight sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Repair of ruptured uterus; P-115

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the woman has requested tubal ligation, perform the procedure at

this time (page P-63).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place an abdominal drain (page C-70).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that there is no bleeding. Remove clots using a sponge.

### Repairing The Uterine Tear — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In all cases, check for injury to the bladder. If a bladder injury is

identified, repair the injury (see below).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture. Note: There is no need to close the bladder peritoneum or the abdominal peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with

gauze and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical

mattress sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

## Repair Of Bladder Injury

### Repair Of Bladder Injury — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify the extent of the injury by grasping each edge of the tear with

a clamp and gently stretching. Determine if the injury is close to the bladder trigone (ureters and urethra).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Dissect the bladder off the lower uterine segment with fine scissors or

with a sponge on a clamp.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Free a 2 cm circle of bladder tissue around the tear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Repair the tear in two layers with continuous 3-0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Suture the bladder mucosa (thin inner layer) and bladder muscle

(outer layer).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Invert (fold) the outer layer over the first layer of suture and place

another layer of suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that sutures do not enter the trigone area.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-116; Repair of ruptured uterus

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Test the repair for leaks:

### Repair Of Bladder Injury — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Fill the bladder with sterile saline or water through a transurethral

catheter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If leaks are present, remove the suture, repair and test again.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If it is not certain that the repair is well away from the ureters and

urethra, complete the repair and refer the woman to a higher-level facility for an intravenous pyelogram.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Keep the bladder catheter in place for at least seven days and until

urine is clear. Continue IV fluids to ensure flushing of the bladder, and encourage the woman to drink fluids.

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or the woman currently has a fever,

give a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of infection, remove the abdominal drain after

48 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible (page S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If tubal ligation was not performed, offer family planning (Table

S-6, page S-15). If the woman wishes to have more children, advise her to have elective caesarean births with future pregnancies.

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

Because there is an increased risk of rupture with subsequent pregnancies, the option of permanent contraception needs to be discussed with the woman after the emergency is over. Permanent contraception should not be performed without informed consent from the woman.

## Ligation

### Ligation — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65), and start an IV infusion (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic

hair, through the skin and to the level of the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up

and down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal

wall muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers to make an opening in the peritoneum near the

### Ligation — phần 2

umbilicus. Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down in order to see the entire uterus. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone and place

self-retaining abdominal retractors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Pull on the uterus to expose the lower part of the broad ligament.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Feel for pulsations of the uterine artery near the junction of the uterus

and cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Using 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture on a large needle, pass

the needle around the artery and through 2–3 cm of myometrium (uterine muscle) at the level where a transverse lower uterine segment incision would be made. Tie the suture securely.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place the sutures as close to the uterus as possible, as the ureter is

generally only 1 cm lateral to the uterine artery.

### Ligation — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat on the other side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the artery has been torn, clamp and tie the bleeding ends.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-118; Uterine and utero-ovarian artery ligation

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ligate the utero-ovarian artery just below the point where the

ovarian suspensory ligament joins the uterus (Fig. P-56).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Repeat on the other side.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Observe for continued bleeding or formation of haematoma.

FIGURE P-56. Sites for ligating uterine and utero-ovarian arteries

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that there is no bleeding. Remove clots using a sponge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Examine carefully for injuries to the bladder and repair any found

(page P-115).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture. Note: There is no need to close the bladder peritoneum or the abdominal peritoneum.

### Ligation — phần 4

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with

gauze and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical

mattress sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Uterine and utero-ovarian artery ligation; P-119

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative (page C-71) and general care principles (page

C-25).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor urine output. If there is blood in the urine or the woman has

loin pain (pain in the sides between the lower ribs and pelvis, and/or in the lower part of the back), refer the woman to a tertiary care centre, if possible, for treatment of an obstructed ureter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or the woman currently has fever, give

a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of infection, remove the abdominal drain after 48

hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible (page S-14).

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-120; Uterine and utero-ovarian artery ligation

## Postpartum Hysterectomy P-121

### Postpartum Hysterectomy P-121 — phần 1

Postpartum hysterectomy can be subtotal (supracervical) unless the cervix and lower uterine segment are involved. Total hysterectomy may be necessary in the case of a tear of the lower segment that extends into the cervix or bleeding after placenta praevia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65), and start an IV infusion (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there is uncontrollable haemorrhage following vaginal birth,

keep in mind that speed is essential. To open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic

hair, through the skin and to the level of the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

### Postpartum Hysterectomy P-121 — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up

and down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal

wall muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers to make an opening in the peritoneum near the

umbilicus. Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down in order to see the entire uterus. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone and place

self-retaining abdominal retractors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If the birth was by caesarean, clamp the sites of bleeding along the

uterine incision:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In case of massive bleeding, have an assistant press fingers over

the aorta in the lower abdomen. This will reduce intraperitoneal bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Extend the skin incision, if needed.

### Postpartum Hysterectomy P-121 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-122; Postpartum hysterectomy

## Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy

### Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Lift the uterus out of the abdomen and gently pull to maintain traction.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Doubly clamp the round ligaments and cut with scissors

(Fig. P-57). Clamp and cut the pedicles, but ligate after the uterine arteries are secured to save time.

FIGURE P-57. Dividing the round ligaments

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; From the edge of the cut round ligament, open the anterior leaf of the

broad ligament. Incise to:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the point where the bladder peritoneum is reflected onto the lower

uterine surface in the midline; or

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; the point where the peritoneal incision was made for the current or

a previous caesarean.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use two fingers to push the posterior leaf of the broad ligament forward,

### Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy — phần 2

just under the fallopian tube and ovary, near the uterine edge. Make a hole the size of a finger in the broad ligament, using scissors. Doubly clamp and cut the tube, the ovarian ligament and the broad ligament through the hole in the broad ligament (Fig. P-58, page P-123).

The ureters are close to the uterine vessels. The ureter must be identified and exposed to avoid injuring it during surgery or including it in a stitch.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Postpartum hysterectomy; P-123

FIGURE P-58. Dividing the tube and ovarian ligaments

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Divide the posterior leaf of the broad ligament downwards towards the

uterosacral ligaments, using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Grasp the edge of the bladder flap with forceps or a small clamp.

Using fingers or scissors, dissect the bladder downwards off of the lower uterine segment. Direct the pressure downwards but inwards towards the cervix and the lower uterine segment.

### Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Reposition the bladder retractor and retract the bladder inferiorly.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Locate the uterine artery and vein on each side of the uterus. Feel for

the junction of the uterus and cervix.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Doubly clamp across the uterine vessels at a 90 degree angle on each

side of the cervix. Cut and doubly ligate with 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture (Fig. P-59, page P-124).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-124; Postpartum hysterectomy

FIGURE P-59. Dividing the uterine vessels

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Observe carefully for any further bleeding. If the uterine arteries are

ligated correctly, bleeding should stop and the uterus should look pale.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Return to the clamped pedicles of the round ligaments and

tubo-ovarian ligaments and ligate them with 0 chromic catgut (or polyglycolic) suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Amputate the uterus above the level where the uterine arteries are

### Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy — phần 4

ligated, using scissors (Fig. P-60, page P-125).

- Dòng dữ liệu: Postpartum hysterectomy; P-125

FIGURE P-60. Line of amputation

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the cervical stump with interrupted 2-0 or 3-0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) sutures.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Carefully inspect the cervical stump, leaves of the broad ligament and

other pelvic floor structures for any bleeding.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If slight bleeding persists or a clotting disorder is suspected, place

a drain through the abdominal wall (page C-70). Do not place a drain through the cervical stump, as this can cause postoperative infection.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that there is no bleeding. Remove clots using a sponge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; In all cases, check for injury to the bladder. If a bladder injury is

identified, repair the injury (page P-115).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

### Subtotal (Supracervical) Hysterectomy — phần 5

polyglycolic) suture. Note: There is no need to close the bladder peritoneum or the abdominal peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with

gauze and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical

mattress sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-126; Postpartum hysterectomy

## Total Hysterectomy

### Total Hysterectomy — phần 1

The following additional steps are required for total hysterectomy:

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Push the bladder down to free the top 2 cm of the vagina.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the posterior leaf of the broad ligament.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clamp, ligate and cut the uterosacral ligaments.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clamp, ligate and cut the cardinal ligaments, which contain the

descending branches of the uterine vessels. This is the critical step in the operation:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Grasp the ligament vertically with a large-toothed clamp

(e.g. Kocher). Place the clamp 5 mm lateral to the cervix and cut the ligament close to the cervix, leaving a stump medial to the clamp for safety.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If the cervix is long, repeat the above step two or three times as

needed.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; The upper 2 cm of the vagina should now be free of attachments.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Clamp and transect the vagina as near to the cervix as possible,

### Total Hysterectomy — phần 2

clamping bleeding points as they appear.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place haemostatic angle sutures, to include round, cardinal and

uterosacral ligaments.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place continuous sutures on the vaginal cuff to stop haemorrhage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen (as above) after placing a drain in the

extraperitoneal space near the stump of the cervix (page C-70).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Monitor urine output. If there is blood in the urine or the woman has

loin pain (pain in the sides between the lower ribs and pelvis and/or in the lower part of the back), refer the woman to a tertiary care centre, if possible, for treatment of an obstructed ureter.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or the woman currently has fever, give

a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: Postpartum hysterectomy; P-127

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are no signs of infection, remove the abdominal drain after 48

hours.

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 2

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible (page S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-128; Postpartum hysterectomy

## Salpingectomy For Ectopic Pregnancy P-129

### Salpingectomy For Ectopic Pregnancy P-129 — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review general care principles (page C-25) and operative care

principles (page C-65), and start an IV infusion (page C-34).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give a single dose of prophylactic antibiotics (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; ampicillin 2 g IV;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; OR cefazolin 1 g IV.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a midline vertical incision below the umbilicus to the pubic

hair, through the skin and to the level of the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Make a 2–3 cm vertical incision in the fascia.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Hold the fascial edge with forceps and lengthen the incision up

and down using scissors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers or scissors to separate the rectus muscles (abdominal

wall muscles).

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Use fingers to make an opening in the peritoneum near the

### Salpingectomy For Ectopic Pregnancy P-129 — phần 2

umbilicus. Use scissors to lengthen the incision up and down in order to see the entire uterus. Carefully, to prevent bladder injury, use scissors to separate layers and open the lower part of the peritoneum.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Place a bladder retractor over the pubic bone and place

self-retaining abdominal retractors.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Identify and bring to view the fallopian tube with the ectopic gestation

and its ovary.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply traction forceps (e.g. Babcock) to increase exposure and clamp

the mesosalpinx to stop haemorrhage.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Aspirate blood from the lower abdomen and remove blood clots.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply gauze moistened with warm saline to pack off the bowel and

omentum from the operative field.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Divide the mesosalpinx using a series of clamps (Fig. P-61 A and B,

page P-130). Apply each clamp close to the tube to preserve ovarian vasculature.

### Salpingectomy For Ectopic Pregnancy P-129 — phần 3

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Transfix and tie the divided mesosalpinx with 2-0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture before releasing the clamps.

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-130; Salpingectomy for ectopic pregnancy

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Place a proximal suture around the tube at its isthmic end and excise

the tube (Fig. P-61 C).

FIGURE P-61. Clamping, dividing and cutting the mesosalpinx

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen:

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Ensure that there is no bleeding. Remove clots using a sponge.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; Close the fascia with continuous 0 chromic catgut (or

polyglycolic) suture.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are signs of infection, pack the subcutaneous tissue with

gauze and place loose 0 catgut (or polyglycolic) sutures. Close the skin with a delayed closure after the infection has cleared.

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; If there are no signs of infection, close the skin with vertical

### Salpingectomy For Ectopic Pregnancy P-129 — phần 4

mattress sutures of 3-0 nylon (or silk) and apply a sterile dressing.

- Dòng dữ liệu: Salpingectomy for ectopic pregnancy; P-131

## Salpingostomy

### Salpingostomy — phần 1

Rarely, when there is little damage to the tube, the gestational sac can be removed and the tube conserved. This should be done only in cases where the conservation of fertility is very important to the woman, because she is at risk for another ectopic pregnancy.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Open the abdomen and expose the appropriate ovary and fallopian

tube (page P-129).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Apply traction forceps (e.g. Babcock) on either side of the unruptured

tubal pregnancy and lift to view.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use a scalpel to make a linear incision through the serosa on the side

opposite the mesentery and along the axis of the tube, but do not cut the gestational sac.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Use the scalpel handle to slide the gestational sac out of the tube.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Ligate bleeding points.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Return the ovary and fallopian tube to the pelvic cavity.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Close the abdomen (page P-130).

## Post-Procedure Care

### Post-Procedure Care — phần 1

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Review postoperative care principles (page C-71).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If there are signs of infection or the woman currently has a fever,

give a combination of antibiotics until she is fever-free for 48 hours (page C-49):

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; clindamycin phosphate 600 mg IV every eight hours;

- Dòng dữ liệu: -; PLUS gentamicin 5 mg/kg body weight IV every 24 hours.

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Give appropriate analgesic drugs (page C-64).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; Offer other health services, if possible (page S-14).

- Dòng dữ liệu: •; If salpingostomy was performed, advise the woman of the risk for

another ectopic pregnancy and offer family planning (Table S-6, page S-15).

- Dòng dữ liệu: P-132; Salpingectomy for ectopic pregnancy

Integrated Management Of Pregnancy And Childbirth

The Integrated Management of Pregnancy and Childbirth (IMPAC) is a strategy to improve access to and quality of maternal and neonatal health care. It comprises:

evidence-based norms and standards of care;

managerial tools to strengthen the capacity of the health care system;

### S-29, S-43, S-105, S-152, S-156, — phần 33

a process to foster involvement of individuals, families and communities;

and

an implementation strategy.

IMPAC is designed to be adapted to local situations and to be implemented in countries by the government, in collaboration with UN and bilateral agencies, non-governmental organizations and other partners at the national level.

For more information, please contact : Department of Maternal, Newborn, Child and adolescent health. Avenue Appia 20, CH-1211 Geneva 27, Switzerland. Tel: +41 22 791 4447 / 3346 Fax: +41 22 791 5853 E-mail: mncah@who.int
